
async function openBankingPayBatchChildModal(batchId, seed = {}) {
  const enc = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function')
    ? escapeHtml
    : (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
        '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;'
      }[c]));

  const deep = (o) => {
    try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o || null)); } catch { return o; }
  };

  const normalizeChildTabKey = (value) => {
    const raw = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    if (!raw) return '';
    const key = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_');
    if (key === 'overview') return 'overview';
    if (key === 'current_payment_status' || key === 'currentpaymentstatus' || key === 'payment_issues' || key === 'payment_issue' || key === 'payment_status' || key === 'paymentstatus' || key === 'issues') return 'payment_issues';
    if (key === 'paye_worksheet' || key === 'paye' || key === 'worksheet' || key === 'paye_worksheets') return 'paye_worksheet';
    if (key === 'remittance' || key === 'remittances' || key === 'communications') return 'remittance';
    return '';
  };

  const batchInputObj = (batchId && typeof batchId === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchId)) ? batchId : null;
  const seedObj = (() => {
    const out = {};
    try {
      if (batchInputObj) Object.assign(out, batchInputObj);
      if (seed && typeof seed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(seed)) Object.assign(out, seed);
    } catch {}
    return out;
  })();
  const rawBatchId = batchInputObj
    ? (
        batchInputObj.batchId ||
        batchInputObj.batch_id ||
        batchInputObj.payBatchId ||
        batchInputObj.pay_batch_id ||
        batchInputObj.id ||
        ''
      )
    : batchId;
  const requestedActiveTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(
    seedObj.activeTabKey ||
    seedObj.activeTab ||
    seedObj.tabKey ||
    seedObj.tab ||
    ''
  );
  const hasRequestedActiveTabKey = !!requestedActiveTabKey;

  const mc = (window.modalCtx && typeof window.modalCtx === 'object') ? window.modalCtx : null;
  if (!mc || String(mc.entity || '') !== 'banking') {
    try { if (typeof window.__toast === 'function') window.__toast('Banking modal is not active.'); } catch {}
    return;
  }

  mc.banking = (mc.banking && typeof mc.banking === 'object') ? mc.banking : {};
  mc.banking.pay = (mc.banking.pay && typeof mc.banking.pay === 'object') ? mc.banking.pay : {};

  const st = mc.banking;
  const pay = st.pay;

  const id = String(rawBatchId || '').trim();
  if (!id) {
    try { if (typeof window.__toast === 'function') window.__toast('pay_batch_id is required'); } catch {}
    return;
  }

  const KIND = 'banking-pay-batch-child';
  const MODAL_CLASS = 'banking-pay-batch-child-modal';

  const getTopModalFrameSafe = () => {
    try { return (typeof window.__getModalFrame === 'function') ? window.__getModalFrame() : null; } catch { return null; }
  };

  const getFrameOpenTokenSafe = (frame) => String(
    frame?.childOpenToken ||
    frame?._childOpenToken ||
    frame?.openToken ||
    frame?._openToken ||
    frame?.options?.childOpenToken ||
    frame?.options?._childOpenToken ||
    frame?.opts?.childOpenToken ||
    frame?.opts?._childOpenToken ||
    ''
  ).trim();

  const getFrameBatchIdSafe = (frame) => String(
    frame?.payBatchId ||
    frame?.pay_batch_id ||
    frame?.batchId ||
    frame?.batch_id ||
    frame?.options?.payBatchId ||
    frame?.options?.pay_batch_id ||
    frame?.options?.batchId ||
    frame?.options?.batch_id ||
    frame?.opts?.payBatchId ||
    frame?.opts?.pay_batch_id ||
    frame?.opts?.batchId ||
    frame?.opts?.batch_id ||
    ''
  ).trim();

  const existingChild = (pay.child && typeof pay.child === 'object') ? pay.child : null;
  const existingChildBatchId = String(existingChild?.batchId || existingChild?.payBatchId || existingChild?.pay_batch_id || '').trim();
  const topFrameForDuplicateGuard = getTopModalFrameSafe();
  const topFrameKindForDuplicateGuard = String(topFrameForDuplicateGuard?.kind || '').trim();
  const topFrameTokenForDuplicateGuard = getFrameOpenTokenSafe(topFrameForDuplicateGuard);
  const topFrameBatchIdForDuplicateGuard = getFrameBatchIdSafe(topFrameForDuplicateGuard);
  const duplicateGuardMatchesCurrentChild = !!(
    existingChild &&
    existingChildBatchId === id &&
    topFrameKindForDuplicateGuard === KIND &&
    (
      !topFrameTokenForDuplicateGuard ||
      !String(existingChild.openToken || '').trim() ||
      topFrameTokenForDuplicateGuard === String(existingChild.openToken || '').trim()
    ) &&
    (
      !topFrameBatchIdForDuplicateGuard ||
      topFrameBatchIdForDuplicateGuard === id
    )
  );

  if (duplicateGuardMatchesCurrentChild) {
    try {
      existingChild.ui = (existingChild.ui && typeof existingChild.ui === 'object') ? existingChild.ui : {};
      if (hasRequestedActiveTabKey) {
        existingChild.ui.activeTabKey = requestedActiveTabKey;
        existingChild.ui.requestedActiveTabKey = requestedActiveTabKey;
        existingChild.ui.hasRequestedActiveTabKey = true;
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      if (typeof topFrameForDuplicateGuard.setTab === 'function') {
        const tabKey = String(existingChild.ui?.activeTabKey || requestedActiveTabKey || topFrameForDuplicateGuard.currentTabKey || 'overview').trim() || 'overview';
        await topFrameForDuplicateGuard.setTab(tabKey);
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      const modal = document.getElementById('modal');
      if (modal && modal.classList) modal.classList.add(MODAL_CLASS);
    } catch {}

    try {
      if (typeof existingChild.__loadBatch === 'function') {
        await existingChild.__loadBatch({ forcePoll: false, silent: true, duplicateOpenRefresh: true });
      } else if (typeof existingChild.__rerenderChild === 'function') {
        await existingChild.__rerenderChild();
      }
    } catch {
      try { if (typeof window.__toast === 'function') window.__toast('Unable to refresh this payment batch. Please refresh Banking and try again.'); } catch {}
    }

    return;
  }

  const stopExistingChildLiveWatchBeforeReplace = () => {
    try {
      const prev = (existingChild && typeof existingChild === 'object' && !Array.isArray(existingChild)) ? existingChild : null;
      if (!prev) return;

      const previousBatchId = String(
        prev.batchId ||
        prev.payBatchId ||
        prev.pay_batch_id ||
        ''
      ).trim();

      if (previousBatchId && typeof stopBankingPayBatchLiveWatch === 'function') {
        try { stopBankingPayBatchLiveWatch(previousBatchId); } catch {}
      }

      try {
        const handle = prev.liveWatch && typeof prev.liveWatch === 'object' ? prev.liveWatch.handle : null;
        if (handle && typeof handle.abort === 'function') handle.abort();
        if (handle && typeof handle.stop === 'function') handle.stop();
        if (handle && typeof handle.cancel === 'function') handle.cancel();
      } catch {}

      try {
        const ctrl = prev.__liveWatchAbortController || prev.__watchAbortController || prev.liveWatchAbortController;
        if (ctrl && typeof ctrl.abort === 'function') ctrl.abort();
      } catch {}

      try {
        if (prev.__liveWatchTimer) {
          clearTimeout(prev.__liveWatchTimer);
          clearInterval(prev.__liveWatchTimer);
          prev.__liveWatchTimer = null;
        }
      } catch {}

      try {
        if (prev.__watchTimer) {
          clearTimeout(prev.__watchTimer);
          clearInterval(prev.__watchTimer);
          prev.__watchTimer = null;
        }
      } catch {}

      try {
        if (prev.__autoPollTimer) {
          clearInterval(prev.__autoPollTimer);
          prev.__autoPollTimer = null;
        }
      } catch {}

      try {
        const timers = (prev.__firstPageTimers && typeof prev.__firstPageTimers === 'object' && !Array.isArray(prev.__firstPageTimers)) ? prev.__firstPageTimers : {};
        for (const handle of Object.values(timers)) {
          try { if (handle) clearTimeout(handle); } catch {}
        }
        prev.__firstPageTimers = {};
      } catch {}

      try {
        prev.__sectionLoadPromises = {};
      } catch {}

      try {
        prev.liveWatch = (prev.liveWatch && typeof prev.liveWatch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(prev.liveWatch)) ? prev.liveWatch : {};
        prev.liveWatch.running = false;
        prev.liveWatch.started = false;
      } catch {}
    } catch {}
  };

  stopExistingChildLiveWatchBeforeReplace();

  const rootId = `bankingPayBatchChild_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;

  const CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS = [
    'overview_items',
    'current_payment_status',
    'candidates',
    'items',
    'item_breakdowns',
    'transfers',
    'finance_case_groups',
    'remittances',
    'communications',
    'auth_history',
    'events'
  ];

  const normaliseChildSectionKey = (value) => {
    const key = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_');
    if (!key) return '';
    if (key === 'overview' || key === 'overview_item' || key === 'overview_items' || key === 'overviewitems') return 'overview_items';
    if (key === 'current_payment_status' || key === 'currentpaymentstatus' || key === 'payment_status' || key === 'paymentstatus') return 'current_payment_status';
    if (key === 'candidate') return 'candidates';
    if (key === 'item') return 'items';
    if (key === 'breakdowns' || key === 'item_breakdown') return 'item_breakdowns';
    if (key === 'transfer') return 'transfers';
    if (key === 'finance_cases' || key === 'finance_case_group') return 'finance_case_groups';
    if (key === 'remittance') return 'remittances';
    if (key === 'communication') return 'communications';
    if (key === 'auth' || key === 'history' || key === 'authorisation_history' || key === 'authorization_history') return 'auth_history';
    if (CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS.includes(key)) return key;
    return '';
  };

  const stableStringify = (value) => {
    const seen = new WeakSet();
    const normalise = (entry) => {
      if (entry === null || entry === undefined) return entry;
      if (typeof entry !== 'object') return entry;
      if (entry instanceof Date) return entry.toISOString();
      if (seen.has(entry)) return '[Circular]';
      seen.add(entry);
      if (Array.isArray(entry)) return entry.map((item) => normalise(item));
      const out = {};
      for (const key of Object.keys(entry).sort()) {
        const item = entry[key];
        if (typeof item === 'function' || item === undefined) continue;
        out[key] = normalise(item);
      }
      return out;
    };
    try { return JSON.stringify(normalise(value)); } catch { return String(value == null ? '' : value); }
  };

  const createChildSectionState = (section, existing = {}) => {
    const sectionKey = normaliseChildSectionKey(section);
    const prev = (existing && typeof existing === 'object' && !Array.isArray(existing)) ? existing : {};
    const rows = Array.isArray(prev.rows) ? prev.rows : (Array.isArray(prev.items) ? prev.items : []);
    const items = Array.isArray(prev.items) ? prev.items : rows;
    const knownTotalRaw = Number(prev.known_total_count ?? prev.knownTotalCount ?? prev.total_count ?? prev.totalCount);
    const explicitRetryRaw = Number(prev.explicit_retry_count ?? prev.explicitRetryCount);
    const loadGenerationRaw = Number(prev.load_generation ?? prev.loadGeneration);
    const errorValue = (() => {
      if (prev.error === null || prev.error === undefined) return '';
      if (typeof prev.error === 'string') return prev.error;
      if (prev.error && typeof prev.error === 'object') return String(prev.error.user_message || prev.error.message || prev.error.error || '');
      return String(prev.error || '');
    })();
    const lastErrorPayload = (prev.last_error_payload && typeof prev.last_error_payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(prev.last_error_payload))
      ? prev.last_error_payload
      : ((prev.lastErrorPayload && typeof prev.lastErrorPayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(prev.lastErrorPayload)) ? prev.lastErrorPayload : null);
    return {
      section: sectionKey,
      rows: rows.slice(),
      items: items.slice(),
      loading: prev.loading === true,
      loaded: prev.loaded === true,
      next_cursor: prev.next_cursor ?? prev.nextCursor ?? null,
      known_total_count: Number.isFinite(knownTotalRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(knownTotalRaw)) : null,
      error: String(errorValue || ''),
      source_purpose: String(prev.source_purpose || prev.sourcePurpose || '').trim(),
      sourcePurpose: String(prev.sourcePurpose || prev.source_purpose || '').trim(),
      current_tab: String(prev.current_tab || prev.currentTab || '').trim(),
      currentTab: String(prev.currentTab || prev.current_tab || '').trim(),
      loaded_at_utc: prev.loaded_at_utc || prev.loadedAtUtc || null,
      in_flight_key: String(prev.in_flight_key || prev.inFlightKey || ''),
      inFlightKey: String(prev.inFlightKey || prev.in_flight_key || ''),
      pending_request_key: String(prev.pending_request_key || prev.pendingRequestKey || ''),
      pendingRequestKey: String(prev.pendingRequestKey || prev.pending_request_key || ''),
      last_request_key: String(prev.last_request_key || prev.lastRequestKey || ''),
      lastRequestKey: String(prev.lastRequestKey || prev.last_request_key || ''),
      last_success_key: String(prev.last_success_key || prev.lastSuccessKey || ''),
      lastSuccessKey: String(prev.lastSuccessKey || prev.last_success_key || ''),
      failed_request_key: String(prev.failed_request_key || prev.failedRequestKey || ''),
      failedRequestKey: String(prev.failedRequestKey || prev.failed_request_key || ''),
      failed_at_utc: prev.failed_at_utc || prev.failedAtUtc || null,
      failedAtUtc: prev.failedAtUtc || prev.failed_at_utc || null,
      failed_cursor: prev.failed_cursor ?? prev.failedCursor ?? null,
      failedCursor: prev.failedCursor ?? prev.failed_cursor ?? null,
      last_error_payload: lastErrorPayload,
      lastErrorPayload: lastErrorPayload,
      explicit_retry_count: Number.isFinite(explicitRetryRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(explicitRetryRaw)) : 0,
      explicitRetryCount: Number.isFinite(explicitRetryRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(explicitRetryRaw)) : 0,
      load_generation: Number.isFinite(loadGenerationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(loadGenerationRaw)) : 0,
      loadGeneration: Number.isFinite(loadGenerationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(loadGenerationRaw)) : 0
    };
  };

  // Child state (stored on banking state so it survives tab switches)
  pay.child = {
    openToken: `pay-batch-child:${id}:${Date.now()}:${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,
    batchId: id,
    rootId,
    __modalShown: false,
    __loadSeq: 0,
    __sectionLoadSeq: 0,
    __sectionLoadPromises: {},
    __firstPageTimers: {},
    loading: false,
    error: '',
    data: null,
    executionRefreshRequired: false,
    execution_refresh_required: false,
    paymentExecutionRefreshRequired: false,
    payment_execution_refresh_required: false,
    activePaymentOperationId: '',
    active_payment_operation_id: '',
    activePaymentOperationType: '',
    active_payment_operation_type: '',
    activePaymentOperationStartedAt: null,
    active_payment_operation_started_at_utc: null,
    executionRefreshInFlight: false,
    execution_refresh_in_flight: false,
    executionRefreshLastReason: '',
    execution_refresh_last_reason: '',
    executionRefreshAppliedAtUtc: null,
    execution_refresh_applied_at_utc: null,
    available_sections: CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS.slice(),
    recommended_page_size: 100,
    sections: Object.fromEntries(CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS.map((sectionKey) => [sectionKey, createChildSectionState(sectionKey)])),
    sectionPages: Object.fromEntries(CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS.map((sectionKey) => [sectionKey, createChildSectionState(sectionKey)])),
    lazy_sections: Object.fromEntries(CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS.map((sectionKey) => [sectionKey, createChildSectionState(sectionKey)])),
    communications: {
      summary: null,
      result: null,
      mode: '',
      has_remittances: false,
      has_payout_notices: false,
      has_both: false
    },
    ui: {
      activeTabKey: requestedActiveTabKey || 'overview',
      requestedActiveTabKey: requestedActiveTabKey || '',
      hasRequestedActiveTabKey,
      expanded: {},
      expandAllDefault: false,
      last_poll_note: ''
    },
    csv: {
      generated_at_utc: null,
      confirmed_at_utc: null,
      payment_date_iso: null,
      bank_confirm_ref: ''
    },
    actionsBusy: {
      refreshing: false,
      polling: false,
      executing: false,
      exportingCsv: false,
      exportingDetailCsv: false,
      exportingDetailPdf: false,
      exportingWorksheet: false,
      printingWorksheet: false,
      retryingBlockedFunds: false,
      resolvingProviderSubmitReview: false
    },
    paye: {
      filterCandidateId: 'ALL',
      netDraft: {},
      importBusy: false,
      importError: '',
      importFriendlyError: null,
      saveBusy: false,
      saveError: '',
      saveFriendlyError: null
    }
  };
  const child = pay.child;

  const bankingPayExecutionRefreshDebugEnabled = () => {
    try {
      if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
      if (window.__CLOUDTMS_DEBUG_BANKING_PAY === true || window.__BANKING_PAY_DEBUG === true || window.__BANKING_PAY_BATCH_REFRESH_AFTER_EXEC_DEBUG === true) return true;
      const storage = window.localStorage;
      return !!(storage && (
        storage.getItem('cloudtms.debug.bankingPay') === 'true' ||
        storage.getItem('cloudtms.debug.bankingPayRefreshAfterExec') === 'true'
      ));
    } catch { return false; }
  };
  const logBankingPayExecutionRefresh = (event, payload = {}) => {
    if (!bankingPayExecutionRefreshDebugEnabled()) return;
    try {
      const safePayload = { ...(payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? payload : {}) };
      delete safePayload.auth;
      delete safePayload.authorization;
      delete safePayload.headers;
      delete safePayload.token;
      delete safePayload.password;
      console.info('[BP][BATCH-REFRESH-AFTER-EXEC]', { event, ...safePayload });
    } catch {}
  };

  const getChildSectionRequestPurpose = (sectionKey, tabKey) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    const activeTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey || child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
    if (key === 'overview_items') return 'overview';
    if (activeTabKey === 'payment_issues' || key === 'current_payment_status') return 'current_payment_status';
    if (activeTabKey === 'paye_worksheet') return 'paye_worksheet';
    if (activeTabKey === 'remittance' || key === 'remittances' || key === 'communications') return 'remittance';
    return 'detail';
  };

  const getChildSectionActionContext = (sectionKey, tabKey, requestPurpose) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    const activeTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey || child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
    if (activeTabKey === 'payment_issues' || key === 'current_payment_status') return 'current_payment_status';
    return String(requestPurpose || getChildSectionRequestPurpose(key, activeTabKey) || 'detail').trim() || 'detail';
  };

  const getChildSectionRequestLimit = (sectionKey) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (key === 'overview_items') return 100;
    const raw = Number(child.recommended_page_size || 100);
    return Number.isFinite(raw) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Math.trunc(raw) || 100)) : 100;
  };

  const buildChildSectionPageKey = ({
    sectionKey,
    cursor = null,
    limit = null,
    purpose = '',
    actionContext = '',
    activeTabKey = '',
    filters = null,
    sort = null,
    currentSectionJson = null
  } = {}) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    const tab = normalizeChildTabKey(activeTabKey || child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
    const cursorKey = cursor === null || cursor === undefined || String(cursor || '').trim() === '' ? 'FIRST' : stableStringify(cursor);
    const limitKey = Number.isFinite(Number(limit)) ? String(Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Math.trunc(Number(limit))))) : '';
    const purposeKey = String(purpose || getChildSectionRequestPurpose(key, tab) || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_') || 'detail';
    const actionKey = String(actionContext || getChildSectionActionContext(key, tab, purposeKey) || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_') || purposeKey;
    return [
      String(child.openToken || '').trim(),
      String(id || '').trim(),
      tab,
      key,
      purposeKey,
      actionKey,
      cursorKey,
      limitKey,
      stableStringify(filters || null),
      stableStringify(sort || null),
      stableStringify(currentSectionJson || null)
    ].join('|');
  };

  const isChildSectionFirstCursor = (cursor) => cursor === null || cursor === undefined || String(cursor || '').trim() === '';

  const shouldShowChildPagingDebugControls = () => {
    try {
      if (child?.ui?.showPagedDetailLoading === true) return true;
      if (child?.ui?.showSectionPagingControls === true) return true;
      if (child?.ui?.loadPaymentIssueSupportSections === true) return true;
      if (window.__CLOUDTMS_DEBUG_BANKING_PAY_CHILD_PAGING === true) return true;
      if (window.__BANKING_PAY_CHILD_PAGING_DEBUG === true) return true;
      const ls = window.localStorage;
      if (ls && String(ls.getItem('cloudtms.debug.bankingPayChildPaging') || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'true') return true;
    } catch {}
    return false;
  };

  const getAutoLoadSectionsForTab = (tabKey) => {
    const key = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey) || 'overview';
    const sectionKeys = getChildSectionsForTab(key);
    if (key === 'payment_issues' && shouldShowChildPagingDebugControls() !== true) {
      return sectionKeys.slice(0, 1);
    }
    return sectionKeys;
  };

  const syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta = (sectionState = null) => {
    try {
      const state = createChildSectionState('current_payment_status', sectionState || child.sections?.current_payment_status || {});
      child.correction = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction)) ? child.correction : {};
      child.correction.current_payment_status_loading = state.loading === true;
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusLoading = state.loading === true;
      child.correction.current_payment_status_loaded = state.loaded === true;
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusLoaded = state.loaded === true;
      child.correction.current_payment_status_error = String(state.error || '');
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusError = String(state.error || '');
      child.correction.current_payment_status_failed_request_key = String(state.failed_request_key || '');
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusFailedRequestKey = String(state.failed_request_key || '');
      child.correction.current_payment_status_section = state;
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusSection = state;
    } catch {}
  };

  const setChildSectionLocalError = (sectionKey, message, errorPayload = null, requestKey = '', cursorValue = null) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (!key) return null;
    ensureChildLazySections(child.data);
    const latest = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
    const msg = String(message || 'Section page could not be loaded.').trim() || 'Section page could not be loaded.';
    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const nextState = {
      ...latest,
      loading: false,
      loaded: latest.loaded === true,
      error: msg,
      in_flight_key: '',
      inFlightKey: '',
      pending_request_key: '',
      pendingRequestKey: '',
      last_request_key: String(requestKey || latest.last_request_key || ''),
      lastRequestKey: String(requestKey || latest.lastRequestKey || ''),
      failed_request_key: String(requestKey || latest.failed_request_key || ''),
      failedRequestKey: String(requestKey || latest.failedRequestKey || ''),
      failed_at_utc: nowIso,
      failedAtUtc: nowIso,
      failed_cursor: cursorValue ?? latest.failed_cursor ?? null,
      failedCursor: cursorValue ?? latest.failedCursor ?? null,
      last_error_payload: errorPayload || latest.last_error_payload || null,
      lastErrorPayload: errorPayload || latest.lastErrorPayload || null
    };
    child.sections[key] = nextState;
    child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
    child.sectionPages[key] = nextState;
    child.lazy_sections = child.sections;
    if (key === 'current_payment_status') syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta(nextState);
    return nextState;
  };

  const clearChildSectionTimer = (timerKey) => {
    try {
      const timers = (child.__firstPageTimers && typeof child.__firstPageTimers === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.__firstPageTimers)) ? child.__firstPageTimers : {};
      const handle = timers[timerKey];
      if (handle) clearTimeout(handle);
      delete timers[timerKey];
      child.__firstPageTimers = timers;
    } catch {}
  };

  const extractLiveVersionsFromBatchPayload = (payload) => {
    const source = (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) ? payload : {};
    const batch = (source.batch && typeof source.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.batch)) ? source.batch : {};
    const liveSignal = (source.live_signal && typeof source.live_signal === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.live_signal)) ? source.live_signal : {};
    return {
      live_signal_version: source.live_signal_version ?? source.version ?? liveSignal.version ?? batch.live_signal_version ?? batch.version ?? null,
      payment_status_version: source.payment_status_version ?? liveSignal.payment_status_version ?? batch.payment_status_version ?? null,
      correction_progress_version: source.correction_progress_version ?? liveSignal.correction_progress_version ?? batch.correction_progress_version ?? null,
      alert_version: source.alert_version ?? liveSignal.alert_version ?? batch.alert_version ?? null,
      overview_version: source.overview_version ?? liveSignal.overview_version ?? batch.overview_version ?? null
    };
  };

  const syncChildKnownLiveVersions = (payload) => {
    try {
      const versions = extractLiveVersionsFromBatchPayload(payload);
      child.liveWatch = (child.liveWatch && typeof child.liveWatch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.liveWatch)) ? child.liveWatch : {};
      child.liveWatch.knownVersions = {
        ...(child.liveWatch.knownVersions && typeof child.liveWatch.knownVersions === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.liveWatch.knownVersions) ? child.liveWatch.knownVersions : {}),
        ...versions
      };
      child.live_signal_version = versions.live_signal_version;
      child.payment_status_version = versions.payment_status_version;
      child.correction_progress_version = versions.correction_progress_version;
      child.alert_version = versions.alert_version;
      child.overview_version = versions.overview_version;
      return versions;
    } catch {
      return { live_signal_version: null, payment_status_version: null, correction_progress_version: null, alert_version: null, overview_version: null };
    }
  };

  const abortChildLiveWatchRequest = () => {
    try {
      const ctrl = child.__liveWatchAbortController || child.__watchAbortController || child.liveWatchAbortController;
      if (ctrl && typeof ctrl.abort === 'function') ctrl.abort();
    } catch {}
    try { child.__liveWatchAbortController = null; } catch {}
    try { child.__watchAbortController = null; } catch {}
    try { child.liveWatchAbortController = null; } catch {}
  };

  const stopChildLiveWatch = () => {
    try { if (typeof stopBankingPayBatchLiveWatch === 'function') stopBankingPayBatchLiveWatch(id); } catch {}
    try {
      const handle = child.liveWatch && typeof child.liveWatch === 'object' ? child.liveWatch.handle : null;
      if (handle && typeof handle.abort === 'function') handle.abort();
      if (handle && typeof handle.stop === 'function') handle.stop();
      if (handle && typeof handle.cancel === 'function') handle.cancel();
    } catch {}
    abortChildLiveWatchRequest();
    try {
      if (child.__liveWatchTimer) {
        clearTimeout(child.__liveWatchTimer);
        clearInterval(child.__liveWatchTimer);
        child.__liveWatchTimer = null;
      }
    } catch {}
    try {
      if (child.__watchTimer) {
        clearTimeout(child.__watchTimer);
        clearInterval(child.__watchTimer);
        child.__watchTimer = null;
      }
    } catch {}
    try {
      child.liveWatch = (child.liveWatch && typeof child.liveWatch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.liveWatch)) ? child.liveWatch : {};
      child.liveWatch.running = false;
      child.liveWatch.started = false;
    } catch {}
  };

  const startChildLiveWatchFromPayload = (payload, { initial = false } = {}) => {
    const versions = syncChildKnownLiveVersions(payload);
    try {
      if (typeof startBankingPayBatchLiveWatch !== 'function') return versions;
      child.liveWatch = (child.liveWatch && typeof child.liveWatch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.liveWatch)) ? child.liveWatch : {};
      if (child.liveWatch.running === true && child.liveWatch.batchId === id && child.liveWatch.openToken === child.openToken) return versions;
      abortChildLiveWatchRequest();
      const controller = (typeof AbortController !== 'undefined') ? new AbortController() : null;
      child.__liveWatchAbortController = controller;
      child.liveWatch = {
        ...child.liveWatch,
        running: true,
        started: true,
        batchId: id,
        openToken: child.openToken,
        knownVersions: versions,
        startedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      const watchHandle = startBankingPayBatchLiveWatch(id, {
        source: 'openBankingPayBatchChildModal',
        childOpenToken: child.openToken,
        openToken: child.openToken,
        initial: initial === true,
        known_version: versions.live_signal_version,
        known_payment_status_version: versions.payment_status_version,
        known_correction_progress_version: versions.correction_progress_version,
        known_alert_version: versions.alert_version,
        known_overview_version: versions.overview_version,
        current_tab: child.ui?.activeTabKey || 'overview',
        current_section_json: { child: true, active_tab: child.ui?.activeTabKey || 'overview' },
        signal: controller ? controller.signal : undefined
      });
      if (watchHandle && typeof watchHandle === 'object') child.liveWatch.handle = watchHandle;
    } catch {}
    return versions;
  };

  const ensureChildLazySections = (data = null) => {
    const source = (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data)) ? data : child.data;
    const batch = source && source.batch && typeof source.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.batch) ? source.batch : {};
    const available = Array.isArray(source?.available_sections)
      ? source.available_sections
      : Array.isArray(batch.available_sections)
        ? batch.available_sections
        : CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS;
    const sectionKeys = Array.from(new Set(
      available
        .map((section) => normaliseChildSectionKey(section))
        .filter(Boolean)
    ));
    const finalSectionKeys = sectionKeys.length ? sectionKeys : CHILD_LAZY_SECTION_KEYS;

    child.sections = (child.sections && typeof child.sections === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sections)) ? child.sections : {};
    child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
    for (const sectionKey of finalSectionKeys) {
      child.sections[sectionKey] = createChildSectionState(sectionKey, child.sections[sectionKey]);
      child.sectionPages[sectionKey] = createChildSectionState(sectionKey, child.sectionPages[sectionKey] || child.sections[sectionKey]);
    }

    child.lazy_sections = child.sections;
    child.available_sections = finalSectionKeys;

    const pageSizeRaw = Number(source?.recommended_page_size ?? batch.recommended_page_size ?? child.recommended_page_size ?? 100);
    child.recommended_page_size = Number.isFinite(pageSizeRaw) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Math.trunc(pageSizeRaw))) : 100;

    return child.sections;
  };

  const isBootstrapBatchPayload = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data)) ? data : null;
    const b = d && d.batch && typeof d.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.batch) ? d.batch : null;
    return !!(d && (d.bootstrap_only === true || b?.bootstrap_only === true));
  };

  const getChildSectionsForTab = (tabKey) => {
    const key = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey) || 'overview';
    if (key === 'overview') return ['overview_items'];
    if (key === 'payment_issues') return ['current_payment_status', 'finance_case_groups', 'items', 'transfers', 'events', 'auth_history'];
    if (key === 'paye_worksheet') return ['candidates', 'items'];
    if (key === 'remittance') return ['remittances', 'communications'];
    return [];
  };

  const fmtUtcToUk = (iso) => {
    try {
      if (!iso) return '';
      const d = new Date(String(iso));
      if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(iso);
      const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone: 'Europe/London',
        year: 'numeric',
        month: '2-digit',
        day: '2-digit',
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        second: '2-digit',
        hour12: false
      });
      return fmt.format(d).replace(',', '');
    } catch {
      return String(iso || '');
    }
  };

  const formatIsoToUkLocal = (ymd) => {
    const s = String(ymd || '').trim();
    if (!s) return '';
    try { if (typeof formatIsoToUk === 'function') return String(formatIsoToUk(s) || ''); } catch {}
    const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
    if (m) return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
    return s;
  };

  const parseUkDateToIsoLocal = (raw) => {
    const s = String(raw || '').trim();
    if (!s) return '';
    try {
      if (typeof parseUkDateToIso === 'function') {
        const iso0 = parseUkDateToIso(s);
        const iso = String(iso0 || '').trim();
        return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(iso) ? iso : '';
      }
    } catch {}
    const m = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
    if (m) return `${m[3]}-${m[2]}-${m[1]}`;
    return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s) ? s : '';
  };

  const closeTop = () => {
    try {
      const btn = document.getElementById('btnCloseModal');
      if (btn && typeof btn.click === 'function') { btn.click(); return; }
    } catch {}
    try { if (typeof closeModal === 'function') closeModal(); } catch {}
  };

  const closeCancelOrChildFramesAfterBatchRemoval = async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
      let kindNow = '';
      try {
        const fr = (typeof window.__getModalFrame === 'function') ? window.__getModalFrame() : null;
        kindNow = String(fr?.kind || '').trim();
      } catch {
        kindNow = '';
      }

      if (kindNow !== KIND && kindNow !== 'import-summary-banking-cancel-pay') break;

      try {
        if (typeof closeCurrentModalFrameSafely === 'function') {
          closeCurrentModalFrameSafely({ expectedKind: kindNow });
        } else {
          closeTop();
        }
      } catch {
        try { closeTop(); } catch {}
      }

      try { await Promise.resolve(); } catch {}
    }
  };

  const ensureChildModalShellClass = () => {
    try {
      const modal = document.getElementById('modal');
      if (modal && modal.classList) modal.classList.add(MODAL_CLASS);
    } catch {}
  };

  const removeChildModalShellClass = () => {
    try {
      const modal = document.getElementById('modal');
      if (modal && modal.classList) modal.classList.remove(MODAL_CLASS);
    } catch {}
  };

  const markDirty = () => {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new Event('modal-dirty')); } catch {}
  };

  const currentChildStateMatches = () => {
    try {
      const ctx = (window.modalCtx && typeof window.modalCtx === 'object') ? window.modalCtx : null;
      if (!ctx || String(ctx.entity || '') !== 'banking') return false;
      const c2 = (ctx.banking && ctx.banking.pay && ctx.banking.pay.child && typeof ctx.banking.pay.child === 'object') ? ctx.banking.pay.child : null;
      if (!c2) return false;
      if (String(c2.batchId || '') !== id) return false;
      if (String(c2.openToken || '') !== String(child.openToken || '')) return false;
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const topChildFrameConflictsWithThisChild = () => {
    try {
      const fr = getTopModalFrameSafe();
      if (!fr || String(fr.kind || '') !== KIND) return false;
      const tok = getFrameOpenTokenSafe(fr);
      const bid = getFrameBatchIdSafe(fr);
      if (tok && tok !== String(child.openToken || '')) return true;
      if (bid && bid !== id) return true;
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const shouldApplyChildMutation = (expectedOpenToken = '', expectedSeq = null) => {
    try {
      const tok = String(expectedOpenToken || child.openToken || '').trim();
      if (tok && tok !== String(child.openToken || '').trim()) return false;
      if (tok && !currentChildStateMatches()) return false;
      if (expectedSeq !== null && expectedSeq !== undefined && Number(child.__loadSeq || 0) !== Number(expectedSeq)) return false;
      if (topChildFrameConflictsWithThisChild()) return false;
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const stillTopIsThisChild = () => {
    try {
      const fr = getTopModalFrameSafe();
      if (!fr) return false;
      if (String(fr.kind || '') !== KIND) return false;
      const ctx = (window.modalCtx && typeof window.modalCtx === 'object') ? window.modalCtx : null;
      if (!ctx || String(ctx.entity || '') !== 'banking') return false;
      if (fr._ctxRef !== ctx) return false;
      const frameToken = getFrameOpenTokenSafe(fr);
      if (frameToken && frameToken !== String(child.openToken || '')) return false;
      const frameBatchId = getFrameBatchIdSafe(fr);
      if (frameBatchId && frameBatchId !== id) return false;
      return currentChildStateMatches();
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const deriveBatchObj = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    const b = d && d.batch && typeof d.batch === 'object' ? d.batch : null;
    return b || null;
  };

  const childBatchStatusFirstText = (...values) => {
    for (const value of values) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const nested = childBatchStatusFirstText(...value);
        if (nested) return nested;
        continue;
      }
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
      if (text) return text;
    }
    return '';
  };

  const childBatchStatusReadCount = (...values) => {
    for (const value of values) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined || String(value).trim() === '') continue;
      const n = Number(value);
      if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) return Math.trunc(n);
    }
    return 0;
  };

  const childBatchStatusReadBool = (value, fallback = false) => {
    if (value === true) return true;
    if (value === false) return false;
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (['true', 't', '1', 'yes', 'y', 'on'].includes(text)) return true;
    if (['false', 'f', '0', 'no', 'n', 'off'].includes(text)) return false;
    return !!fallback;
  };

  const formatBankingPayBatchStatusLabelFallback = (dataPayload = null) => {
    const source = (dataPayload && typeof dataPayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(dataPayload)) ? dataPayload : {};
    const batchObj = deriveBatchObj(source) || {};
    const displaySummary = (source.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof source.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.pay_batch_display_summary))
      ? source.pay_batch_display_summary
      : ((source.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof source.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.payBatchDisplaySummary))
          ? source.payBatchDisplaySummary
          : ((source.display_summary && typeof source.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.display_summary))
              ? source.display_summary
              : ((source.displaySummary && typeof source.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.displaySummary))
                  ? source.displaySummary
                  : ((batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary))
                      ? batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary
                      : ((batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary))
                          ? batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary
                          : ((batchObj.display_summary && typeof batchObj.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.display_summary))
                              ? batchObj.display_summary
                              : ((batchObj.displaySummary && typeof batchObj.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.displaySummary)) ? batchObj.displaySummary : {})))))));
    const outcomeSummary = (source.payment_outcome_summary && typeof source.payment_outcome_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.payment_outcome_summary))
      ? source.payment_outcome_summary
      : ((source.paymentOutcomeSummary && typeof source.paymentOutcomeSummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.paymentOutcomeSummary))
          ? source.paymentOutcomeSummary
          : ((batchObj.payment_outcome_summary && typeof batchObj.payment_outcome_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.payment_outcome_summary))
              ? batchObj.payment_outcome_summary
              : ((batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary && typeof batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary)) ? batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary : displaySummary)));

    const rawStatus = String(childBatchStatusFirstText(
      batchObj.batch_status_raw,
      batchObj.batchStatusRaw,
      source.batch_status_raw,
      source.batchStatusRaw,
      batchObj.status,
      source.status
    ) || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const pendingCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(
      outcomeSummary.pending_bank_outcome_count,
      outcomeSummary.pendingBankOutcomeCount,
      batchObj.pending_bank_outcome_count,
      batchObj.pendingBankOutcomeCount,
      source.pending_bank_outcome_count,
      source.pendingBankOutcomeCount,
      displaySummary.pending_bank_outcome_count,
      displaySummary.pendingBankOutcomeCount
    );
    const unknownCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(
      outcomeSummary.unknown_bank_outcome_count,
      outcomeSummary.unknownBankOutcomeCount,
      batchObj.unknown_bank_outcome_count,
      batchObj.unknownBankOutcomeCount,
      source.unknown_bank_outcome_count,
      source.unknownBankOutcomeCount,
      displaySummary.unknown_bank_outcome_count,
      displaySummary.unknownBankOutcomeCount
    );
    const terminalSuccessCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(
      outcomeSummary.terminal_success_payment_count,
      outcomeSummary.terminalSuccessPaymentCount,
      batchObj.terminal_success_payment_count,
      batchObj.terminalSuccessPaymentCount,
      source.terminal_success_payment_count,
      source.terminalSuccessPaymentCount,
      displaySummary.terminal_success_payment_count,
      displaySummary.terminalSuccessPaymentCount
    );
    const terminalFailedCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(
      outcomeSummary.terminal_failed_payment_count,
      outcomeSummary.terminalFailedPaymentCount,
      batchObj.terminal_failed_payment_count,
      batchObj.terminalFailedPaymentCount,
      source.terminal_failed_payment_count,
      source.terminalFailedPaymentCount,
      displaySummary.terminal_failed_payment_count,
      displaySummary.terminalFailedPaymentCount
    );

    const normaliseExplicitBatchStatusLabel = (labelValue) => {
      const rawLabel = String(labelValue == null ? '' : labelValue).trim();
      const upperLabel = rawLabel.toUpperCase();
      if (!rawLabel) return '';
      if (upperLabel === 'FAILED') return 'Completed with failed payments';
      if (upperLabel === 'SETTLED' || upperLabel === 'COMPLETED' || upperLabel === 'COMMITTED') return 'Completed';
      if (upperLabel === 'WAITING_BANK_CONFIRM') {
        if (pendingCount > 0 || unknownCount > 0) return 'Waiting for bank confirmation';
        if (terminalFailedCount > 0) return 'Completed with failed payments';
        if (terminalSuccessCount > 0) return 'Completed';
        return 'Waiting for bank confirmation';
      }
      return rawLabel;
    };
    const explicitLabel = normaliseExplicitBatchStatusLabel(childBatchStatusFirstText(
      batchObj.batch_status_label,
      batchObj.batchStatusLabel,
      source.batch_status_label,
      source.batchStatusLabel,
      outcomeSummary.batch_status_label,
      outcomeSummary.batchStatusLabel,
      displaySummary.batch_status_label,
      displaySummary.batchStatusLabel
    ));
    if (explicitLabel) return explicitLabel;
    if (rawStatus === 'FAILED') return 'Completed with failed payments';
    if (rawStatus === 'SETTLED' || rawStatus === 'COMPLETED' || rawStatus === 'COMMITTED') return 'Completed';
    if (rawStatus === 'WAITING_BANK_CONFIRM') {
      if (pendingCount > 0 || unknownCount > 0) return 'Waiting for bank confirmation';
      if (terminalFailedCount > 0) return 'Completed with failed payments';
      if (terminalSuccessCount > 0) return 'Completed';
      return 'Waiting for bank confirmation';
    }
    if (rawStatus === 'PARTIAL') return terminalFailedCount > 0 ? 'Completed with failed payments' : 'Partially completed';
    if (rawStatus === 'DRAFT' || rawStatus === 'DRAFT_CREATED') return 'Draft';
    if (rawStatus === 'READY') return 'Ready';
    if (rawStatus === 'CANCELLED' || rawStatus === 'CANCELED') return 'Cancelled';
    return rawStatus || '';
  };

  const syncBankingPayBatchStatusDisplayFields = (dataPayload = null) => {
    const source = (dataPayload && typeof dataPayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(dataPayload)) ? dataPayload : dataPayload;
    if (!source || typeof source !== 'object' || Array.isArray(source)) return source;
    const batchObj = deriveBatchObj(source);
    if (!batchObj || typeof batchObj !== 'object' || Array.isArray(batchObj)) return source;

    const displaySummary = (source.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof source.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.pay_batch_display_summary))
      ? source.pay_batch_display_summary
      : ((source.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof source.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.payBatchDisplaySummary))
          ? source.payBatchDisplaySummary
          : ((source.display_summary && typeof source.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.display_summary))
              ? source.display_summary
              : ((source.displaySummary && typeof source.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.displaySummary))
                  ? source.displaySummary
                  : ((batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary))
                      ? batchObj.pay_batch_display_summary
                      : ((batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary))
                          ? batchObj.payBatchDisplaySummary
                          : ((batchObj.display_summary && typeof batchObj.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.display_summary))
                              ? batchObj.display_summary
                              : ((batchObj.displaySummary && typeof batchObj.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.displaySummary)) ? batchObj.displaySummary : {})))))));
    const outcomeSummary = (source.payment_outcome_summary && typeof source.payment_outcome_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.payment_outcome_summary))
      ? source.payment_outcome_summary
      : ((source.paymentOutcomeSummary && typeof source.paymentOutcomeSummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(source.paymentOutcomeSummary))
          ? source.paymentOutcomeSummary
          : ((batchObj.payment_outcome_summary && typeof batchObj.payment_outcome_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.payment_outcome_summary))
              ? batchObj.payment_outcome_summary
              : ((batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary && typeof batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary)) ? batchObj.paymentOutcomeSummary : displaySummary)));
    const rawStatus = childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batch_status_raw, batchObj.batchStatusRaw, source.batch_status_raw, source.batchStatusRaw, batchObj.status, source.status);
    const pendingCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(outcomeSummary.pending_bank_outcome_count, outcomeSummary.pendingBankOutcomeCount, batchObj.pending_bank_outcome_count, batchObj.pendingBankOutcomeCount, source.pending_bank_outcome_count, source.pendingBankOutcomeCount, displaySummary.pending_bank_outcome_count, displaySummary.pendingBankOutcomeCount);
    const unknownCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(outcomeSummary.unknown_bank_outcome_count, outcomeSummary.unknownBankOutcomeCount, batchObj.unknown_bank_outcome_count, batchObj.unknownBankOutcomeCount, source.unknown_bank_outcome_count, source.unknownBankOutcomeCount, displaySummary.unknown_bank_outcome_count, displaySummary.unknownBankOutcomeCount);
    const terminalFailedCount = childBatchStatusReadCount(outcomeSummary.terminal_failed_payment_count, outcomeSummary.terminalFailedPaymentCount, batchObj.terminal_failed_payment_count, batchObj.terminalFailedPaymentCount, source.terminal_failed_payment_count, source.terminalFailedPaymentCount, displaySummary.terminal_failed_payment_count, displaySummary.terminalFailedPaymentCount);
    const label = formatBankingPayBatchStatusLabelFallback(source);
    const terminalWithFailedPayments = childBatchStatusReadBool(
      childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batch_terminal_with_failed_payments, batchObj.batchTerminalWithFailedPayments, source.batch_terminal_with_failed_payments, source.batchTerminalWithFailedPayments, outcomeSummary.batch_terminal_with_failed_payments, outcomeSummary.batchTerminalWithFailedPayments),
      String(rawStatus || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'FAILED' || (terminalFailedCount > 0 && pendingCount === 0 && unknownCount === 0)
    );

    batchObj.batch_status_raw = childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batch_status_raw, rawStatus);
    batchObj.batchStatusRaw = childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batchStatusRaw, batchObj.batch_status_raw);
    batchObj.batch_status_label = childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batch_status_label, label);
    batchObj.batchStatusLabel = childBatchStatusFirstText(batchObj.batchStatusLabel, batchObj.batch_status_label);
    batchObj.batch_terminal_with_failed_payments = terminalWithFailedPayments;
    batchObj.batchTerminalWithFailedPayments = terminalWithFailedPayments;

    source.batch_status_raw = childBatchStatusFirstText(source.batch_status_raw, batchObj.batch_status_raw);
    source.batchStatusRaw = childBatchStatusFirstText(source.batchStatusRaw, source.batch_status_raw);
    source.batch_status_label = childBatchStatusFirstText(source.batch_status_label, batchObj.batch_status_label);
    source.batchStatusLabel = childBatchStatusFirstText(source.batchStatusLabel, source.batch_status_label);
    source.batch_terminal_with_failed_payments = terminalWithFailedPayments;
    source.batchTerminalWithFailedPayments = terminalWithFailedPayments;
    return source;
  };

  const syncChildCsvEvidenceFromData = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data)) ? data : {};
    const b = (d.batch && typeof d.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.batch)) ? d.batch : {};
    const csvSummary = (d.bank_csv_export_json && typeof d.bank_csv_export_json === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.bank_csv_export_json))
      ? d.bank_csv_export_json
      : ((b.bank_csv_export_json && typeof b.bank_csv_export_json === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.bank_csv_export_json)) ? b.bank_csv_export_json : null);
    const ev = (typeof deriveBankingPayBankCsvEvidence === 'function')
      ? deriveBankingPayBankCsvEvidence(data)
      : { generated_at_utc: null };
    child.csv = (child.csv && typeof child.csv === 'object') ? child.csv : {};
    child.csv.generated_at_utc =
      ev.generated_at_utc ||
      csvSummary?.generated_at_utc ||
      d.csv_file_generated_at_utc ||
      b.csv_file_generated_at_utc ||
      null;
    child.csv.filename =
      ev.filename ||
      csvSummary?.filename ||
      d.csv_filename ||
      b.csv_filename ||
      child.csv.filename ||
      '';
    child.csv.row_count =
      ev.row_count ??
      csvSummary?.row_count ??
      d.csv_row_count ??
      b.csv_row_count ??
      child.csv.row_count ??
      null;
    child.csv.total_amount =
      ev.total_amount ??
      csvSummary?.total_amount ??
      d.csv_total_amount ??
      b.csv_total_amount ??
      child.csv.total_amount ??
      null;
    child.csv.evidence_summary = {
      ...(child.csv.evidence_summary && typeof child.csv.evidence_summary === 'object' ? child.csv.evidence_summary : {}),
      ...(csvSummary || {}),
      generated_at_utc: child.csv.generated_at_utc,
      filename: child.csv.filename,
      row_count: child.csv.row_count,
      total_amount: child.csv.total_amount
    };
  };
  const deriveTransfers = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    return Array.isArray(d?.transfers) ? d.transfers : [];
  };

   const deriveCandidates = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    return Array.isArray(d?.candidates) ? d.candidates : [];
  };

  const EMPTY_DRAFT_NO_ACTIVE_PAYMENTS_MESSAGE = 'This draft batch has no active payments. Delete the draft or create a new batch.';

  const firstFiniteNonNegativeInteger = (...values) => {
    for (const value of values) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) continue;
      const s = String(value).trim();
      if (!s) continue;
      const n = Number(s);
      if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0) return Math.trunc(n);
    }
    return null;
  };

  const firstFiniteNumber = (...values) => {
    for (const value of values) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) continue;
      const s = String(value).trim();
      if (!s) continue;
      const n = Number(s);
      if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n;
    }
    return null;
  };

  const countCandidateLineEntriesForBatch = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    if (!d || !Array.isArray(d.candidate_lines)) return null;

    let count = 0;
    for (const entry of d.candidate_lines) {
      if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') continue;

      const normalizedBlock = (entry.candidate_lines_normalized && typeof entry.candidate_lines_normalized === 'object') ? entry.candidate_lines_normalized : null;
      if (normalizedBlock) {
        if (Array.isArray(normalizedBlock.tsLines)) count += normalizedBlock.tsLines.filter(Boolean).length;
        if (Array.isArray(normalizedBlock.nonTsLines)) count += normalizedBlock.nonTsLines.filter(Boolean).length;
        continue;
      }

      if (Array.isArray(entry.ts_lines) || Array.isArray(entry.non_ts_lines)) {
        if (Array.isArray(entry.ts_lines)) count += entry.ts_lines.filter(Boolean).length;
        if (Array.isArray(entry.non_ts_lines)) count += entry.non_ts_lines.filter(Boolean).length;
        continue;
      }

      if (Array.isArray(entry.lines)) { count += entry.lines.filter(Boolean).length; continue; }
      if (Array.isArray(entry.line_items)) { count += entry.line_items.filter(Boolean).length; continue; }
      if (Array.isArray(entry.items)) { count += entry.items.filter(Boolean).length; continue; }
      if (Array.isArray(entry.breakdown_lines)) { count += entry.breakdown_lines.filter(Boolean).length; continue; }
      if (Array.isArray(entry.timesheets)) { count += entry.timesheets.filter(Boolean).length; continue; }

      count += 1;
    }

    return count;
  };

  const getActivePaymentStateForBatch = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    const b = deriveBatchObj(d) || {};
    const displaySummary = (d?.display_summary && typeof d.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.display_summary))
      ? d.display_summary
      : ((d?.displaySummary && typeof d.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.displaySummary))
          ? d.displaySummary
          : ((d?.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof d.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.pay_batch_display_summary))
              ? d.pay_batch_display_summary
              : ((d?.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof d.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.payBatchDisplaySummary))
                  ? d.payBatchDisplaySummary
                  : ((b?.display_summary && typeof b.display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.display_summary))
                      ? b.display_summary
                      : ((b?.displaySummary && typeof b.displaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.displaySummary))
                          ? b.displaySummary
                          : ((b?.pay_batch_display_summary && typeof b.pay_batch_display_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.pay_batch_display_summary))
                              ? b.pay_batch_display_summary
                              : ((b?.payBatchDisplaySummary && typeof b.payBatchDisplaySummary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.payBatchDisplaySummary)) ? b.payBatchDisplaySummary : {})))))));
    const durableCounts = (d?.durable_counts && typeof d.durable_counts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.durable_counts))
      ? d.durable_counts
      : ((d?.durableCounts && typeof d.durableCounts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.durableCounts))
          ? d.durableCounts
          : ((b?.durable_counts && typeof b.durable_counts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.durable_counts))
              ? b.durable_counts
              : ((b?.durableCounts && typeof b.durableCounts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b.durableCounts)) ? b.durableCounts : {})));
    const stx = String(b.status || d?.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const isDraftLikeBatch = (stx === 'DRAFT' || stx === 'DRAFT_CREATED');

    const numericValues = (...values) => values.map((value) => {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
      const s = String(value).trim();
      if (!s) return null;
      const n = Number(s);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
    }).filter((value) => value !== null);
    const firstPositiveOrFirstFinite = (...values) => {
      const nums = numericValues(...values);
      const positive = nums.find((value) => value > 0);
      return positive !== undefined ? positive : (nums.length ? nums[0] : null);
    };
    const firstPositiveOrFirstNonNegativeInteger = (...values) => {
      const nums = numericValues(...values).filter((value) => value >= 0).map((value) => Math.trunc(value));
      const positive = nums.find((value) => value > 0);
      return positive !== undefined ? positive : (nums.length ? nums[0] : null);
    };

    const durableCount = firstPositiveOrFirstNonNegativeInteger(
      displaySummary.item_count,
      displaySummary.itemCount,
      displaySummary.pay_batch_item_count,
      displaySummary.payBatchItemCount,
      durableCounts.item_count,
      durableCounts.itemCount,
      durableCounts.pay_batch_item_count,
      durableCounts.payBatchItemCount,
      d?.durable_item_count,
      d?.durableItemCount,
      d?.item_count,
      d?.itemCount,
      b?.durable_item_count,
      b?.durableItemCount,
      b?.item_count,
      b?.itemCount
    );
    const durableAmount = firstPositiveOrFirstFinite(
      displaySummary.total_payable,
      displaySummary.totalPayable,
      displaySummary.total_amount_inc_vat,
      displaySummary.totalAmountIncVat,
      displaySummary.total_amount,
      displaySummary.totalAmount,
      durableCounts.total_payable,
      durableCounts.totalPayable,
      durableCounts.total_amount_inc_vat,
      durableCounts.totalAmountIncVat,
      durableCounts.total_amount,
      durableCounts.totalAmount,
      d?.durable_total_payable,
      d?.durableTotalPayable,
      d?.total_payable,
      d?.totalPayable,
      d?.total_amount_inc_vat,
      d?.totalAmountIncVat,
      b?.durable_total_payable,
      b?.durableTotalPayable,
      b?.total_payable,
      b?.totalPayable,
      b?.total_amount_inc_vat,
      b?.totalAmountIncVat
    );

    const explicitActiveCount = firstPositiveOrFirstNonNegativeInteger(
      d?.active_item_count,
      d?.active_items,
      d?.active_batch_item_count,
      d?.active_batch_items,
      d?.active_payment_item_count,
      d?.active_pay_batch_item_count,
      d?.active_batch_item_count_after,
      b?.active_item_count,
      b?.active_items,
      b?.active_batch_item_count,
      b?.active_batch_items,
      b?.active_payment_item_count,
      b?.active_pay_batch_item_count,
      b?.active_batch_item_count_after
    );

    const explicitActiveAmount = firstPositiveOrFirstFinite(
      d?.active_amount_inc_vat,
      d?.active_batch_amount_inc_vat,
      d?.active_payment_amount_inc_vat,
      d?.active_bank_out,
      d?.active_batch_amount_inc_vat_after,
      b?.active_amount_inc_vat,
      b?.active_batch_amount_inc_vat,
      b?.active_payment_amount_inc_vat,
      b?.active_bank_out,
      b?.active_batch_amount_inc_vat_after
    );

    const dataItems = Array.isArray(d?.items) ? d.items : null;
    const activeCountFromItems = dataItems ? dataItems.filter((item) => {
      if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return false;
      if (item.is_voided === true || item.voided === true) return false;
      const itemStatus = String(item.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (itemStatus === 'VOIDED' || itemStatus === 'CANCELLED' || itemStatus === 'CANCELED') return false;
      const n = firstFiniteNumber(
        item.frozen_target_amount_inc_vat,
        item.frozenTargetAmountIncVat,
        item.amount_inc_vat,
        item.amountIncVat,
        item.frozen_target_amount_ex_vat,
        item.frozenTargetAmountExVat,
        item.amount_ex_vat,
        item.amountExVat,
        item.amount
      );
      return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0;
    }).length : null;

    const activeAmountFromItems = dataItems ? dataItems.reduce((total, item) => {
      if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return total;
      if (item.is_voided === true || item.voided === true) return total;
      const itemStatus = String(item.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (itemStatus === 'VOIDED' || itemStatus === 'CANCELLED' || itemStatus === 'CANCELED') return total;
      const n = firstFiniteNumber(
        item.frozen_target_amount_inc_vat,
        item.frozenTargetAmountIncVat,
        item.amount_inc_vat,
        item.amountIncVat,
        item.frozen_target_amount_ex_vat,
        item.frozenTargetAmountExVat,
        item.amount_ex_vat,
        item.amountExVat,
        item.amount
      );
      return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? total + n : total;
    }, 0) : null;

    const activeCountFromLines = countCandidateLineEntriesForBatch(d);
    const knownReliable = durableCount !== null || durableAmount !== null || activeCountFromItems !== null || activeAmountFromItems !== null || activeCountFromLines !== null;
    const hasPayableContent = (durableCount !== null && durableCount > 0) ||
      (durableAmount !== null && durableAmount > 0) ||
      (explicitActiveCount !== null && explicitActiveCount > 0) ||
      (explicitActiveAmount !== null && explicitActiveAmount > 0) ||
      (activeCountFromItems !== null && activeCountFromItems > 0) ||
      (activeAmountFromItems !== null && activeAmountFromItems > 0) ||
      (activeCountFromLines !== null && activeCountFromLines > 0);
    const count = firstPositiveOrFirstNonNegativeInteger(durableCount, explicitActiveCount, activeCountFromItems, activeCountFromLines) ?? 0;
    const amount = firstPositiveOrFirstFinite(durableAmount, explicitActiveAmount, activeAmountFromItems);

    return {
      known: knownReliable,
      count,
      amount_inc_vat: amount,
      hasActivePayments: hasPayableContent || !knownReliable,
      emptyDraftNoActivePayments: isDraftLikeBatch && knownReliable && !hasPayableContent,
      message: EMPTY_DRAFT_NO_ACTIVE_PAYMENTS_MESSAGE
    };
  };

  const hasActivePaymentsForBatch = (data) => getActivePaymentStateForBatch(data).hasActivePayments === true;

  const isEmptyDraftWithNoActivePayments = (data) => getActivePaymentStateForBatch(data).emptyDraftNoActivePayments === true;

  const blockNoActivePaymentsAction = async () => {
    const message = EMPTY_DRAFT_NO_ACTIVE_PAYMENTS_MESSAGE;
    child.error = message;
    try { toast(message); } catch {}
    await rerenderChild();
    return true;
  };

  const getCandidateExpansionKey = (candidate) => {
    const c = (candidate && typeof candidate === 'object') ? candidate : {};
    return String(
      c.pay_batch_candidate_id ||
      c.payBatchCandidateId ||
      c.id ||
      c.candidate_row_id ||
      c.candidateRowId ||
      c.candidate_id ||
      ''
    ).trim();
  };

  const getCandidateLegacyExpansionKey = (candidate) => {
    const c = (candidate && typeof candidate === 'object') ? candidate : {};
    return String(c.candidate_id || '').trim();
  };

  const normalizeCandidateExpansionStateForData = (data) => {
    try {
      const cands = deriveCandidates(data);
      child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
      const current = (child.ui.expanded && typeof child.ui.expanded === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.ui.expanded)) ? child.ui.expanded : {};
      const next = {};

      for (const candidate of cands) {
        const canonicalKey = getCandidateExpansionKey(candidate);
        const legacyKey = getCandidateLegacyExpansionKey(candidate);
        if (!canonicalKey) continue;
        if (current[canonicalKey] || (legacyKey && legacyKey !== canonicalKey && current[legacyKey])) {
          next[canonicalKey] = true;
        }
      }

      child.ui.expanded = next;
    } catch {}
  };
  const deriveChildCommunicationsState = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    const b = (d && d.batch && typeof d.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.batch)) ? d.batch : null;

    const summary =
      (d && d.communications && typeof d.communications === 'object')
        ? deep(d.communications)
        : ((d && d.communication_status && typeof d.communication_status === 'object')
            ? deep(d.communication_status)
            : ((b && b.communications && typeof b.communications === 'object')
                ? deep(b.communications)
                : null));

    const remittanceSummary =
      (summary && summary.remittance && typeof summary.remittance === 'object')
        ? summary.remittance
        : ((d && d.remittance_summary && typeof d.remittance_summary === 'object')
            ? d.remittance_summary
            : ((b && b.remittance_summary && typeof b.remittance_summary === 'object') ? b.remittance_summary : null));

    const payoutNoticeSummary =
      (summary && summary.payout_notice && typeof summary.payout_notice === 'object')
        ? summary.payout_notice
        : ((d && d.payout_notice_summary && typeof d.payout_notice_summary === 'object')
            ? d.payout_notice_summary
            : ((b && b.payout_notice_summary && typeof b.payout_notice_summary === 'object') ? b.payout_notice_summary : null));

    const remittanceResult = (d && d.remittance_result && typeof d.remittance_result === 'object')
      ? deep(d.remittance_result)
      : null;

    const payoutNoticeResult = (d && d.payout_notice_result && typeof d.payout_notice_result === 'object')
      ? deep(d.payout_notice_result)
      : null;

    const remittanceSection = child.sections && child.sections.remittances ? child.sections.remittances : null;
    const communicationsSection = child.sections && child.sections.communications ? child.sections.communications : null;

    const mode = String(
      d?.communication_mode ||
      summary?.mode ||
      summary?.communication_mode ||
      ''
    ).trim().toUpperCase();

    const hasRemittances =
      (remittanceSummary && remittanceSummary.has_remittances === true) ||
      (remittanceSummary && remittanceSummary.has_targets === true) ||
      (Number(remittanceSummary?.target_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(remittanceSummary?.queued_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(remittanceSummary?.sent_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(d?.remittance_count || b?.remittance_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (remittanceSection && (Number(remittanceSection.known_total_count || 0) > 0 || (Array.isArray(remittanceSection.rows) && remittanceSection.rows.length > 0))) ||
      (remittanceResult && (
        Number(remittanceResult.queued_count || 0) > 0 ||
        Number(remittanceResult.sent_count || 0) > 0 ||
        Number(remittanceResult.skipped_count || 0) > 0
      )) ||
      false;

    const hasPayoutNotices =
      (payoutNoticeSummary && payoutNoticeSummary.has_payout_notices === true) ||
      (payoutNoticeSummary && payoutNoticeSummary.has_targets === true) ||
      (Number(payoutNoticeSummary?.target_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(payoutNoticeSummary?.queued_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(payoutNoticeSummary?.sent_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (Number(d?.payout_notice_count || b?.payout_notice_count || 0) > 0) ||
      (communicationsSection && (Number(communicationsSection.known_total_count || 0) > 0 || (Array.isArray(communicationsSection.rows) && communicationsSection.rows.length > 0))) ||
      (payoutNoticeResult && (
        Number(payoutNoticeResult.queued_count || 0) > 0 ||
        Number(payoutNoticeResult.sent_count || 0) > 0 ||
        Number(payoutNoticeResult.skipped_count || 0) > 0
      )) ||
      false;

    return {
      summary: summary || {
        remittance: remittanceSummary || null,
        payout_notice: payoutNoticeSummary || null
      },
      result: {
        mode,
        remittance: remittanceResult,
        payout_notice: payoutNoticeResult
      },
      mode,
      has_remittances: !!hasRemittances,
      has_payout_notices: !!hasPayoutNotices,
      has_both: !!(hasRemittances && hasPayoutNotices)
    };
  };

  const syncChildCommunicationsState = (data) => {
    child.communications = deriveChildCommunicationsState(data);
  };

  const deriveBatchKindInfo = (data) => {
    if (typeof bankingNormalizeBatchKind === 'function') return bankingNormalizeBatchKind(data);
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : null;
    const b = deriveBatchObj(d);
    const raw = String(d?.batch_kind || b?.batch_kind || b?.batch_kind_fixed || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return {
      rawKind: raw,
      normalizedKind: raw,
      isKnown: !!raw,
      isLoans: raw === 'LOANS',
      isPaye: raw === 'PAYE',
      isUmbrella: raw === 'UMBRELLA',
      isMixed: raw === 'MIXED',
      isPayeish: raw === 'PAYE' || raw === 'MIXED',
      isUmbrellaish: raw === 'UMBRELLA' || raw === 'MIXED',
      label: raw || 'UNKNOWN'
    };
  };

  const deriveScopeForBatch = (data) => {
    const info = deriveBatchKindInfo(data);
    if (info.isPaye && !info.isMixed) return 'PAYE';
    if (info.isUmbrella && !info.isMixed) return 'UMBRELLA';
    return 'ALL';
  };

  const getCurrentWorkbenchSessionId = () => String(
    st?.pay?.draftWizard?.workbench?.session_id ||
    st?.pay?.draftWizard?.decisions?.session_id ||
    ''
  ).trim();

  const hasPendingTransfers = (data) => {
    const transfers = deriveTransfers(data);
    for (const t of transfers) {
      const stx = String(t?.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (stx === 'PENDING') return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const hasPollablePendingTransfers = (data) => {
    const transfers = deriveTransfers(data);
    for (const t of transfers) {
      const stx = String(t?.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (stx !== 'PENDING') continue;
      if (typeof bankingPayHasPollableRailEvidence === 'function' && bankingPayHasPollableRailEvidence(t)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const shouldAutoPoll = (data) => {
    const b = deriveBatchObj(data);
    if (!b) return false;
    if (!hasPollablePendingTransfers(data)) return false;

    const last = String(b.last_status_checked_at_utc || '').trim();
    if (!last) return true;

    const t = new Date(last).getTime();
    if (!Number.isFinite(t)) return true;

    const ageMs = Date.now() - t;
    return ageMs > 2 * 60 * 1000;
  };

  const isExecuteAllowedByStatus = (data) => {
    try {
      const b = deriveBatchObj(data) || {};
      const stx = String(b.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const commitState = String(b.execution_commit_state || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (commitState === 'COMMITTED' || commitState === 'SETTLED' || commitState === 'CANCELLED') return false;
      if (commitState === 'SUBMITTED_NOT_COMMITTED') return false;
      if (stx === 'COMMITTED' || stx === 'CANCELLED' || stx === 'SETTLED' || stx === 'SUBMITTED_NOT_COMMITTED') return false;
      if (!hasActivePaymentsForBatch(data)) return false;
      if (typeof hasProviderOrBankExecutionEvidence === 'function' && hasProviderOrBankExecutionEvidence(data)) return false;
      return (
        stx === 'DRAFT' ||
        stx === 'DRAFT_CREATED' ||
        stx === 'READY' ||
        stx === 'WAITING_BANK_CONFIRM' ||
        stx === 'PARTIAL'
      );
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const getBatchStatusUpper = (data) => {
    try {
      const b = deriveBatchObj(data) || {};
      return String(b.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    } catch {
      return '';
    }
  };

  const isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus = (data) => {
    const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(data);
    return (stx === 'DRAFT' || stx === 'DRAFT_CREATED');
  };

  const isCsvExportAllowedByStatus = (data) => {
    if (!hasActivePaymentsForBatch(data)) return false;
    try {
      if (typeof bankingPayIsCsvExportAllowedByStatus === 'function' && bankingPayIsCsvExportAllowedByStatus(data) === true) return true;
    } catch {}
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data)) ? data : {};
    const b = deriveBatchObj(d) || {};
    const explicitBankCsvAllowed =
      childBatchStatusReadBool(d.can_create_bank_csv_file) ||
      childBatchStatusReadBool(d.canCreateBankCsvFile) ||
      childBatchStatusReadBool(b.can_create_bank_csv_file) ||
      childBatchStatusReadBool(b.canCreateBankCsvFile);
    if (explicitBankCsvAllowed) return true;
    const stx = String(b.status || d.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const commitState = String(b.execution_commit_state || d.execution_commit_state || 'NOT_SUBMITTED').trim().toUpperCase() || 'NOT_SUBMITTED';
    if (commitState !== 'NOT_SUBMITTED') return false;
    if (!['DRAFT', 'DRAFT_CREATED', 'READY'].includes(stx)) return false;
    if (hasProviderOrBankExecutionEvidence(d) === true) return false;
    return true;
  };

  const isPayeNetCompleteForBatch = (data) => {
    const info = deriveBatchKindInfo(data);
    if (!info.isPayeish || info.isLoans) return true;

    const candidates = deriveCandidates(data);
    const payeCands = candidates.filter(c => {
      const ps = (c && typeof c === 'object') ? c.paye_state : null;
      return ps != null && String(ps).trim() !== '';
    });

    if (!payeCands.length) return false;

    for (const c of payeCands) {
      const stx = String(c?.paye_state || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (stx !== 'READY') return false;

      const v = c?.paye_net_amount;
      if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === '') return false;

      const n = Number(v);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return false;
      if (n < 0) return false;
    }

    return true;
  };

  const toast = (msg) => {
    try { if (typeof window.__toast === 'function') window.__toast(String(msg || '').trim()); } catch {}
  };

 const looksTechnicalChildMessage = (value) => {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    if (!text) return false;
    const upper = text.toUpperCase();
    if (text.includes('{') || text.includes('}')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('SQLSTATE') || upper.includes('SQL STATE')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('RPC ') || upper.includes(' RPC') || upper.includes('RPC_')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('PLPGSQL') || upper.includes('POSTGRES') || upper.includes('SUPABASE') || upper.includes('POSTGREST')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('DUPLICATE KEY') || upper.includes('UNIQUE CONSTRAINT') || upper.includes('FOREIGN KEY')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('CONSTRAINT') || upper.includes('STACK TRACE') || upper.includes('TRACEBACK')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('PUBLIC.') || upper.includes('PAY_BATCHES') || upper.includes('PAY_BANK_TRANSFERS') || upper.includes('BANKING_ALERT_ACKNOWLEDGEMENTS')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('MANUAL_DEBT_RECOVERY')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('KEY_TYPE')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('KEY_VALUE')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('TIMESHEET_ID')) return true;
    if (/\bDIFF\b/.test(upper) || /\bDIFFS\b/.test(upper) || /\bDIFF_JSON\b/.test(upper)) return true;
    if (/\bEXPECTED\b/.test(upper)) return true;
    if (/\bACTUAL\b/.test(upper)) return true;
    if (/[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}/i.test(text)) return true;
    if (upper.includes('UQ_') || upper.includes('IDX_') || upper.includes('_PKEY') || upper.includes('PG_')) return true;
    if (upper.includes('VIOLATES') || upper.includes('RELATION "') || upper.includes('COLUMN "') || upper.includes('FUNCTION "')) return true;
    if (/\b(RELATION|COLUMN|FUNCTION|TABLE|SCHEMA|TYPE|OPERATOR|TRIGGER|POLICY|INDEX)\s+[\"']?[A-Z0-9_.-]+/.test(upper)) return true;
    if (/\b[A-Z0-9_]+\([^)]*\)/.test(upper)) return true;
    if (/\b(P[0-9A-Z]{4}|[0-9]{5})\b/.test(upper)) return true;
    return false;
  };


  const pickSafeChildMessage = (values, fallback) => {
    const list = Array.isArray(values) ? values : [values];
    for (const value of list) {
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
      if (text && !looksTechnicalChildMessage(text)) return text;
    }
    return String(fallback || 'CloudTMS could not complete this Banking action. Please refresh and try again.').trim();
  };

 const buildRetryUnsentPaymentsNoticeCopy = (payload, error = null, fallbackMessage = '') => {
    const p = (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) ? payload : {};
    const retryAlreadyInProgress = p.retry_already_in_progress === true || p.retryAlreadyInProgress === true;
    const retryInProgress = retryAlreadyInProgress || p.retry_in_progress === true || p.retryInProgress === true;
