    const retryEligibleCount = Number.isFinite(Number(p.retry_eligible_count ?? p.retryEligibleCount)) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(Number(p.retry_eligible_count ?? p.retryEligibleCount))) : 0;
    const retryOperationStatus = String(p.retry_operation_status || p.retryOperationStatus || p.status || '').trim();
    const title = retryAlreadyInProgress ? 'Retry already in progress' : 'Retry unsent payments';
    const message = retryAlreadyInProgress
      ? 'Retry already in progress.'
      : (retryInProgress
          ? 'Retrying unsent payments — you can continue using CloudTMS while this runs.'
          : (fallbackMessage || 'Retry unsent payments request has been sent. You can continue using CloudTMS while this runs.'));
    const detailRows = [];
    if (retryEligibleCount > 0) detailRows.push(['Unsent payments', String(retryEligibleCount)]);
    if (retryOperationStatus) detailRows.push(['Status', retryOperationStatus]);
    return { title, message, detailRows, confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary' };
  };


  const showRetryUnsentPaymentsNotice = async ({ title, message, detailRows = [], confirmClass = 'btn btn-primary' } = {}) => {
    // Retired blocking dialog. Retry unsent payments is now
    // backend queued and non-blocking, so only show a transient notice/toast.
    const msg = String(message || title || 'Retrying unsent payments — you can continue using CloudTMS while this runs.').trim();
    try {
      if (typeof bankingShowTransientNotice === 'function') {
        bankingShowTransientNotice(msg, { kind: 'info' });
        return;
      }
    } catch {}
    try { toast(msg); } catch {}
  };

  const readProviderSubmitBool = (value) => {
    if (value === true) return true;
    if (value === false) return false;
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (['true', 't', '1', 'yes', 'y', 'on'].includes(text)) return true;
    if (['false', 'f', '0', 'no', 'n', 'off'].includes(text)) return false;
    return false;
  };

  const providerSubmitObject = (value) => (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) ? value : {};

  const providerSubmitText = (value) => String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();

  const providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources = (diagnostic) => {
    const root = providerSubmitObject(diagnostic);
    const railMeta = providerSubmitObject(root.rail_meta_json || root.railMetaJson);
    const rawPayload = providerSubmitObject(root.raw_payload || root.rawPayload);
    const directDiagnostic = providerSubmitObject(root.provider_submit_diagnostic || root.providerSubmitDiagnostic);
    const railDiagnostic = providerSubmitObject(railMeta.provider_submit_diagnostic || railMeta.providerSubmitDiagnostic);
    const rawDiagnostic = providerSubmitObject(rawPayload.provider_submit_diagnostic || rawPayload.providerSubmitDiagnostic);
    const providerResponse = providerSubmitObject(root.provider_response || root.providerResponse || root.provider_response_redacted || root.providerResponseRedacted || root.provider_response_json || root.providerResponseJson);
    const rawProviderResponse = providerSubmitObject(rawPayload.provider_response || rawPayload.providerResponse || rawPayload.provider_response_redacted || rawPayload.providerResponseRedacted || rawPayload.provider_response_json || rawPayload.providerResponseJson);
    return [root, railMeta, rawPayload, directDiagnostic, railDiagnostic, rawDiagnostic, providerResponse, rawProviderResponse]
      .filter((obj) => obj && Object.keys(obj).length);
  };

  const providerSubmitGetFirstText = (source, keys = []) => {
    const obj = providerSubmitObject(source);
    for (const key of keys) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
      const text = providerSubmitText(obj[key]);
      if (text) return text;
    }
    return '';
  };

  const providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic = (diagnostic) => {
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      const status = providerSubmitGetFirstText(obj, [
        'provider_submission_status',
        'providerSubmissionStatus',
        'outcome_code',
        'outcomeCode'
      ]).toUpperCase();
      if (status) return status;
    }
    return '';
  };

  const providerSubmitReviewReasonFromDiagnostic = (diagnostic) => {
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      const reason = providerSubmitGetFirstText(obj, [
        'review_reason_code',
        'reviewReasonCode',
        'provider_submit_review_reason_code',
        'providerSubmitReviewReasonCode',
        'claim_blocker_code',
        'claimBlockerCode'
      ]).toUpperCase();
      if (reason) return reason;
    }
    return '';
  };

  const providerSubmitLocalReferenceSet = (diagnostic) => {
    const out = new Set();
    const localKeys = [
      'id', 'operation_id', 'operationId', 'chunk_id', 'chunkId', 'transfer_id', 'transferId',
      'pay_bank_transfer_id', 'payBankTransferId', 'transfer_scope_id', 'transferScopeId',
      'scope_id', 'scopeId', 'auth_request_id', 'authRequestId', 'pay_batch_id', 'payBatchId',
      'batch_id', 'batchId', 'request_id', 'requestId', 'idempotency_key', 'idempotencyKey',
      'local_provider_request_id', 'localProviderRequestId', 'provider_request_id', 'providerRequestId',
      'payment_reference', 'paymentReference', 'bulk_reference', 'bulkReference', 'correlation_id', 'correlationId'
    ];
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      for (const key of localKeys) {
        const text = providerSubmitText(obj[key]);
        if (text) out.add(text.toLowerCase());
      }
    }
    return out;
  };

  const providerSubmitExternalIdentifierPresent = (diagnostic) => {
    const localRefs = providerSubmitLocalReferenceSet(diagnostic);
    const externalKeys = [
      'rail_tx_id', 'railTxId', 'provider_transaction_id', 'providerTransactionId',
      'provider_payment_id', 'providerPaymentId', 'provider_event_id', 'providerEventId',
      'provider_reference', 'providerReference', 'provider_submission_id', 'providerSubmissionId',
      'submission_id', 'submissionId', 'rail_submission_id', 'railSubmissionId',
      'external_payment_id', 'externalPaymentId', 'revolut_payment_id', 'revolutPaymentId',
      'provider_transfer_id', 'providerTransferId', 'external_transfer_id', 'externalTransferId',
      'external_reference', 'externalReference', 'bank_reference', 'bankReference',
      'transaction_id', 'transactionId', 'payment_id', 'paymentId'
    ];
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      for (const key of externalKeys) {
        const text = providerSubmitText(obj[key]);
        if (!text) continue;
        if (localRefs.has(text.toLowerCase())) continue;
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  const providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent = (diagnostic) => {
    const localRefs = providerSubmitLocalReferenceSet(diagnostic);
    const transactionKeys = [
      'rail_tx_id', 'railTxId',
      'provider_transaction_id', 'providerTransactionId',
      'provider_payment_id', 'providerPaymentId',
      'external_payment_id', 'externalPaymentId',
      'revolut_payment_id', 'revolutPaymentId',
      'provider_transfer_id', 'providerTransferId',
      'external_transfer_id', 'externalTransferId',
      'bank_reference', 'bankReference',
      'transaction_id', 'transactionId',
      'payment_id', 'paymentId'
    ];
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      for (const key of transactionKeys) {
        const text = providerSubmitText(obj[key]);
        if (!text) continue;
        if (localRefs.has(text.toLowerCase())) continue;
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  const providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence = (diagnostic) => {
    const status = providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(diagnostic);
    const rejectedOrNoProofStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED_PRE_CALL',
      'MANUAL_RESOLVED_NO_PAYMENT_MADE'
    ]);
    for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(diagnostic)) {
      if (readProviderSubmitBool(obj.provider_acceptance_evidence_present ?? obj.providerAcceptanceEvidencePresent) === true) return true;
    }
    if (status === 'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ACCEPTED') return providerSubmitExternalIdentifierPresent(diagnostic);
    if (rejectedOrNoProofStatuses.has(status)) return providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent(diagnostic);
    return providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent(diagnostic);
  };

  const providerSubmitManualResolutionRequiredFrom = (...sources) => {
    const manualStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID'
    ]);
    const manualReasons = new Set([
      'STALE_RUNNING_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_CHUNK',
      'PROVIDER_REQUEST_SENT_NO_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_MALFORMED',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_PRESENT_NO_EXTERNAL_ID'
    ]);
    for (const source of sources) {
      for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(source)) {
        if (readProviderSubmitBool(obj.manual_resolution_required ?? obj.manualResolutionRequired) === true) return true;
      }
      if (manualStatuses.has(providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(source))) return true;
      if (manualReasons.has(providerSubmitReviewReasonFromDiagnostic(source))) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const providerSubmitReviewIssuePresent = (...sources) => {
    const providerIssueStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED_PRE_CALL'
    ]);
    const providerIssueKinds = new Set([
      'PAYMENT_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW',
      'BANK_TRANSFER_PROVIDER_REVIEW_REQUIRED'
    ]);
    for (const source of sources) {
      const sourceStatus = providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(source);
      const acceptedWithExternalEvidence = sourceStatus === 'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ACCEPTED' && providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(source) === true;
      if (acceptedWithExternalEvidence) continue;
      for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(source)) {
        const objStatus = providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(obj);
        const objAcceptedWithExternalEvidence = objStatus === 'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ACCEPTED' && providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(obj) === true;
        if (objAcceptedWithExternalEvidence) continue;
        const issueKind = providerSubmitGetFirstText(obj, [
          'issueKindForDisplay', 'issue_kind_for_display', 'issue_kind', 'issueKind',
          'kind', 'type', 'alert_kind', 'alertKind', 'payment_issue_kind', 'paymentIssueKind'
        ]).toUpperCase();
        if (providerIssueKinds.has(issueKind)) return true;
        if (readProviderSubmitBool(obj.provider_submit_review_issue ?? obj.providerSubmitReviewIssue) === true) return true;
      }
      if (providerSubmitManualResolutionRequiredFrom(source)) return true;
      if (providerIssueStatuses.has(sourceStatus)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const hasProviderOrBankExecutionEvidence = (data) => {
    const d = (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data)) ? data : {};
    const b = deriveBatchObj(d) || {};
    if (String(b.execution_commit_state || d.execution_commit_state || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== '' && String(b.execution_commit_state || d.execution_commit_state || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== 'NOT_SUBMITTED') return true;
    if (String(b.execution_commit_ref || d.execution_commit_ref || '').trim()) return true;
    if (String(b.execution_committed_at_utc || d.execution_committed_at_utc || '').trim()) return true;
    if (b.settlement_confirmation_json || d.settlement_confirmation_json) return true;
    if (providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(d) === true) return true;
    if (providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent(d) === true) return true;
    const submittedStates = new Set(['SUBMITTED', 'SENT', 'ACCEPTED', 'PROCESSING', 'IN_FLIGHT', 'PENDING_SETTLEMENT', 'COMPLETED', 'SETTLED', 'PAID', 'COMMITTED', 'EXECUTED']);
    const eventHasExecutionEvidence = (eventValue) => {
      const ev = (eventValue && typeof eventValue === 'object' && !Array.isArray(eventValue)) ? eventValue : {};
      if (!ev || !Object.keys(ev).length) return false;
      if (providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(ev) === true) return true;
      if (providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent(ev) === true) return true;
      const eventStates = [
        ev.status,
        ev.state,
        ev.rail_state,
        ev.railState,
        ev.provider_state,
        ev.providerState,
        ev.payment_lifecycle_state,
        ev.paymentLifecycleState,
        ev.normalised_state,
        ev.normalized_state
      ].map((value) => String(value || '').trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
      if (eventStates.some((state) => submittedStates.has(state))) return true;
      const eventKind = String(ev.event_type || ev.eventType || ev.kind || ev.type || ev.action || ev.action_type || ev.actionType || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      return /(?:PROVIDER|BANK|RAIL)/.test(eventKind) && /(?:SUBMIT|SUBMITTED|SENT|ACCEPT|ACCEPTED|PAID|SETTLED|SETTLEMENT|COMMIT|COMMITTED|EXECUT)/.test(eventKind);
    };
    for (const transfer of deriveTransfers(d)) {
      if (providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(transfer) === true) return true;
      if (providerSubmitTransactionIdentifierPresent(transfer) === true) return true;
      const states = [transfer?.status, transfer?.rail_state, transfer?.railState, transfer?.normalised_state, transfer?.normalized_state].map((value) => String(value || '').trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
      if (states.some((state) => submittedStates.has(state))) return true;
      if (String(transfer?.completed_at_utc || transfer?.completedAtUtc || transfer?.settled_at_utc || transfer?.settledAtUtc || '').trim()) return true;
    }
    const eventSources = [d.bank_evidence, d.bankEvidence, d.payment_events, d.paymentEvents, d.transfer_events, d.transferEvents, d.provider_events, d.providerEvents, d.provider_transfer_events, d.providerTransferEvents];
    if (eventSources.some((value) => Array.isArray(value) && value.some((eventValue) => eventHasExecutionEvidence(eventValue)))) return true;
    const diagStatus = providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(d);
    const safeRetry = (() => {
      for (const obj of providerSubmitCollectDiagnosticSources(d)) {
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'safe_retry_available') || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'safeRetryAvailable')) {
          return readProviderSubmitBool(obj.safe_retry_available ?? obj.safeRetryAvailable);
        }
      }
      return null;
    })();
    if (safeRetry === false && diagStatus && diagStatus.startsWith('PROVIDER_SUBMISSION')) return true;
    return false;
  };

  const showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice = async ({ title, message, confirmClass = 'btn btn-primary' } = {}) => {
    const titleOut = String(title || 'Current Payment Status').trim() || 'Current Payment Status';
    const msg = String(message || '').trim() || 'Current Payment Status updated.';
    try {
      if (typeof openUiConfirmModal === 'function') {
        await openUiConfirmModal({
          title: titleOut,
          message_html: `<div class="mini" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">${enc(msg)}</div>`,
          confirm_label: 'OK',
          hide_cancel: true,
          confirm_class: String(confirmClass || 'btn btn-primary').trim() || 'btn btn-primary',
          kind: 'import-summary-banking-provider-submit-review-result'
        });
        return;
      }
    } catch {}
    try { toast(msg || titleOut); } catch {}
  };

  const fmtMoney = (v) => {
    const n = Number(v);
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '';
    return (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2);
  };

  const normalizePayeNet2dp = (raw) => {
    if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
    const s = String(raw).trim();
    if (!s) return null;
    const n = Number(s);
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return { error: 'NOT_NUMERIC' };
    if (n < 0) return { error: 'NEGATIVE' };
    return (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2);
  };

  const getChildPayeCandidates = (dataObj) => {
    const dataNow = (dataObj && typeof dataObj === 'object') ? dataObj : null;
    const candidatesAll = Array.isArray(dataNow?.candidates) ? dataNow.candidates.slice() : [];
    const payeCandidates = candidatesAll.filter((candidate) => {
      const payeState = String(candidate?.paye_state || '').trim();
      if (payeState) return true;
      const summary = (candidate && typeof candidate === 'object' && candidate.deductionsSummary && typeof candidate.deductionsSummary === 'object')
        ? candidate.deductionsSummary
        : ((typeof bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary === 'function')
            ? bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary(candidate)
            : null);
      return !!summary?.awaitingNet || summary?.payeNetAmount != null;
    });

    payeCandidates.sort((a, b) => {
      const an = String(a?.candidate_display_name || a?.display_name || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const bn = String(b?.candidate_display_name || b?.display_name || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (an < bn) return -1;
      if (an > bn) return 1;

      const at = String(a?.candidate_tms_ref || a?.tms_ref || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const bt = String(b?.candidate_tms_ref || b?.tms_ref || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (at < bt) return -1;
      if (at > bt) return 1;

      const ai = String(a?.candidate_id || '').trim();
      const bi = String(b?.candidate_id || '').trim();
      if (ai < bi) return -1;
      if (ai > bi) return 1;
      return 0;
    });

    return payeCandidates;
  };

  const hasDirtyChildPayeDrafts = () => {
    const draft = (child?.paye?.netDraft && typeof child.paye.netDraft === 'object') ? child.paye.netDraft : {};
    const candidates = getChildPayeCandidates(child.data);

    for (const c of candidates) {
      const cid = String(c?.candidate_id || '').trim();
      if (!cid) continue;
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(draft, cid)) continue;

      const next = normalizePayeNet2dp(draft[cid]);
      const prev = normalizePayeNet2dp(c?.paye_net_amount);

      if (next && typeof next === 'object' && next.error) return true;

      const prevStr = (prev && typeof prev === 'object' && prev.error) ? '' : ((prev == null) ? '' : String(prev));
      const nextStr = (next && typeof next === 'object' && next.error) ? String(draft[cid] || '').trim() : ((next == null) ? '' : String(next));

      if (prevStr !== nextStr) return true;
    }

    return false;
  };

  const syncChildFooterState = () => {
    try {
      const fr = (typeof window.__getModalFrame === 'function') ? window.__getModalFrame() : null;
      if (!fr) return;
      if (String(fr.kind || '') !== KIND) return;

      const activeTab = String(child.ui?.activeTabKey || fr.currentTabKey || 'overview').trim() || 'overview';
      const dirty = hasDirtyChildPayeDrafts();
      const canSaveWorksheet = (activeTab === 'paye_worksheet') && isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data);

      fr.isDirty = !!dirty;
      fr._primaryLabel = 'Save';
      fr._showSave = !!(canSaveWorksheet || dirty);

      if (typeof fr._updateButtons === 'function') fr._updateButtons();
    } catch {}
  };

  const buildChildPayePatchEntries = () => {
    const candidates = getChildPayeCandidates(child.data);
    const draft = (child?.paye?.netDraft && typeof child.paye.netDraft === 'object') ? child.paye.netDraft : {};
    const entries = [];

    for (const c of candidates) {
      const candId = String(c?.candidate_id || '').trim();
      if (!candId) continue;

      const payeStateRaw = (c && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(c, 'paye_state')) ? c.paye_state : null;
      if (payeStateRaw === null || payeStateRaw === undefined || String(payeStateRaw).trim() === '') continue;

      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(draft, candId)) continue;

      const next = normalizePayeNet2dp(draft[candId]);
      if (next && typeof next === 'object' && next.error) {
        const name = String(c?.candidate_display_name || c?.display_name || candId).trim();
        throw new Error(`Invalid net amount for ${name} (${next.error}). Net must be 0 or greater.`);
      }

      const prev = normalizePayeNet2dp(c?.paye_net_amount);
      const prevStr = (prev && typeof prev === 'object' && prev.error) ? '' : ((prev == null) ? '' : String(prev));
      const nextStr = (next == null) ? '' : String(next);

      if (prevStr === nextStr) continue;

      const tms = String(c?.candidate_tms_ref || c?.tms_ref || '').trim() || null;

      entries.push({
        pay_batch_candidate_id: (c?.pay_batch_candidate_id != null) ? String(c.pay_batch_candidate_id).trim() : null,
        candidate_id: candId,
        tms_ref: tms,
        net_amount: (next == null) ? null : nextStr
      });
    }

    return entries;
  };

  const exportChildPayeWorksheetCsv = async () => {
    if (child.actionsBusy.exportingWorksheet) return;
    child.actionsBusy.exportingWorksheet = true;
    child.paye.saveError = '';
    await rerenderChild();

    try {
      const candidates = getChildPayeCandidates(child.data);
      const draft = (child?.paye?.netDraft && typeof child.paye.netDraft === 'object') ? child.paye.netDraft : {};

      const escCsv = (value) => {
        const s = String(value == null ? '' : value);
        return /[",\r\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };

      const header = [
        'Candidate',
        'Works No.',
        'Gross',
        'PAYE Net',
        'Overpayment',
        'Repayment',
        'Final Bank Amount'
      ];

      const rows = candidates.map((c) => {
        const deductions = (typeof bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary === 'function')
          ? bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary(c)
          : {
              grossPositive: Number(c?.gross_preview || 0),
              manualDebtRecovery: Number(c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0),
              manualDebtNetDeductions: Number(c?.manual_debt_net_deductions || c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0),
              paymentAdvanceRepayment: Number(c?.payment_advance_repayment || c?.loan_repayment_taken || 0),
              loanRepayment: Number(c?.loan_repayment_taken || c?.payment_advance_repayment || 0),
              repayment: ((c?.repayment !== null && c?.repayment !== undefined && String(c.repayment).trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(c.repayment)))
                ? Number(c.repayment)
                : (Number(c?.payment_advance_repayment || c?.loan_repayment_taken || 0) + Number(c?.manual_debt_net_deductions || c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0))),
              overpaymentRecovery: Number(c?.overpayment_recovery_taken || 0),
              finalPayable: (c?.net_bank_amount != null) ? Number(c.net_bank_amount) : null,
              awaitingNet: !!c?.awaiting_net_amount,
              payeNetAmount: (c?.paye_net_amount != null) ? Number(c.paye_net_amount) : null,
              pendingAdvisory: !!c?.awaiting_net_amount ? 'Final deductions will be confirmed after PAYE net is entered.' : '',
              hasKnownFinalPayable: c?.net_bank_amount != null,
              raw: c
            };

        const cid = String(c?.candidate_id || '').trim();
        const draftRaw = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(draft, cid) ? String(draft[cid] || '').trim() : '';
        const draftNorm = normalizePayeNet2dp(draftRaw);
        const draftNet = (draftNorm && typeof draftNorm === 'object' && draftNorm.error) ? '' : ((draftNorm == null) ? '' : String(draftNorm));
        const existingNet = (c?.paye_net_amount != null) ? fmtMoney(c.paye_net_amount) : '';

        return [
          String(c?.candidate_display_name || c?.display_name || '').trim(),
          String(c?.candidate_tms_ref || c?.tms_ref || '').trim(),
          fmtMoney(deductions.grossPositive),
          draftNet || existingNet,
          fmtMoney(deductions.overpaymentRecovery),
          fmtMoney(deductions.repayment),
          deductions.finalPayable != null ? fmtMoney(deductions.finalPayable) : ''
        ];
      });

      const csv = [
        header.map(escCsv).join(','),
        ...rows.map((row) => row.map(escCsv).join(','))
      ].join('\r\n');

      const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `pay_batch_${id}_paye_worksheet.csv`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      setTimeout(() => { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); } catch {} }, 2000);

      toast('PAYE worksheet exported');
    } finally {
      child.actionsBusy.exportingWorksheet = false;
      await rerenderChild();
    }
  };

  const printChildPayeWorksheet = async () => {
    if (child.actionsBusy.printingWorksheet) return;
    child.actionsBusy.printingWorksheet = true;
    await rerenderChild();

    try {
      const candidates = getChildPayeCandidates(child.data);
      const title = `PAYE Worksheet — ${id.slice(0, 8)}`;

      const rows = candidates.map((c) => {
        const deductions = (typeof bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary === 'function')
          ? bankingNormalizeCandidateDeductionsSummary(c)
          : {
              grossPositive: Number(c?.gross_preview || 0),
              manualDebtRecovery: Number(c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0),
              manualDebtNetDeductions: Number(c?.manual_debt_net_deductions || c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0),
              paymentAdvanceRepayment: Number(c?.payment_advance_repayment || c?.loan_repayment_taken || 0),
              loanRepayment: Number(c?.loan_repayment_taken || c?.payment_advance_repayment || 0),
              repayment: ((c?.repayment !== null && c?.repayment !== undefined && String(c.repayment).trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(c.repayment)))
                ? Number(c.repayment)
                : (Number(c?.payment_advance_repayment || c?.loan_repayment_taken || 0) + Number(c?.manual_debt_net_deductions || c?.manual_debt_recovery_taken || c?.manual_debt_recovery || 0))),
              overpaymentRecovery: Number(c?.overpayment_recovery_taken || 0),
              finalPayable: (c?.net_bank_amount != null) ? Number(c.net_bank_amount) : null,
              awaitingNet: !!c?.awaiting_net_amount,
              payeNetAmount: (c?.paye_net_amount != null) ? Number(c.paye_net_amount) : null,
              pendingAdvisory: !!c?.awaiting_net_amount ? 'Final deductions will be confirmed after PAYE net is entered.' : '',
              hasKnownFinalPayable: c?.net_bank_amount != null,
              raw: c
            };

        const name = String(c?.candidate_display_name || c?.display_name || '').trim() || '—';
        const tms = String(c?.candidate_tms_ref || c?.tms_ref || '').trim();
        const gross = `£${fmtMoney(deductions.grossPositive)}`;
        const net = deductions.payeNetAmount != null ? `£${fmtMoney(deductions.payeNetAmount)}` : '';
        const overpayment = `£${fmtMoney(deductions.overpaymentRecovery)}`;
        const repayment = `£${fmtMoney(deductions.repayment)}`;
        const finalBank = deductions.finalPayable != null ? `£${fmtMoney(deductions.finalPayable)}` : 'Pending';

        return `
          <tr>
            <td>${enc(name)}</td>
            <td class="mono">${enc(tms || '')}</td>
            <td class="mono" style="text-align:right;">${enc(gross)}</td>
            <td class="mono" style="text-align:right;">${enc(net || '—')}</td>
            <td class="mono" style="text-align:right;">${enc(overpayment)}</td>
            <td class="mono" style="text-align:right;">${enc(repayment)}</td>
            <td class="mono" style="text-align:right;">${enc(finalBank)}</td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');

      const w = window.open('', '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer,width=1180,height=760');
      if (!w) throw new Error('Popup blocked');

      w.document.open();
      w.document.write(`
        <html>
          <head>
            <title>${enc(title)}</title>
            <style>
              body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; padding: 18px; }
              h1 { font-size: 16px; margin: 0 0 10px 0; }
              .meta { font-size: 12px; color: #555; margin-bottom: 12px; }
              table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
              th, td { padding: 8px; border: 1px solid #ddd; font-size: 12px; }
              th { background: #f6f6f6; text-align: left; }
              .mono { font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", "Courier New", monospace; }
            </style>
          </head>
          <body>
            <h1>${enc(title)}</h1>
            <div class="meta">Printed: ${enc(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone:'Europe/London', year:'numeric', month:'2-digit', day:'2-digit', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit', hour12:false }).format(new Date()).replace(',', ''))}</div>
            <div class="meta">Deductions become final after PAYE net is entered.</div>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Candidate</th>
                  <th>Works No.</th>
                  <th style="text-align:right;">Gross</th>
                  <th style="text-align:right;">PAYE Net</th>
                  <th style="text-align:right;">Overpayment</th>
                  <th style="text-align:right;">Repayment</th>
                  <th style="text-align:right;">Final Bank</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${rows || `<tr><td colspan="7">No PAYE candidates.</td></tr>`}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            <script>
              setTimeout(() => { try { window.print(); } catch(e) {} }, 50);
            </script>
          </body>
        </html>
      `);
      w.document.close();
    } finally {
      child.actionsBusy.printingWorksheet = false;
      await rerenderChild();
    }
  };

  const onSave = async () => {
    if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return false;

    if (!isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
      child.paye.saveError = 'PAYE worksheet can only be saved while the batch is in DRAFT.';
      await rerenderChild();
      toast(child.paye.saveError);
      return false;
    }

    if (child.paye.saveBusy) return false;

    child.paye.saveBusy = true;
    child.paye.saveError = '';
    child.paye.saveFriendlyError = null;
    await rerenderChild();

    try {
      const entries = buildChildPayePatchEntries();

      await postJson(`/api/banking/pay/batch/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/paye-net/manual`, { entries });

      child.paye.netDraft = {};
      await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false });

      toast('PAYE net payments saved');
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      const friendly = normaliseChildFriendlyError(e, 'MANUAL_NET_INVALID', {
        action: 'PAYE_NET_SAVE',
        userInitiated: true,
        silent: true,
        scope: deriveScopeForBatch(child.data)
      });
      child.paye.saveError = String(friendly?.message || 'PAYE net amounts could not be saved.').trim();
      child.paye.saveFriendlyError = friendly;
      await rerenderChild();
      return false;
    } finally {
      child.paye.saveBusy = false;
      await rerenderChild();
    }
  };

  const stopAutoPoll = () => {
    try {
      if (child.__autoPollTimer) {
        clearInterval(child.__autoPollTimer);
        child.__autoPollTimer = null;
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const startAutoPoll = () => {
    // Live watch now drives routine batch status updates. Keep this only as
    // backward-compatible cleanup so older timers are not left running.
    stopAutoPoll();
  };

  const maybeAdjustAutoPollAfterLoad = () => {
    // Do not restart heavy full-batch polling. Current Payment Status updates
    // are handled by the lightweight live watch; explicit Check provider status
    // still uses the one-shot poll path via loadBatch({ forcePoll: true }).
    stopAutoPoll();
  };

  const rerenderChild = async () => {
    let fr = null;
    let suppressDirtySet = false;
    try {
      if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return;
      fr = getTopModalFrameSafe();
      if (!fr) return;
      const tabKey = String(child.ui?.activeTabKey || fr.currentTabKey || 'overview').trim() || 'overview';
      fr._suppressDirty = true;
      suppressDirtySet = true;
      await fr.setTab(tabKey);
    } catch {
    } finally {
      try { if (fr && suppressDirtySet) fr._suppressDirty = false; } catch {}
    }

    try { wire(); } catch {}

    try { if (fr && typeof fr._updateButtons === 'function') fr._updateButtons(); } catch {}
    try { syncChildFooterState(); } catch {}

    try {
      if (typeof attachUkDatePicker === 'function') {
        const root = document.getElementById(rootId);
        const els = root ? root.querySelectorAll('[data-uk-date="1"]') : [];
        for (const el of els) {
          try { attachUkDatePicker(el, {}); } catch {}
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const clearPaymentIssueSupportExpansion = () => {
    try {
      const correctionState = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction)) ? child.correction : null;
      if (!correctionState) return;
      correctionState.expandedSupportRowKey = '';
      correctionState.supportDetailsRowKey = '';
      correctionState.expandedRowKey = '';
      correctionState.detailsRowKey = '';
      correctionState.viewDetailsRowKey = '';
      correctionState.ui = (correctionState.ui && typeof correctionState.ui === 'object' && !Array.isArray(correctionState.ui)) ? correctionState.ui : {};
      correctionState.ui.expandedSupportRowKey = '';
      correctionState.ui.supportDetailsRowKey = '';
    } catch {}
  };

  const paymentIssuesPanelInsideChildRoot = () => {
    try {
      const root = document.getElementById(rootId);
      const panel = document.getElementById('payment-issue-panel');
      return !!(root && panel && root.contains(panel));
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly = async () => {
    try {
      child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
      child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';

      if (typeof rerenderBankingPaymentIssuePanel === 'function') {
        const ok = rerenderBankingPaymentIssuePanel();
        if (ok && paymentIssuesPanelInsideChildRoot()) {
          try { wire(); } catch {}
          syncChildFooterState();
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    await rerenderChild();
  };

  const fetchJsonGet = async (path) => {
    if (typeof authFetch !== 'function' || typeof API !== 'function') throw new Error('authFetch/API missing');
    const res = await authFetch(API(path), { method: 'GET' });
    const txt = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    let parsed = null;
    try { parsed = txt ? JSON.parse(txt) : null; } catch { parsed = null; }
    if (!res.ok) {
      const msg = (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && (parsed.error || parsed.message))
        ? String(parsed.error || parsed.message)
        : (txt || `Request failed (${res.status})`);
      const err = new Error(String(msg || 'Request failed'));
      err.status = res.status;
      err.body = txt;
      err.json = parsed;
      throw err;
    }
    return (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') ? parsed : {};
  };

  const rowFirstTextForSection = (row, keys = []) => {
    const r = (row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row)) ? row : {};
    for (const key of keys) {
      const value = r[key];
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const found = value.map((entry) => String(entry == null ? '' : entry).trim()).find(Boolean);
        if (found) return found;
        continue;
      }
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
      if (text) return text;
    }
    return '';
  };

  const rowStableKeyForSection = (sectionKey, row, indexFallback = 0) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    const r = (row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row)) ? row : {};
    const first = (keys) => rowFirstTextForSection(r, keys);
    const compositeKey = (prefix, parts, fallbackValue = r) => {
      const hasMeaningfulPart = Object.values(parts || {}).some((value) => String(value == null ? '' : value).trim() !== '');
      if (hasMeaningfulPart) return `${key || 'section'}:${prefix}:${stableStringify(parts)}`;
      const fallback = stableStringify(fallbackValue);
      return `${key || 'section'}:${prefix}:${fallback || indexFallback}`;
    };
    if (key === 'overview_items') {
      const itemId = first(['pay_batch_item_id', 'payBatchItemId', 'pay_item_id', 'payItemId']);
      if (itemId) return `${key}:item:${itemId}`;
      const idValue = first(['id', 'item_id', 'itemId']);
      if (idValue) return `${key}:id:${idValue}`;
      const candidateId = first(['pay_batch_candidate_id', 'payBatchCandidateId', 'candidate_id', 'candidateId']);
      const itemType = first(['item_type', 'itemType', 'type', 'kind', 'row_kind', 'rowKind']);
      const description = first(['description', 'detail', 'label', 'title']);
      const sourceRef = first(['source_ref', 'sourceRef', 'reference', 'ref', 'source_reference', 'sourceReference']);
      const amount = first(['amount_inc_vat', 'amountIncVat', 'amount', 'net_amount', 'netAmount', 'frozen_target_amount_inc_vat', 'frozenTargetAmountIncVat']);
      return compositeKey('composite', { candidateId, itemType, description, sourceRef, amount });
    }
    if (key === 'current_payment_status' || key === 'transfers') {
      const transferId = first(['pay_bank_transfer_id', 'payBankTransferId', 'transfer_id', 'transferId']);
      if (transferId) return `${key}:transfer:${transferId}`;
      const idValue = first(['id']);
      if (idValue) return `${key}:id:${idValue}`;
      const railId = first(['rail_tx_id', 'railTxId', 'provider_transaction_id', 'providerTransactionId', 'provider_payment_id', 'providerPaymentId']);
      if (railId) return `${key}:rail:${railId}`;
      const candidateId = first(['pay_batch_candidate_id', 'payBatchCandidateId', 'candidate_id', 'candidateId']);
      const amount = first(['amount', 'amount_inc_vat', 'amountIncVat', 'bank_amount', 'bankAmount']);
      const status = first(['status', 'rail_state', 'railState', 'payment_lifecycle_state', 'paymentLifecycleState']);
      const providerStatus = first(['provider_submission_status', 'providerSubmissionStatus', 'provider_state', 'providerState']);
      return compositeKey('composite', { candidateId, amount, status, providerStatus });
    }
    if (key === 'finance_case_groups') {
      const groupId = first(['group_id', 'groupId']);
      if (groupId) return `${key}:group:${groupId}`;
      const caseId = first(['finance_case_id', 'financeCaseId', 'case_id', 'caseId']);
      if (caseId) return `${key}:case:${caseId}`;
      const componentId = first(['finance_component_id', 'financeComponentId', 'component_id', 'componentId']);
      if (componentId) return `${key}:component:${componentId}`;
      const idValue = first(['id']);
      if (idValue) return `${key}:id:${idValue}`;
      return compositeKey('composite', {
        candidateId: first(['pay_batch_candidate_id', 'payBatchCandidateId', 'candidate_id', 'candidateId']),
        amount: first(['amount', 'amount_inc_vat', 'amountIncVat', 'balance', 'balance_amount', 'balanceAmount']),
        label: first(['label', 'title', 'description', 'name'])
      });
    }
    if (key === 'events' || key === 'auth_history') {
      const idValue = first(['id', 'event_id', 'eventId', 'auth_event_id', 'authEventId', 'history_id', 'historyId']);
      if (idValue) return `${key}:id:${idValue}`;
      const occurredAt = first(['occurred_at_utc', 'occurredAtUtc', 'created_at_utc', 'createdAtUtc', 'created_at', 'createdAt', 'timestamp', 'time']);
      const eventType = first(['event_type', 'eventType', 'type', 'kind', 'action', 'status']);
      return compositeKey('event', { occurredAt, eventType });
    }
    const idValue = first(['id', 'row_id', 'rowId']);
    if (idValue) return `${key || 'section'}:id:${idValue}`;
    return `${key || 'section'}:row:${stableStringify(r) || indexFallback}`;
  };

  const valueLooksEmptyForSectionMerge = (value) => value === null || value === undefined || (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '');

  const mergeDuplicateSectionRowsPreservingFirst = (firstRow, laterRow) => {
    if (!firstRow || typeof firstRow !== 'object' || Array.isArray(firstRow)) return laterRow;
    if (!laterRow || typeof laterRow !== 'object' || Array.isArray(laterRow)) return firstRow;
    const out = { ...firstRow };
    for (const key of Object.keys(laterRow)) {
      const existing = out[key];
      const incoming = laterRow[key];
      if (valueLooksEmptyForSectionMerge(existing) && !valueLooksEmptyForSectionMerge(incoming)) {
        out[key] = incoming;
        continue;
      }
      if (
        existing && incoming &&
        typeof existing === 'object' && typeof incoming === 'object' &&
        !Array.isArray(existing) && !Array.isArray(incoming)
      ) {
        out[key] = mergeDuplicateSectionRowsPreservingFirst(existing, incoming);
      }
    }
    return out;
  };

  const dedupeRowsForSection = (sectionKey, rows) => {
    const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
    const out = [];
    const indexByKey = new Map();
    list.forEach((row, index) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object' || Array.isArray(row)) {
        out.push(row);
        return;
      }
      const stableKey = rowStableKeyForSection(sectionKey, row, index);
      if (!stableKey || !indexByKey.has(stableKey)) {
        if (stableKey) indexByKey.set(stableKey, out.length);
        out.push(row);
        return;
      }
      const existingIndex = indexByKey.get(stableKey);
      out[existingIndex] = mergeDuplicateSectionRowsPreservingFirst(out[existingIndex], row);
    });
    return out;
  };

  const isFirstPagePayload = (pagePayload) => {
    const payload = (pagePayload && typeof pagePayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(pagePayload)) ? pagePayload : {};
    if (payload.reset === true || payload.first_page === true || payload.firstPage === true) return true;
    if (payload.append === false) return true;
    const cursorKeys = ['cursor', 'request_cursor', 'requestCursor', 'page_cursor', 'pageCursor', 'previous_cursor', 'previousCursor'];
    for (const key of cursorKeys) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(payload, key)) continue;
      if (isChildSectionFirstCursor(payload[key])) return true;
    }
    const pageKey = String(payload.request_key || payload.requestKey || payload.page_key || payload.pageKey || '').trim();
    return !!(pageKey && pageKey.includes('|FIRST|'));
  };

  const mergeSectionPageIntoChild = (sectionKey, pagePayload) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (!key) return null;
    ensureChildLazySections(child.data);

    const current = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
    const payload = (pagePayload && typeof pagePayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(pagePayload)) ? pagePayload : {};
    const rawRows = Array.isArray(payload.items)
      ? payload.items
      : Array.isArray(payload.rows)
        ? payload.rows
        : Array.isArray(payload.data)
          ? payload.data
          : [];
    const sourcePurposeValue = String(payload.source_purpose || payload.sourcePurpose || payload.purpose || payload.section_purpose || payload.sectionPurpose || '').trim() || (key === 'overview_items' ? 'overview' : 'detail');
    const currentTabValue = String(payload.current_tab || payload.currentTab || child.ui?.activeTabKey || '').trim();
    const rows = rawRows.map((row) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object' || Array.isArray(row)) return row;
      if (key === 'overview_items') {
        return {
          ...row,
          overview_item: true,
          overviewItem: true,
          is_overview_item: true,
          isOverviewItem: true,
          source_section: row.source_section || row.sourceSection || 'overview_items',
          sourceSection: row.sourceSection || row.source_section || 'overview_items',
          source_purpose: row.source_purpose || row.sourcePurpose || 'overview',
          sourcePurpose: row.sourcePurpose || row.source_purpose || 'overview',
          presentation_source: row.presentation_source || row.presentationSource || 'overview_items',
          presentationSource: row.presentationSource || row.presentation_source || 'overview_items',
          current_tab: row.current_tab || row.currentTab || currentTabValue || 'overview',
          currentTab: row.currentTab || row.current_tab || currentTabValue || 'overview'
        };
      }
      return {
        ...row,
        source_section: row.source_section || row.sourceSection || key,
        sourceSection: row.sourceSection || row.source_section || key,
        source_purpose: row.source_purpose || row.sourcePurpose || sourcePurposeValue,
        sourcePurpose: row.sourcePurpose || row.source_purpose || sourcePurposeValue,
        current_tab: row.current_tab || row.currentTab || currentTabValue,
        currentTab: row.currentTab || row.current_tab || currentTabValue
      };
    });
    const firstPage = isFirstPagePayload(payload);
    const append = firstPage ? false : payload.append === true;
    const nextRows = dedupeRowsForSection(key, append ? current.rows.concat(rows) : rows.slice());
    const knownTotalRaw = Number(payload.known_total_count ?? payload.knownTotalCount ?? payload.total_count ?? payload.totalCount ?? current.known_total_count);
    const requestKey = String(payload.request_key || payload.requestKey || payload.page_key || payload.pageKey || current.pending_request_key || current.in_flight_key || '').trim();
    const loadedAtUtc = new Date().toISOString();
    const retryCountRaw = Number(current.explicit_retry_count ?? current.explicitRetryCount ?? 0);
    const generationRaw = Number(current.load_generation ?? current.loadGeneration ?? 0);

    child.sections[key] = {
      ...current,
      section: key,
      rows: nextRows,
      items: nextRows,
      loading: false,
      loaded: true,
      next_cursor: payload.next_cursor ?? payload.nextCursor ?? null,
      known_total_count: Number.isFinite(knownTotalRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(knownTotalRaw)) : current.known_total_count,
      error: '',
      source_purpose: sourcePurposeValue,
      sourcePurpose: sourcePurposeValue,
      current_tab: currentTabValue,
      currentTab: currentTabValue,
      loaded_at_utc: loadedAtUtc,
      in_flight_key: '',
      inFlightKey: '',
      pending_request_key: '',
      pendingRequestKey: '',
      last_request_key: requestKey || current.last_request_key || '',
      lastRequestKey: requestKey || current.lastRequestKey || '',
      last_success_key: requestKey || current.last_success_key || '',
      lastSuccessKey: requestKey || current.lastSuccessKey || '',
      failed_request_key: '',
      failedRequestKey: '',
      failed_at_utc: null,
      failedAtUtc: null,
      failed_cursor: null,
      failedCursor: null,
      last_error_payload: null,
      lastErrorPayload: null,
      stale: false,
      stale_reason: '',
      staleReason: '',
      marked_stale_at_utc: null,
      markedStaleAtUtc: null,
      explicit_retry_count: Number.isFinite(retryCountRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(retryCountRaw)) : 0,
      explicitRetryCount: Number.isFinite(retryCountRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(retryCountRaw)) : 0,
      load_generation: Number.isFinite(generationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(generationRaw)) : 0,
      loadGeneration: Number.isFinite(generationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(generationRaw)) : 0
    };
    child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
    child.sectionPages[key] = child.sections[key];
    child.lazy_sections = child.sections;

    if (key === 'overview_items') {
      child.overview_items = nextRows.slice();
      child.overviewItems = nextRows.slice();
      child.overview_items_page = child.sections[key];
      child.overviewItemsPage = child.sections[key];
      if (child.data && typeof child.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.data)) {
        child.data.overview_items = nextRows.slice();
        child.data.overviewItems = nextRows.slice();
        child.data.overview_items_page = child.sections[key];
        child.data.overviewItemsPage = child.sections[key];
      }
    }

    if (key === 'current_payment_status') {
      child.correction = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction)) ? child.correction : {};
      child.correction.rows = nextRows.slice();
      child.correction.current_payment_status_rows = nextRows.slice();
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusRows = nextRows.slice();
      child.correction.sectionRows = {
        ...(child.correction.sectionRows && typeof child.correction.sectionRows === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction.sectionRows) ? child.correction.sectionRows : {}),
        current_payment_status: nextRows.slice()
      };
      syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta(child.sections[key]);
      if (child.data && typeof child.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.data)) {
        child.data.current_payment_status_rows = nextRows.slice();
        child.data.currentPaymentStatusRows = nextRows.slice();
      }
    }

    return child.sections[key];
  };

  const markChildSectionStale = (sectionKey, reason = '', options = {}) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (!key) return null;
    ensureChildLazySections(child.data);
    const optsLocal = (options && typeof options === 'object' && !Array.isArray(options)) ? options : {};
    const preserveDataRows = optsLocal.preserveDataRows === true || optsLocal.preserve_data_rows === true || optsLocal.preserveData === true || optsLocal.preserve_data === true;
    const current = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections?.[key] || {});
    const generationRaw = Number(current.load_generation ?? current.loadGeneration ?? 0);
    const nextGeneration = Number.isFinite(generationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(generationRaw)) + 1 : 1;
    const staleReason = String(reason || optsLocal.reason || 'section_stale').trim() || 'section_stale';
    const nextCursor = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(optsLocal, 'next_cursor')
      ? optsLocal.next_cursor
      : (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(optsLocal, 'nextCursor') ? optsLocal.nextCursor : null);
    const knownTotalCount = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(optsLocal, 'known_total_count')
      ? optsLocal.known_total_count
      : (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(optsLocal, 'knownTotalCount') ? optsLocal.knownTotalCount : null);
    const nextState = {
      ...current,
      loading: false,
      loaded: false,
      error: '',
      stale: true,
      stale_reason: staleReason,
      staleReason,
      in_flight_key: '',
      inFlightKey: '',
      pending_request_key: '',
      pendingRequestKey: '',
      last_request_key: '',
      lastRequestKey: '',
      failed_request_key: '',
      failedRequestKey: '',
      failed_at_utc: null,
      failedAtUtc: null,
      failed_cursor: null,
      failedCursor: null,
      last_error_payload: null,
      lastErrorPayload: null,
      next_cursor: nextCursor,
      nextCursor,
      known_total_count: knownTotalCount,
      knownTotalCount: knownTotalCount,
      load_generation: nextGeneration,
      loadGeneration: nextGeneration,
      marked_stale_at_utc: new Date().toISOString(),
      markedStaleAtUtc: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    if (key === 'current_payment_status') {
      nextState.rows = [];
      nextState.items = [];
      nextState.data = [];
      nextState.no_rows = false;
      nextState.noRows = false;
      nextState.empty = false;
      nextState.empty_message = '';
      nextState.emptyMessage = '';
      nextState.known_total_count = null;
      nextState.knownTotalCount = null;
      nextState.next_cursor = null;
      nextState.nextCursor = null;
      child.correction = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction)) ? child.correction : {};
      child.correction.rows = [];
      child.correction.current_payment_status_rows = [];
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusRows = [];
      child.correction.sectionRows = (child.correction.sectionRows && typeof child.correction.sectionRows === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.correction.sectionRows)) ? child.correction.sectionRows : {};
      child.correction.sectionRows.current_payment_status = [];
      child.correction.current_payment_status_loaded = false;
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusLoaded = false;
      child.correction.current_payment_status_loading = false;
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusLoading = false;
      child.correction.current_payment_status_error = '';
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusError = '';
      child.correction.current_payment_status_failed_request_key = '';
      child.correction.currentPaymentStatusFailedRequestKey = '';
      if (!preserveDataRows && child.data && typeof child.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.data)) {
        child.data.current_payment_status_rows = [];
        child.data.currentPaymentStatusRows = [];
        child.data.payment_status_rows = [];
        child.data.paymentStatusRows = [];
      }
    }
    child.sections[key] = nextState;
    child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
    child.sectionPages[key] = nextState;
    child.lazy_sections = child.sections;
    if (key === 'current_payment_status') syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta(nextState);
    return nextState;
  };

  const loadChildSectionPage = async (sectionKey, {
    reset = false,
    silent = false,
    forceRetry = false,
    reason = '',
    cursor = undefined,
    userInitiated = undefined,
    filters = null,
    sort = null,
    current_section_json = null,
    currentSectionJson = null,
    allowNonBootstrap = false,
    allow_non_bootstrap = false,
    allowFullDetailSectionLoad = false,
    allow_full_detail_section_load = false
  } = {}) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (!key) return null;
    const allowNonBootstrapSectionLoad = allowNonBootstrap === true || allow_non_bootstrap === true || allowFullDetailSectionLoad === true || allow_full_detail_section_load === true;
    if (!isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data) && !allowNonBootstrapSectionLoad) return null;

    ensureChildLazySections(child.data);

    const activeTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
    const requestPurpose = getChildSectionRequestPurpose(key, activeTabKey);
    const requestActionContext = getChildSectionActionContext(key, activeTabKey, requestPurpose);
    const requestLimit = getChildSectionRequestLimit(key);
    const current = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
    const requestedCursor = cursor !== undefined ? cursor : (reset === true ? null : current.next_cursor);
    const firstPage = reset === true || isChildSectionFirstCursor(requestedCursor);
    const currentSectionJsonValue = currentSectionJson !== null && currentSectionJson !== undefined ? currentSectionJson : current_section_json;
    const pageKey = buildChildSectionPageKey({
      sectionKey: key,
      cursor: firstPage ? null : requestedCursor,
      limit: requestLimit,
      purpose: requestPurpose,
      actionContext: requestActionContext,
      activeTabKey,
      filters,
      sort,
      currentSectionJson: currentSectionJsonValue
    });
    const explicitUserInitiated = userInitiated === undefined ? !silent : userInitiated === true;
    const expectedOpenToken = String(child.openToken || '').trim();
    const reasonText = String(reason || '').trim();

    child.__sectionLoadPromises = (child.__sectionLoadPromises && typeof child.__sectionLoadPromises === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.__sectionLoadPromises)) ? child.__sectionLoadPromises : {};

    if (typeof bankingPayBatchGetSectionPage !== 'function') {
      const missingMessage = 'Payment batch paging helper is not available.';
      const failed = setChildSectionLocalError(key, missingMessage, { code: 'BANKING_PAY_SECTION_HELPER_MISSING', reason: reasonText }, pageKey, firstPage ? null : requestedCursor);
      if (!silent) await rerenderChild();
      return failed;
    }

    if (child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey] && reset !== true) {
      try { return await child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey]; } catch { return createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]); }
    }

    if (current.loading === true && reset !== true) {
      if (current.in_flight_key && current.in_flight_key === pageKey && child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey]) {
        try { return await child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey]; } catch { return createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]); }
      }
      return current;
    }

    if (forceRetry !== true && current.failed_request_key && current.failed_request_key === pageKey) {
      return current;
    }

    if (forceRetry !== true && firstPage === true && current.loaded === true && !reset) {
      return current;
    }

    if (forceRetry !== true && firstPage !== true && !current.next_cursor) {
      return current;
    }

    const retryCountRaw = Number(current.explicit_retry_count ?? current.explicitRetryCount ?? 0);
    const generationRaw = Number(current.load_generation ?? current.loadGeneration ?? 0);
    const retryCount = Number.isFinite(retryCountRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(retryCountRaw)) + (forceRetry === true ? 1 : 0) : (forceRetry === true ? 1 : 0);
    const generation = Number.isFinite(generationRaw) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(generationRaw)) + 1 : 1;
    const loadingState = {
      ...current,
      loading: true,
      error: '',
      in_flight_key: pageKey,
      inFlightKey: pageKey,
      pending_request_key: pageKey,
      pendingRequestKey: pageKey,
      last_request_key: pageKey,
      lastRequestKey: pageKey,
      failed_request_key: forceRetry === true ? '' : current.failed_request_key,
      failedRequestKey: forceRetry === true ? '' : current.failedRequestKey,
      failed_at_utc: forceRetry === true ? null : current.failed_at_utc,
      failedAtUtc: forceRetry === true ? null : current.failedAtUtc,
      failed_cursor: forceRetry === true ? null : current.failed_cursor,
      failedCursor: forceRetry === true ? null : current.failedCursor,
      last_error_payload: forceRetry === true ? null : current.last_error_payload,
      lastErrorPayload: forceRetry === true ? null : current.lastErrorPayload,
      explicit_retry_count: retryCount,
      explicitRetryCount: retryCount,
      load_generation: generation,
      loadGeneration: generation
    };
    child.sections[key] = loadingState;
    child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
    child.sectionPages[key] = loadingState;
    child.lazy_sections = child.sections;
    if (key === 'current_payment_status') syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta(loadingState);
    if (!silent) await rerenderChild();

    const loadPromise = (async () => {
      try {
        const page = await bankingPayBatchGetSectionPage(id, key, {
          cursor: firstPage ? null : requestedCursor,
          limit: requestLimit,
          purpose: requestPurpose,
          section_purpose: requestPurpose,
          sectionPurpose: requestPurpose,
          current_tab: activeTabKey,
          currentTab: activeTabKey,
          action_context: requestActionContext,
          actionContext: requestActionContext,
          filters: filters || undefined,
          sort: sort || undefined,
          current_section_json: currentSectionJsonValue || undefined,
          currentSectionJson: currentSectionJsonValue || undefined,
          userInitiated: explicitUserInitiated,
          silent
        });

        if (!shouldApplyChildMutation(expectedOpenToken, null)) return createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);

        const latestBeforeMerge = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
        const latestGeneration = Number(latestBeforeMerge.load_generation ?? latestBeforeMerge.loadGeneration ?? 0);
        if (Number.isFinite(latestGeneration) && latestGeneration !== generation) return latestBeforeMerge;
        if (latestBeforeMerge.in_flight_key && latestBeforeMerge.in_flight_key !== pageKey) return latestBeforeMerge;

        const merged = mergeSectionPageIntoChild(key, {
          ...page,
          append: firstPage ? false : true,
          reset: firstPage,
          cursor: firstPage ? null : requestedCursor,
          request_cursor: firstPage ? null : requestedCursor,
          requestCursor: firstPage ? null : requestedCursor,
          request_key: pageKey,
          requestKey: pageKey,
          source_purpose: requestPurpose,
          sourcePurpose: requestPurpose,
          current_tab: activeTabKey,
          currentTab: activeTabKey
        });

        syncChildCommunicationsState(child.data);
        if (key !== 'overview_items' && (key === 'current_payment_status' || key === 'finance_case_groups' || key === 'items' || key === 'transfers' || key === 'events' || key === 'auth_history')) hydratePaymentIssueState();
        return merged;
      } catch (e) {
        const backendPayload = (e?.json && typeof e.json === 'object' && !Array.isArray(e.json))
          ? e.json
          : ((e?.payload && typeof e.payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(e.payload)) ? e.payload : null);
        let friendly = null;
        try {
          friendly = normaliseChildFriendlyError(e, 'PAY_BATCH_GET_FAILED', {
            action: 'GET_BATCH_SECTION_PAGE',
            userInitiated: explicitUserInitiated,
            silent: true,
            scope: key,
            backendPayload,
            technicalError: e
          });
        } catch {
          friendly = null;
        }
        const message = String(
          friendly?.user_message ||
          friendly?.message ||
          friendly?.error ||
          e?.json?.user_message ||
          e?.json?.message ||
          e?.message ||
          e ||
          'Section page could not be loaded.'
        ).trim() || 'Section page could not be loaded.';
        const latestBeforeError = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
        const latestErrorGeneration = Number(latestBeforeError.load_generation ?? latestBeforeError.loadGeneration ?? 0);
        if (Number.isFinite(latestErrorGeneration) && latestErrorGeneration !== generation) return latestBeforeError;
        const failed = setChildSectionLocalError(key, message, backendPayload || friendly || { message }, pageKey, firstPage ? null : requestedCursor);
        if (explicitUserInitiated === true && forceRetry === true && silent !== true) {
          try { toast(message); } catch {}
        }
        return failed;
      } finally {
        try { if (child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey] === loadPromise) delete child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey]; } catch {}
        try {
          const latest = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
          const latestFinallyGeneration = Number(latest.load_generation ?? latest.loadGeneration ?? 0);
          if ((latest.in_flight_key === pageKey || latest.pending_request_key === pageKey) && Number.isFinite(latestFinallyGeneration) && latestFinallyGeneration === generation) {
            const next = {
              ...latest,
              loading: false,
              in_flight_key: '',
              inFlightKey: '',
              pending_request_key: '',
              pendingRequestKey: ''
            };
            child.sections[key] = next;
            child.sectionPages = (child.sectionPages && typeof child.sectionPages === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.sectionPages)) ? child.sectionPages : {};
            child.sectionPages[key] = next;
            child.lazy_sections = child.sections;
            if (key === 'current_payment_status') syncCurrentPaymentStatusSectionMeta(next);
          }
        } catch {}
        if (!silent && shouldApplyChildMutation(expectedOpenToken, null)) await rerenderChild();
      }
    })();

    child.__sectionLoadPromises[pageKey] = loadPromise;
    return await loadPromise;
  };

  const ensureFirstPageForActiveTab = (tabKey) => {
    try {
      if (!isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data)) return;
      const activeTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey || child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
      const sectionKeys = getAutoLoadSectionsForTab(activeTabKey);
      if (!sectionKeys.length) return;
      ensureChildLazySections(child.data);
      child.__firstPageTimers = (child.__firstPageTimers && typeof child.__firstPageTimers === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.__firstPageTimers)) ? child.__firstPageTimers : {};

      for (const sectionKey of sectionKeys) {
        const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
        if (!key) continue;
        const requestPurpose = getChildSectionRequestPurpose(key, activeTabKey);
        const requestActionContext = getChildSectionActionContext(key, activeTabKey, requestPurpose);
        const requestLimit = getChildSectionRequestLimit(key);
        const pageKey = buildChildSectionPageKey({
          sectionKey: key,
          cursor: null,
          limit: requestLimit,
          purpose: requestPurpose,
          actionContext: requestActionContext,
          activeTabKey,
          filters: null,
          sort: null,
          currentSectionJson: null
        });
        const state = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
        if (state.loaded === true) continue;
        if (state.loading === true) continue;
        if (state.in_flight_key && state.in_flight_key === pageKey) continue;
        if (state.pending_request_key && state.pending_request_key === pageKey) continue;
        if (state.failed_request_key && state.failed_request_key === pageKey) continue;
        if (state.error && state.failed_request_key) continue;
        if (child.__firstPageTimers[pageKey]) continue;

        const expectedOpenToken = String(child.openToken || '').trim();
        const expectedBatchId = String(id || '').trim();
        const expectedTabKey = activeTabKey;
        const expectedSectionKey = key;
        child.__firstPageTimers[pageKey] = setTimeout(() => {
          try {
            clearChildSectionTimer(pageKey);
            if (!currentChildStateMatches()) return;
            if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return;
            if (String(child.openToken || '').trim() !== expectedOpenToken) return;
            if (String(child.batchId || child.payBatchId || child.pay_batch_id || '').trim() !== expectedBatchId) return;
            const liveTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
            if (liveTabKey !== expectedTabKey) return;
            const latest = createChildSectionState(expectedSectionKey, child.sections[expectedSectionKey]);
            if (latest.loaded === true || latest.loading === true) return;
            if (latest.failed_request_key && latest.failed_request_key === pageKey) return;
            if (latest.error && latest.failed_request_key) return;
            loadChildSectionPage(expectedSectionKey, {
              reset: true,
              silent: false,
              forceRetry: false,
              reason: 'auto-first-page',
              userInitiated: false
            }).catch(() => {});
          } catch {}
        }, 0);
        break;
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const renderSectionLoadMoreHtml = (tabKey) => {
    if (!isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data)) return '';
    const sectionKeys = getChildSectionsForTab(tabKey);
    if (!sectionKeys.length) return '';
    ensureChildLazySections(child.data);

    const shouldShowDebugPagingControls = shouldShowChildPagingDebugControls();

    const rows = sectionKeys.map((sectionKey) => {
      const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
      const state = createChildSectionState(key, child.sections[key]);
      const label = key.replace(/_/g, ' ');
      const rowCount = Array.isArray(state.rows) ? state.rows.length : 0;
      const knownTotal = Number.isFinite(Number(state.known_total_count))
        ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(Number(state.known_total_count)))
        : null;
      const displayTotal = knownTotal !== null && knownTotal >= rowCount ? knownTotal : null;
      const totalText = displayTotal === null ? '' : ` of ${displayTotal}`;
      const hasNextPage = !!state.next_cursor;
      const hasError = !!String(state.error || '').trim();
      const isLoading = state.loading === true;
      const isUnloadedDebugSection = shouldShowDebugPagingControls && state.loaded !== true && isLoading !== true && hasError !== true;
      const shouldShowRow = shouldShowDebugPagingControls || hasNextPage || hasError || (isLoading && (hasNextPage || state.loaded !== true));
      if (!shouldShowRow) return '';
      const canLoadMore = isLoading !== true && (hasNextPage || hasError || isUnloadedDebugSection);
      const loadButtonLabel = isLoading ? 'Loading…' : (hasError ? 'Retry section' : (isUnloadedDebugSection ? 'Load section' : 'Load more'));
      const loadButtonTitle = hasError
        ? 'Retry loading this section'
        : (hasNextPage ? 'Load the next page' : (isUnloadedDebugSection ? 'Load this paged section' : 'No more rows to load'));
      return `
        <div class="card" style="padding:8px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <div class="mini" style="font-weight:700;opacity:.9;">${enc(label)}</div>
            <div class="mini" style="opacity:.82;">Loaded <span class="mono">${enc(String(rowCount))}</span>${enc(totalText)}${isLoading ? ' · loading…' : ''}${hasError ? ` · ${enc(state.error)}` : ''}${isUnloadedDebugSection ? ' · not loaded' : ''}</div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="btn btn-sm btn-outline"
            data-action="banking:pay:child:loadSectionMore"
            data-section="${enc(key)}"
            ${canLoadMore ? '' : 'data-disabled="1" aria-disabled="true" style="opacity:.45;filter:saturate(0.6) brightness(0.9);"'}
            title="${enc(loadButtonTitle)}"
          >${enc(loadButtonLabel)}</button>
        </div>
      `;
    }).filter((rowHtml) => String(rowHtml || '').trim()).join('');

    if (!rows) return '';

    return `
      <details class="card" style="padding:10px;margin-top:10px;"${shouldShowDebugPagingControls ? ' open' : ''}>
        <summary class="mini" style="font-weight:700;opacity:.9;cursor:pointer;">Additional paged detail rows</summary>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;">${rows}</div>
      </details>
    `;
  };

  const postJson = async (path, payload) => {
    if (typeof apiPostJson !== 'function') throw new Error('apiPostJson missing');
    return await apiPostJson(path, payload || {});
  };

  const patchPaymentIssueRowsForManualMode = (rows, manualPaidActionMode) => {
    const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
    const toTextLocal = (value) => String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    const toUpperLocal = (value) => toTextLocal(value).toUpperCase();

    for (const row of list) {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') continue;
      const issueKind = toUpperLocal(row.issueKindForDisplay || row.issue_kind_for_display || row.issue_kind || '');
      const displayState = toUpperLocal(row.displayState || row.display_state || '');
      const oldAction = toUpperLocal(row.actionLabel || row.action_label || '');
      if (issueKind === 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION') row.issueKindForDisplay = displayState || 'PAYMENT_PAID';
      row.manualActionAvailable = false;
      row.manual_action_available = false;
      if (manualPaidActionMode === true && (displayState === 'PAYMENT_SENT' || displayState === 'PAYMENT_PAID' || issueKind === 'PAID' || issueKind === 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION')) {
        row.actionType = row.actionType || 'CANDIDATE_RETURNED_FUNDS';
        row.actionLabel = 'Recover overpayment in next pay run';
      } else if (oldAction.includes('ELIGIBLE')) {
        row.actionLabel = (typeof getBankingPaymentIssueActionLabel === 'function') ? getBankingPaymentIssueActionLabel(row) : 'View details';
      }
      if (issueKind === 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION') {
        row.selectable = false;
        row.selectableForApply = false;
        row.selectableForReview = false;
      }
    }

    return list;
  };

  const resetPaymentIssueLocalSelectionAndPlan = (correctionState) => {
    const state = (correctionState && typeof correctionState === 'object') ? correctionState : null;
    if (!state) return;

    state.selectedRowKeys = new Set();
    state.selectedPayBatchCandidateIds = new Set();
    state.selectedTransferIds = new Set();
    state.selectedPayBatchItemIds = new Set();
    state.selectedUmbrellaGroup = null;
    state.selectedTransferGroupKey = '';
    state.selectionScopeMode = '';
    state.lastSelectionMode = '';
    state.selection = null;
    state.plan = null;
    state.planSelectionSignature = '';
    state.planError = '';
    state.acceptedResolutionJson = null;
    state.acceptedResolutionSummary = null;

    try {
      if (Array.isArray(state.rows)) {
        for (const row of state.rows) {
          if (row && typeof row === 'object') row.selected = false;
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      if (typeof invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan === 'function') invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan(state);
    } catch {}
  };


  const rowIsDraftOnlyPaymentIssueRemoval = (row) => {
    const r = (row && typeof row === 'object') ? row : {};
    const issueKind = String(r.issueKindForDisplay || r.issue_kind_for_display || r.issue_kind || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return r.draftOnlyRemoval === true || r.draft_only_removal === true || issueKind === 'DRAFT_REMOVE';
  };

  const selectionIsDraftOnlyPaymentIssueRemoval = (selection, rowsForAction = []) => {
    const sel = (selection && typeof selection === 'object') ? selection : {};
    const sourceContext = String(sel.source_context || sel.sourceContext || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (sourceContext === 'DRAFT_REMOVE_FROM_BATCH') return true;

    const requestedAction = String(sel.requested_action || sel.requestedAction || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (requestedAction && requestedAction !== 'CANCEL_PAYMENT_ATTEMPT') return false;

    const selectedList = Array.isArray(rowsForAction) ? rowsForAction : [];
    return selectedList.length > 0 && selectedList.every((row) => rowIsDraftOnlyPaymentIssueRemoval(row));
  };

  const getPaymentIssueFinanceSuggestionEnvelope = (planObj) => {
    const raw = (typeof getBankingPaymentIssueFinanceSuggestion === 'function')
      ? getBankingPaymentIssueFinanceSuggestion(planObj)
      : null;
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return { suggestion: null, required: false, raw: null };
    const suggestion = (raw.suggestion && typeof raw.suggestion === 'object')
      ? raw.suggestion
      : ((raw.suggested_resolution && typeof raw.suggested_resolution === 'object') ? raw.suggested_resolution : raw);
    return {
      suggestion: suggestion || null,
      required: raw.required === true,
      raw
    };
  };

 const paymentIssueFinanceResolutionRequired = (planObj, context = {}) => {
    const planSafe = (planObj && typeof planObj === 'object') ? planObj : null;
    if (!planSafe) return false;
    const contextSafe = (context && typeof context === 'object') ? context : {};
    if (contextSafe.draftOnlyRemoval === true || contextSafe.draft_only_removal === true) return false;
    const sourceContext = String(planSafe?.selection?.source_context || planSafe?.selection?.sourceContext || planSafe?.source_context || planSafe?.sourceContext || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const requestedAction = String(planSafe?.selection?.requested_action || planSafe?.selection?.requestedAction || planSafe?.requested_action || planSafe?.requestedAction || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const classification = String(planSafe?.classification || planSafe?.movement_classification_detail?.classification || planSafe?.plan?.classification || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (sourceContext === 'DRAFT_REMOVE_FROM_BATCH' && requestedAction === 'CANCEL_PAYMENT_ATTEMPT' && classification === 'PRE_BANK_CANCEL') return false;
    const envelope = getPaymentIssueFinanceSuggestionEnvelope(planSafe);
    if (envelope.required === true) return true;
    return planSafe.finance_review_required === true ||
      planSafe.financeReviewRequired === true ||
      planSafe.suggested_resolution_required === true ||
      planSafe?.suggested_resolution?.required === true ||
      planSafe?.suggestedResolution?.required === true ||
      planSafe?.suggested_resolution_payload?.required === true ||
      (Array.isArray(planSafe.hard_blockers) && planSafe.hard_blockers.some((item) => {
        if (typeof item === 'string') return item.trim().toUpperCase() === 'SUGGESTED_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED';
        if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return false;
        return String(item.code || item.id || item.key || item.reason || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'SUGGESTED_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED';
      })) ||
      (Array.isArray(planSafe.blockers) && planSafe.blockers.some((item) => {
        if (typeof item === 'string') return item.trim().toUpperCase() === 'SUGGESTED_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED';
        if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return false;
        return String(item.code || item.id || item.key || item.reason || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'SUGGESTED_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED';
      }));
  };


  const refreshAfterPaymentIssueAction = async ({ draftOnlyRemoval = false, result = null } = {}) => {
    const resultObj = (result && typeof result === 'object') ? result : null;
    const refreshOpenToken = String(child.openToken || '').trim();

    const findRefreshPayload = (value, depth = 0, seen = new WeakSet()) => {
      if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value) || depth > 5) return null;
      if (seen.has(value)) return null;
      seen.add(value);

      const directCandidates = [
        value.post_action_refresh,
        value.postActionRefresh,
        value.post_cancel_refresh,
        value.postCancelRefresh,
        value.preview_refresh,
        value.previewRefresh,
        value.refresh,
        value.refresh_payload,
        value.refreshPayload
      ];
