      for (const candidate of directCandidates) {
        if (candidate && typeof candidate === 'object' && !Array.isArray(candidate)) return candidate;
      }

      const nestedCandidates = [
        value.result,
        value.start_result,
        value.startResult,
        value.process_result,
        value.processResult,
        value.apply_result,
        value.applyResult,
        value.correction_result,
        value.correctionResult,
        value.batch_get,
        value.batchGet,
        value.response,
        value.payload,
        value.data
      ];

      for (const candidate of nestedCandidates) {
        const found = findRefreshPayload(candidate, depth + 1, seen);
        if (found) return found;
      }

      return null;
    };

    const postActionRefresh = findRefreshPayload(resultObj) || {};
    const assertRefreshStillCurrent = () => shouldApplyChildMutation(refreshOpenToken, null);

    if (!assertRefreshStillCurrent()) return;

    child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
    child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';
    await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false, expectedOpenToken: refreshOpenToken });

    if (!assertRefreshStillCurrent()) return;

    try {
      if (typeof bankingPayBatchesList === 'function') {
        await bankingPayBatchesList({
          status: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.statusFilter : null,
          limit: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.limit : null,
          offset: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.offset : null
        });
      }
    } catch {
      try { toast('Batch list refresh failed. Refresh Banking and try again.'); } catch {}
    }

    if (!assertRefreshStillCurrent()) return;

    if (draftOnlyRemoval) {
      try {
        if (typeof resetPayPreviewAndDecisions === 'function') {
          await resetPayPreviewAndDecisions({
            mode: 'POST_ACTION_REOPEN',
            reason: 'PAYMENT_ISSUE_DRAFT_REMOVAL_SUCCESS',
            source_session_id: String(postActionRefresh.source_session_id || getCurrentWorkbenchSessionId() || '').trim() || null,
            replacement_session_id: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_session_id || '').trim() || null,
            replacement_session_signature: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_session_signature || '').trim() || null,
            replacement_snapshot_run_id: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_snapshot_run_id || '').trim() || null,
            replacement_session_version: postActionRefresh.replacement_session_version ?? null,
            dirty_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postActionRefresh.dirty_candidate_ids) ? postActionRefresh.dirty_candidate_ids : [],
            refresh_job_ids: Array.isArray(postActionRefresh.refresh_job_ids) ? postActionRefresh.refresh_job_ids : [],
            preview_reopen_required: true
          });
        } else {
          const pd = String(st?.pay?.draftWizard?.pay_date || '').trim();
          if (pd && typeof bankingPayPreview === 'function') {
            await bankingPayPreview({
              pay_date: pd,
              mode: 'POST_ACTION_REOPEN',
              hard_session_reload: true,
              replacement_session_id: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_session_id || '').trim() || null,
              replacement_session_signature: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_session_signature || '').trim() || null,
              replacement_snapshot_run_id: String(postActionRefresh.replacement_snapshot_run_id || '').trim() || null,
              replacement_session_version: postActionRefresh.replacement_session_version ?? null,
              dirty_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postActionRefresh.dirty_candidate_ids) ? postActionRefresh.dirty_candidate_ids : [],
              refresh_job_ids: Array.isArray(postActionRefresh.refresh_job_ids) ? postActionRefresh.refresh_job_ids : [],
              preview_reopen_required: true
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        await reportChildFriendlyError(e, 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED', {
          action: 'PREVIEW_REFRESH',
          userInitiated: false,
          silent: true,
          scope: null
        });
        await rerenderChild();
      }
    }

    if (!assertRefreshStillCurrent()) return;

    try {
      if (typeof refreshBankingNavAttentionFromCachedRows === 'function') refreshBankingNavAttentionFromCachedRows();
    } catch {}

    try {
      if (typeof bankingRerender === 'function') await bankingRerender(null);
    } catch (e) {
      await reportChildFriendlyError(e, 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', {
        action: 'BANKING_RERENDER',
        userInitiated: false,
        silent: true,
        scope: null
      });
    }

    if (draftOnlyRemoval) {
      try { toast('Payment removed from batch'); } catch {}
    }
  };


  const isRealPaymentIssueState = () => {
    const correctionState = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object') ? child.correction : {};
    const issueState = (correctionState.issueState && typeof correctionState.issueState === 'object') ? correctionState.issueState : {};
    const rows = Array.isArray(correctionState.rows) ? correctionState.rows : [];
    const toText = (value) => String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    const toUpper = (value) => toText(value).toUpperCase();

    if (false) return true;
    if (issueState.requiresUserAction === true || issueState.navAlert === true || issueState.hasActionableIssueRows === true) return true;

    const kind = toUpper(issueState.issueKindForDisplay || issueState.issueKind || issueState.issue_kind || '');
    if (kind && !['NONE', 'PAID', 'CLEAN_PAID', 'CANCELLED', 'SETTLED_NO_CORRECTION_REQUIRED', 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION'].includes(kind)) return true;

    if (issueState.hasIssue === true && !['NONE', 'PAID', 'CLEAN_PAID', 'CANCELLED', 'SETTLED_NO_CORRECTION_REQUIRED', 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION'].includes(kind || '')) return true;

    return rows.some((row) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return false;
      const rowKind = toUpper(row.issueKindForDisplay || row.issue_kind_for_display || row.issue_kind || '');
      if (rowKind === 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION') return false;
      return (row.selectableForApply === true || row.selectableForReview === true) && !['NONE', 'PAID', 'CLEAN_PAID', 'CANCELLED'].includes(rowKind || 'NONE');
    });
  };

  const getPaymentIssueSectionRows = (sectionKey) => {
    const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
    if (!key) return [];
    const out = [];
    const seenObjects = new Set();
    const collect = (state) => {
      if (!state || typeof state !== 'object' || Array.isArray(state)) return;
      const stateSection = normaliseChildSectionKey(state.section || key) || key;
      const statePurpose = String(state.source_purpose || state.sourcePurpose || '').trim();
      const stateTab = String(state.current_tab || state.currentTab || '').trim();
      const candidates = [
        Array.isArray(state.rows) ? state.rows : [],
        Array.isArray(state.items) ? state.items : []
      ];
      for (const list of candidates) {
        for (const row of list) {
          if (!row || typeof row !== 'object' || Array.isArray(row)) continue;
          if (seenObjects.has(row)) continue;
          seenObjects.add(row);
          out.push({
            ...row,
            source_section: row.source_section || row.sourceSection || stateSection,
            sourceSection: row.sourceSection || row.source_section || stateSection,
            source_purpose: row.source_purpose || row.sourcePurpose || statePurpose,
            sourcePurpose: row.sourcePurpose || row.source_purpose || statePurpose,
            current_tab: row.current_tab || row.currentTab || stateTab,
            currentTab: row.currentTab || row.current_tab || stateTab
          });
        }
      }
    };
    try { collect(child.sections && child.sections[key]); } catch {}
    try { collect(child.lazy_sections && child.lazy_sections[key]); } catch {}
    return out;
  };

  const mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity = (sources, identityKeys = []) => {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const push = (entry) => {
      if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(entry)) return;
      const keyParts = [];
      for (const key of identityKeys) {
        const value = String(entry[key] ?? '').trim();
        if (value) keyParts.push(`${key}:${value}`);
      }
      const stableKey = keyParts.length ? keyParts.join('|') : '';
      if (stableKey && seen.has(stableKey)) return;
      if (stableKey) seen.add(stableKey);
      out.push(entry);
    };
    for (const source of sources) {
      const list = Array.isArray(source) ? source : [];
      for (const entry of list) push(entry);
    }
    return out;
  };

const buildPaymentIssueEvidencePayload = () => {
    const base = (child.data && typeof child.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.data)) ? deep(child.data) : {};
    const baseObj = (base && typeof base === 'object' && !Array.isArray(base)) ? base : {};
    const baseBatch = (baseObj.batch && typeof baseObj.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(baseObj.batch)) ? baseObj.batch : {};
    const bootstrapOnly = isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data);
    const minimalBatch = bootstrapOnly
      ? {
          id: baseBatch.id || baseObj.id || id,
          pay_batch_id: baseBatch.pay_batch_id || baseObj.pay_batch_id || id,
          payBatchId: baseBatch.payBatchId || baseObj.payBatchId || id,
          status: baseBatch.status || baseObj.status || null,
          batch_kind: baseBatch.batch_kind || baseObj.batch_kind || null,
          batch_kind_fixed: baseBatch.batch_kind_fixed || baseObj.batch_kind_fixed || null,
          pay_date: baseBatch.pay_date || baseObj.pay_date || null,
          execution_commit_state: baseBatch.execution_commit_state || baseObj.execution_commit_state || null,
          bootstrap_only: true
        }
      : baseBatch;
    const payload = bootstrapOnly
      ? {
          ok: baseObj.ok !== false,
          pay_batch_id: id,
          payBatchId: id,
          batch_id: id,
          batchId: id,
          status: minimalBatch.status || null,
          bootstrap_only: true,
          source_purpose: 'current_payment_status',
          evidence_source: 'explicit_loaded_payment_status_sections',
          batch: minimalBatch
        }
      : baseObj;
    const detail = (!bootstrapOnly && payload.detail && typeof payload.detail === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.detail))
      ? payload.detail
      : ((!bootstrapOnly && payload.details && typeof payload.details === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.details)) ? payload.details : {});
    const batch = (payload.batch && typeof payload.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.batch)) ? payload.batch : {};
    const markEvidenceRows = (rows, sectionName) => (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [])
      .filter((row) => row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row))
      .filter((row) => {
        const sectionNameKey = String(sectionName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const sectionNameUpper = sectionNameKey.toUpperCase();
        const section = String(row.section || row.source_section || row.sourceSection || row.page_section || row.pageSection || row.presentation_source || row.presentationSource || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const purpose = String(row.purpose || row.source_purpose || row.sourcePurpose || row.section_purpose || row.sectionPurpose || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const evidenceSource = String(row.evidence_source || row.evidenceSource || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const rowKind = String(row.row_kind || row.rowKind || row.kind || row.type || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const actionText = String(row.actionType || row.action_type || row.bulkActionType || row.bulk_action_type || row.backendAction || row.backend_action || row.recommended_action || row.recommendedAction || row.requested_action || row.requestedAction || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const issueKind = String(row.issueKindForDisplay || row.issue_kind_for_display || row.issue_kind || row.issueKind || row.issue_type || row.issueType || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const explicitEvidencePurposes = new Set([
          'CURRENT_PAYMENT_STATUS',
          'PAYMENT_STATUS',
          'PAYMENT_ISSUES',
          'PAYMENT_ISSUE_EVIDENCE',
          'PROVIDER_EVIDENCE',
          'PROVIDER_PAYMENT_STATUS',
          'ACTION_DIAGNOSTIC',
          'CANCEL_REVERSE_CORRECT',
          'CORRECTION_ACTION'
        ]);
        const explicitEvidenceSectionNames = new Set([
          'current_payment_status',
          'payment_issues',
          'bank_evidence',
          'transfer_events',
          'provider_events',
          'provider_transfer_events',
          'provider_submit_chunks',
          'transfer_provider_outcomes'
        ]);
        const isExplicitEvidencePurpose = explicitEvidenceSectionNames.has(sectionNameKey) ||
          section === 'CURRENT_PAYMENT_STATUS' ||
          explicitEvidencePurposes.has(section) ||
          explicitEvidencePurposes.has(purpose) ||
          explicitEvidencePurposes.has(evidenceSource);
        if (!isExplicitEvidencePurpose) return false;
        if (section === 'OVERVIEW' || section === 'OVERVIEW_ITEMS' || section === 'OVERVIEW_ITEM') return false;
        if (row.overview_item === true || row.overviewItem === true || row.is_overview_item === true || row.isOverviewItem === true) return false;
        if (rowKind === 'OVERVIEW_ITEM' || rowKind === 'OVERVIEW_SUPPORT' || rowKind === 'ITEM_SUPPORT') return false;
        const batchStatus = String(batch.status || payload.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const commitState = String(batch.execution_commit_state || payload.execution_commit_state || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const transferId = String(row.pay_bank_transfer_id || row.payBankTransferId || row.transfer_id || row.transferId || '').trim();
        const providerRef = String(row.rail_tx_id || row.railTxId || row.provider_transaction_id || row.providerTransactionId || row.provider_reference || row.providerReference || '').trim();
        const providerDiagnosticPresent = !!(
          row.providerSubmitDiagnostic ||
          row.provider_submit_diagnostic ||
          row.provider_response ||
          row.providerResponse ||
          row.provider_response_json ||
          row.providerResponseJson ||
          row.provider_submission_status ||
          row.providerSubmissionStatus ||
          row.outcome_code ||
          row.outcomeCode ||
          row.provider_error_code ||
          row.providerErrorCode ||
          row.provider_http_status ||
          row.providerHttpStatus
        );
        const draftOnlyEvidenceAction = row.draftOnlyRemoval === true ||
          row.draft_only_removal === true ||
          rowKind === 'DRAFT_REMOVE' ||
          issueKind === 'DRAFT_REMOVE' ||
          actionText === 'DRAFT_REMOVE' ||
          actionText === 'CANCEL_PAYMENT_ATTEMPT' ||
          actionText === 'PRE_PROVIDER_CANCEL_AND_RECALCULATE';
        const draftOrNotSubmitted = batchStatus === 'DRAFT' || batchStatus === 'DRAFT_CREATED' || batchStatus === 'READY' || commitState === 'NOT_SUBMITTED';
        if (draftOrNotSubmitted && !transferId && !providerRef && !providerDiagnosticPresent && !draftOnlyEvidenceAction) return false;
        return true;
      })
      .map((row) => ({
        ...row,
        source_section: row.source_section || row.sourceSection || sectionName,
        sourceSection: row.sourceSection || row.source_section || sectionName,
        source_purpose: row.source_purpose || row.sourcePurpose || (sectionName === 'current_payment_status' ? 'current_payment_status' : 'payment_issue_evidence'),
        sourcePurpose: row.sourcePurpose || row.source_purpose || (sectionName === 'current_payment_status' ? 'current_payment_status' : 'payment_issue_evidence'),
        evidence_source: row.evidence_source || row.evidenceSource || 'explicit_loaded_section',
        evidenceSource: row.evidenceSource || row.evidence_source || 'explicit_loaded_section'
      }));

    try { delete payload.overview_items; delete payload.overviewItems; delete payload.overview_items_page; delete payload.overviewItemsPage; delete payload.overview_page; delete payload.overviewPage; } catch {}

    const sectionCandidates = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('candidates'), 'candidates');
    const sectionItems = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('items'), 'items');
    const sectionTransfers = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('transfers'), 'transfers');
    const sectionFinanceGroups = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('finance_case_groups'), 'finance_case_groups');
    const sectionEvents = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('events'), 'events');
    const sectionAuthHistory = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('auth_history'), 'auth_history');
    const sectionCurrentPaymentStatus = markEvidenceRows(getPaymentIssueSectionRows('current_payment_status'), 'current_payment_status');

    const candidateKeys = ['pay_batch_candidate_id', 'payBatchCandidateId', 'candidate_id', 'candidateId', 'id'];
    const itemKeys = ['pay_batch_item_id', 'payBatchItemId', 'item_id', 'itemId', 'id'];
    const transferKeys = ['pay_bank_transfer_id', 'payBankTransferId', 'transfer_id', 'transferId', 'id'];
    const eventKeys = ['event_id', 'eventId', 'id', 'pay_bank_transfer_event_id', 'payBankTransferEventId', 'pay_bank_transfer_id', 'payBankTransferId'];
    const issueKeys = ['rowKey', 'row_key', 'issue_id', 'issueId', 'pay_bank_transfer_id', 'payBankTransferId', 'transfer_id', 'transferId'];

    payload.candidates = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.candidates)) ? payload.candidates : [], 'candidates'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.candidates)) ? detail.candidates : [], 'candidates'),
      sectionCandidates
    ], candidateKeys);

    payload.items = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.items)) ? payload.items : [], 'items'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.items)) ? detail.items : [], 'items'),
      sectionItems
    ], itemKeys);

    payload.current_payment_status_rows = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.current_payment_status_rows)) ? payload.current_payment_status_rows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.currentPaymentStatusRows)) ? payload.currentPaymentStatusRows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.payment_status_rows)) ? payload.payment_status_rows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.paymentStatusRows)) ? payload.paymentStatusRows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.current_payment_status_rows)) ? detail.current_payment_status_rows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.currentPaymentStatusRows)) ? detail.currentPaymentStatusRows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.payment_status_rows)) ? detail.payment_status_rows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.paymentStatusRows)) ? detail.paymentStatusRows : [], 'current_payment_status'),
      sectionCurrentPaymentStatus
    ], transferKeys);
    payload.currentPaymentStatusRows = payload.current_payment_status_rows;
    payload.payment_status_rows = payload.current_payment_status_rows;
    payload.paymentStatusRows = payload.current_payment_status_rows;

    payload.transfers = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      payload.current_payment_status_rows,
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.transfers)) ? payload.transfers : [], 'transfers'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.transfers)) ? detail.transfers : [], 'transfers'),
      sectionTransfers
    ], transferKeys);

    payload.finance_case_groups = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.finance_case_groups)) ? payload.finance_case_groups : [], 'finance_case_groups'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.financeCaseGroups)) ? payload.financeCaseGroups : [], 'finance_case_groups'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.finance_case_groups)) ? detail.finance_case_groups : [], 'finance_case_groups'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.financeCaseGroups)) ? detail.financeCaseGroups : [], 'finance_case_groups'),
      sectionFinanceGroups
    ], ['finance_case_id', 'financeCaseId', 'case_id', 'caseId', 'id']);

    payload.bank_evidence = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.bank_evidence)) ? payload.bank_evidence : [], 'bank_evidence'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.bankEvidence)) ? payload.bankEvidence : [], 'bank_evidence'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.bank_evidence)) ? detail.bank_evidence : [], 'bank_evidence'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.bankEvidence)) ? detail.bankEvidence : [], 'bank_evidence'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(batch.bank_evidence)) ? batch.bank_evidence : [], 'bank_evidence'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(batch.bankEvidence)) ? batch.bankEvidence : [], 'bank_evidence')
    ], eventKeys);

    payload.payment_issues = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.payment_issues)) ? payload.payment_issues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.paymentIssues)) ? payload.paymentIssues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.issues)) ? payload.issues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.payment_issues)) ? detail.payment_issues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.paymentIssues)) ? detail.paymentIssues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.issues)) ? detail.issues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(batch.payment_issues)) ? batch.payment_issues : [], 'payment_issues'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(batch.paymentIssues)) ? batch.paymentIssues : [], 'payment_issues')
    ], issueKeys);

    payload.events = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.events)) ? payload.events : [], 'events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.events)) ? detail.events : [], 'events'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], eventKeys);

    payload.transfer_events = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.transfer_events)) ? payload.transfer_events : [], 'transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.transferEvents)) ? payload.transferEvents : [], 'transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.transfer_events)) ? detail.transfer_events : [], 'transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.transferEvents)) ? detail.transferEvents : [], 'transfer_events'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], eventKeys);

    payload.provider_events = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.provider_events)) ? payload.provider_events : [], 'provider_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.providerEvents)) ? payload.providerEvents : [], 'provider_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.provider_events)) ? detail.provider_events : [], 'provider_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.providerEvents)) ? detail.providerEvents : [], 'provider_events'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], eventKeys);

    payload.provider_transfer_events = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.provider_transfer_events)) ? payload.provider_transfer_events : [], 'provider_transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.providerTransferEvents)) ? payload.providerTransferEvents : [], 'provider_transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.provider_transfer_events)) ? detail.provider_transfer_events : [], 'provider_transfer_events'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.providerTransferEvents)) ? detail.providerTransferEvents : [], 'provider_transfer_events'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], eventKeys);

    payload.provider_submit_chunks = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.provider_submit_chunks)) ? payload.provider_submit_chunks : [], 'provider_submit_chunks'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.providerSubmitChunks)) ? payload.providerSubmitChunks : [], 'provider_submit_chunks'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.provider_submit_chunks)) ? detail.provider_submit_chunks : [], 'provider_submit_chunks'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.providerSubmitChunks)) ? detail.providerSubmitChunks : [], 'provider_submit_chunks'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], ['provider_submit_chunk_id', 'providerSubmitChunkId', 'chunk_id', 'chunkId', 'id']);

    payload.transfer_provider_outcomes = mergePaymentIssueArrayByIdentity([
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.transfer_provider_outcomes)) ? payload.transfer_provider_outcomes : [], 'transfer_provider_outcomes'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(payload.transferProviderOutcomes)) ? payload.transferProviderOutcomes : [], 'transfer_provider_outcomes'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.transfer_provider_outcomes)) ? detail.transfer_provider_outcomes : [], 'transfer_provider_outcomes'),
      markEvidenceRows((!bootstrapOnly && Array.isArray(detail.transferProviderOutcomes)) ? detail.transferProviderOutcomes : [], 'transfer_provider_outcomes'),
      sectionEvents,
      sectionAuthHistory
    ], transferKeys);

    const paymentIssueSummary = (!bootstrapOnly && payload.payment_issue_summary && typeof payload.payment_issue_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.payment_issue_summary))
      ? payload.payment_issue_summary
      : {};
    const detailPaymentIssueSummary = (!bootstrapOnly && detail.payment_issue_summary && typeof detail.payment_issue_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(detail.payment_issue_summary))
      ? detail.payment_issue_summary
      : {};
    const batchPaymentIssueSummary = (!bootstrapOnly && batch.payment_issue_summary && typeof batch.payment_issue_summary === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batch.payment_issue_summary))
      ? batch.payment_issue_summary
      : {};
    payload.payment_issue_summary = {
      ...batchPaymentIssueSummary,
      ...detailPaymentIssueSummary,
      ...paymentIssueSummary
    };

    const sectionLoadedFlag = (sectionKey) => {
      try {
        const key = normaliseChildSectionKey(sectionKey);
        const state = child.sections && child.sections[key] ? child.sections[key] : null;
        return state && state.loaded === true;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    };

    payload.loaded_transfer_section_data = {
      current_payment_status: sectionCurrentPaymentStatus,
      transfers: sectionTransfers,
      items: sectionItems,
      finance_case_groups: sectionFinanceGroups
    };
    payload.loaded_transfer_section_flags = {
      current_payment_status: sectionLoadedFlag('current_payment_status'),
      transfers: sectionLoadedFlag('transfers'),
      items: sectionLoadedFlag('items'),
      finance_case_groups: sectionLoadedFlag('finance_case_groups')
    };
    payload.loaded_event_section_data = {
      events: sectionEvents,
      auth_history: sectionAuthHistory,
      transfer_events: payload.transfer_events,
      provider_events: payload.provider_events,
      provider_transfer_events: payload.provider_transfer_events
    };
    payload.loaded_event_section_flags = {
      events: sectionLoadedFlag('events'),
      auth_history: sectionLoadedFlag('auth_history')
    };

    payload.__payment_issue_evidence_payload = true;
    payload.__payment_issue_evidence_source = 'explicit_loaded_payment_status_sections';
    payload.source_purpose = 'current_payment_status';
    payload.sourcePurpose = 'current_payment_status';
    payload.excludes_overview_items = true;
    payload.excludes_bootstrap_display_summary = bootstrapOnly === true;

    payload.detail = {
      ...detail,
      candidates: payload.candidates,
      current_payment_status_rows: payload.current_payment_status_rows,
      currentPaymentStatusRows: payload.currentPaymentStatusRows,
      payment_status_rows: payload.payment_status_rows,
      items: payload.items,
      transfers: payload.transfers,
      finance_case_groups: payload.finance_case_groups,
      bank_evidence: payload.bank_evidence,
      payment_issues: payload.payment_issues,
      payment_issue_summary: payload.payment_issue_summary,
      events: payload.events,
      auth_history: sectionAuthHistory,
      transfer_events: payload.transfer_events,
      provider_events: payload.provider_events,
      provider_transfer_events: payload.provider_transfer_events,
      provider_submit_chunks: payload.provider_submit_chunks,
      transfer_provider_outcomes: payload.transfer_provider_outcomes
    };

    return payload;
  };

  const paymentIssuePayloadHasLoadedEvidenceSections = (payload) => {
    try {
      const loaded = payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? payload.loaded_transfer_section_data : null;
      const events = payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? payload.loaded_event_section_data : null;
      const loadedFlags = payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? payload.loaded_transfer_section_flags : null;
      const eventFlags = payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload) ? payload.loaded_event_section_flags : null;
      return !!(
        loadedFlags?.current_payment_status === true ||
        loadedFlags?.transfers === true ||
        loadedFlags?.items === true ||
        loadedFlags?.finance_case_groups === true ||
        eventFlags?.events === true ||
        eventFlags?.auth_history === true ||
        (Array.isArray(loaded?.current_payment_status) && loaded.current_payment_status.length > 0) ||
        (Array.isArray(loaded?.transfers) && loaded.transfers.length > 0) ||
        (Array.isArray(loaded?.items) && loaded.items.length > 0) ||
        (Array.isArray(loaded?.finance_case_groups) && loaded.finance_case_groups.length > 0) ||
        (Array.isArray(events?.events) && events.events.length > 0) ||
        (Array.isArray(events?.auth_history) && events.auth_history.length > 0)
      );
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const paymentIssuePayloadHasProviderSubmitEvidence = (payload) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    const queue = [payload];
    const providerStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ACCEPTED',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED_PRE_CALL',
      'PROVIDER_REJECTED_PAYMENT',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_MALFORMED',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_PRESENT_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'STALE_RUNNING_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_CHUNK',
      'PROVIDER_REQUEST_SENT_NO_RESPONSE'
    ]);
    const providerIssueKinds = new Set([
      'PAYMENT_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW',
      'BANK_TRANSFER_PROVIDER_REVIEW_REQUIRED'
    ]);
    const weakNonProviderStatuses = new Set([
      'BLOCKED_FUNDS',
      'NO_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED',
      'CLAIMED_NOT_PROVIDER_CALLED_YET',
      'FUNDS_CHECK_FAILED',
      'FUNDING_ACCOUNT_REF_MISSING',
      'SCHEDULED_BATCH_MISSING_FUNDING_ACCOUNT_REF'
    ]);
    const asBool = (value) => {
      if (value === true) return true;
      if (value === false) return false;
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (['true', 't', '1', 'yes', 'y', 'on'].includes(text)) return true;
      if (['false', 'f', '0', 'no', 'n', 'off'].includes(text)) return false;
      return false;
    };
    while (queue.length) {
      const value = queue.shift();
      if (value == null) continue;
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (const item of value) queue.push(item);
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof value === 'string') {
        const text = value.trim();
        if (!text) continue;
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
          if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') queue.push(parsed);
        } catch {}
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);

      const providerStatus = String(
        value.provider_submission_status ||
        value.providerSubmissionStatus ||
        value.outcome_code ||
        value.outcomeCode ||
        value.provider_state ||
        value.providerState ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();
      const reviewReason = String(
        value.review_reason_code ||
        value.reviewReasonCode ||
        value.provider_submit_review_reason_code ||
        value.providerSubmitReviewReasonCode ||
        value.claim_blocker_code ||
        value.claimBlockerCode ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();
      const issueKind = String(
        value.issueKindForDisplay ||
        value.issue_kind_for_display ||
        value.issue_kind ||
        value.issueKind ||
        value.kind ||
        value.type ||
        value.alert_kind ||
        value.alertKind ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();
      const railState = String(value.rail_state || value.railState || '').trim().toUpperCase();

      if (providerStatuses.has(providerStatus) || providerStatuses.has(reviewReason) || providerIssueKinds.has(issueKind)) return true;
      if (providerStatus && providerStatus.startsWith('PROVIDER_') && !weakNonProviderStatuses.has(providerStatus)) return true;
      if (reviewReason && reviewReason.startsWith('PROVIDER_') && !weakNonProviderStatuses.has(reviewReason)) return true;
      if (asBool(value.provider_request_sent) || asBool(value.providerRequestSent) || asBool(value.provider_request_sent_confirmed) || asBool(value.providerRequestSentConfirmed)) return true;
      if (asBool(value.provider_response_present) || asBool(value.providerResponsePresent) || asBool(value.provider_response_received) || asBool(value.providerResponseReceived)) return true;
      if (asBool(value.provider_submission_attempted) || asBool(value.providerSubmissionAttempted) || asBool(value.provider_called) || asBool(value.providerCalled)) return true;
      if (asBool(value.provider_submission_rejected) || asBool(value.providerSubmissionRejected) || asBool(value.provider_submission_unknown) || asBool(value.providerSubmissionUnknown)) return true;
      if (String(value.provider_error_code || value.providerErrorCode || '').trim()) return true;
      if (String(value.provider_error_message_redacted || value.providerErrorMessageRedacted || value.provider_error_message || value.providerErrorMessage || '').trim()) return true;
      if (String(value.provider_http_status || value.providerHttpStatus || '').trim()) return true;
      if (Array.isArray(value.transfer_provider_outcomes) && value.transfer_provider_outcomes.length > 0) return true;
      if (Array.isArray(value.transferProviderOutcomes) && value.transferProviderOutcomes.length > 0) return true;
      if (['REJECTED', 'DECLINED'].includes(railState) && (String(value.provider || value.rail_provider || value.railProvider || '').trim() || String(value.provider_response || value.providerResponse || '').trim())) return true;

      for (const nested of Object.values(value)) {
        if (nested && typeof nested === 'object') queue.push(nested);
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  const hydratePaymentIssueState = () => {
    const previous = (child.correction && typeof child.correction === 'object') ? child.correction : {};

    if (typeof normaliseBankingPaymentIssueState !== 'function') {
      throw new Error('Missing dependency: normaliseBankingPaymentIssueState');
    }
    if (typeof buildBankingPaymentIssueRows !== 'function') {
      throw new Error('Missing dependency: buildBankingPaymentIssueRows');
    }

    const toText = (value) => String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    const asSet = (value) => {
      try {
        if (value instanceof Set) return new Set(Array.from(value).map((v) => toText(v)).filter(Boolean));
        if (Array.isArray(value)) return new Set(value.map((v) => toText(v)).filter(Boolean));
      } catch {}
      return new Set();
    };
    const addValuesToSet = (target, values) => {
      try {
        const list = Array.isArray(values) ? values : [values];
        for (const value of list) {
          const s = toText(value);
          if (s) target.add(s);
        }
      } catch {}
    };
    const rowKeyOf = (row) => toText(row && (row.rowKey || row.row_key));
    const lastMode = toText(previous.lastSelectionMode || previous.selectionScopeMode).toLowerCase();
    const isReviewMode = lastMode === 'review' || lastMode === 'reviewonly' || lastMode === 'review_only';
    const canKeepSelected = (row) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return false;
      if (isReviewMode) return row.selectableForReview === true || row.selectable === true;
      return row.selectableForApply === true || row.selectable === true;
    };

    const previousSelectedRowKeys = asSet(previous.selectedRowKeys);
    const previousRows = Array.isArray(previous.rows) ? previous.rows : [];
    for (const row of previousRows) {
      if (row && row.selected === true) {
        const key = rowKeyOf(row);
        if (key) previousSelectedRowKeys.add(key);
      }
    }

     const previousManualPaidActionMode = false;
    const currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState = createChildSectionState('current_payment_status', child.sections?.current_payment_status || {});
    const currentPaymentStatusSectionMeta = {
      current_payment_status_loading: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.loading === true,
      currentPaymentStatusLoading: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.loading === true,
      current_payment_status_loaded: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.loaded === true,
      currentPaymentStatusLoaded: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.loaded === true,
      current_payment_status_error: String(currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.error || ''),
      currentPaymentStatusError: String(currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.error || ''),
      current_payment_status_failed_request_key: String(currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.failed_request_key || ''),
      currentPaymentStatusFailedRequestKey: String(currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState.failed_request_key || ''),
      current_payment_status_section: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState,
      currentPaymentStatusSection: currentPaymentStatusSectionForIssueState
    };
    const paymentIssuePayload = buildPaymentIssueEvidencePayload();
    const providerSubmitEvidencePresent = paymentIssuePayloadHasProviderSubmitEvidence(paymentIssuePayload);
    const loadedPaymentIssueEvidenceSectionsPresent = paymentIssuePayloadHasLoadedEvidenceSections(paymentIssuePayload);

    if (isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data) && !providerSubmitEvidencePresent && !loadedPaymentIssueEvidenceSectionsPresent) {
      child.correction = {
        ...previous,
        ...currentPaymentStatusSectionMeta,
        issueState: {
          ...(previous.issueState && typeof previous.issueState === 'object' ? previous.issueState : {}),
          manualPaidActionMode: previousManualPaidActionMode,
          bootstrap_only: true,
          evidence_loaded: false,
          requiresUserAction: false,
          hasActionableIssueRows: false,
          hasSelectableRows: false,
          headline: 'Payment status'
        },
        rows: [],
        manualPaidActionMode: previousManualPaidActionMode,
        manualPaidActionAvailable: false,
        selectedRowKeys: new Set(),
        selectedPayBatchCandidateIds: new Set(),
        selectedTransferIds: new Set(),
        selectedPayBatchItemIds: new Set(),
        selectedUmbrellaGroup: null,
        selectedTransferGroupKey: '',
        selectionScopeMode: '',
        lastSelectionMode: '',
        sortKey: toText(previous.sortKey) || 'candidate_name',
        sortDir: String(previous.sortDir || 'asc').toLowerCase() === 'desc' ? 'desc' : 'asc',
        filterKey: toText(previous.filterKey) || 'action_needed',
        selection: null,
        plan: null,
        planSelectionSignature: '',
        planError: '',
        request: previous.request || null,
        progress: previous.progress || null,
        acceptedResolutionJson: null,
        acceptedResolutionSummary: null,
        reauthToken: toText(previous.reauthToken),
        reauthVerifiedAtUtc: toText(previous.reauthVerifiedAtUtc),
        lastStatusLoadedAtUtc: toText(previous.lastStatusLoadedAtUtc)
      };
      return;
    }

    const nextIssueStateRaw = normaliseBankingPaymentIssueState(paymentIssuePayload, { manualPaidActionMode: previousManualPaidActionMode, correctionState: previous });
    const nextIssueState = (nextIssueStateRaw && typeof nextIssueStateRaw === 'object') ? { ...nextIssueStateRaw } : {};
    nextIssueState.manualPaidActionMode = previousManualPaidActionMode;
    if (previousManualPaidActionMode) {
      nextIssueState.headline = 'Current Payment Status';
      nextIssueState.summaryText = 'Use Current Payment Status row actions for supported payments.';
      nextIssueState.statusText = 'Current Payment Status';
      nextIssueState.issueKindForDisplay = 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION';
      nextIssueState.requiresUserAction = false;
      nextIssueState.navAlert = false;
    }
    let nextRows = patchPaymentIssueRowsForManualMode(
      buildBankingPaymentIssueRows(paymentIssuePayload, { manualPaidActionMode: previousManualPaidActionMode, correctionState: previous }),
      previousManualPaidActionMode
    );
    const rowLooksProviderSubmitReview = (row) => {
      const r = (row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row)) ? row : {};
      const kind = String(r.issueKindForDisplay || r.issue_kind_for_display || r.issue_kind || r.issueType || r.issue_type || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const status = String(r.provider_submission_status || r.providerSubmissionStatus || r.backendClassification || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      return kind === 'PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW' || kind === 'PAYMENT_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_REVIEW' || status.startsWith('PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_') || !!(r.providerSubmitDiagnostic || r.provider_submit_diagnostic);
    };
    const rowLooksDraftOnlyRemoval = (row) => {
      const r = (row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row)) ? row : {};
      const kind = String(r.issueKindForDisplay || r.issue_kind_for_display || r.issue_kind || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      return r.draftOnlyRemoval === true || r.draft_only_removal === true || kind === 'DRAFT_REMOVE';
    };
    if (providerSubmitEvidencePresent && nextRows.length > 0 && nextRows.every((row) => rowLooksDraftOnlyRemoval(row))) {
      const previousProviderRows = previousRows.filter((row) => rowLooksProviderSubmitReview(row));
      if (previousProviderRows.length) {
        nextRows = patchPaymentIssueRowsForManualMode(previousProviderRows.map((row) => ({ ...row, selected: false })), previousManualPaidActionMode);
      }
    }
    nextIssueState.manualPaidActionAvailable = nextIssueState.manualPaidActionAvailable === true || nextRows.some((row) => row && row.manualActionAvailable === true);
    nextIssueState.hasActionableIssueRows = nextIssueState.hasActionableIssueRows === true || nextRows.some((row) => row && row.selectableForApply === true && String(row.issueKindForDisplay || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION');
    nextIssueState.hasSelectableRows = nextIssueState.hasSelectableRows === true || nextRows.some((row) => row && (row.selectableForApply === true || row.selectableForReview === true));
    const nextSelectedRowKeys = new Set();
    const nextSelectedPayBatchCandidateIds = new Set();
    const nextSelectedTransferIds = new Set();
    const nextSelectedPayBatchItemIds = new Set();

    for (const row of nextRows) {
      const key = rowKeyOf(row);
      const keepSelected = !!(key && previousSelectedRowKeys.has(key) && canKeepSelected(row));
      row.selected = keepSelected;
      if (!keepSelected) continue;

      nextSelectedRowKeys.add(key);
      addValuesToSet(nextSelectedPayBatchCandidateIds, row.payBatchCandidateIds || row.pay_batch_candidate_ids || row.payBatchCandidateId || row.pay_batch_candidate_id);
      addValuesToSet(nextSelectedTransferIds, row.payBankTransferIds || row.pay_bank_transfer_ids || row.payBankTransferId || row.pay_bank_transfer_id);
      addValuesToSet(nextSelectedPayBatchItemIds, row.payBatchItemIds || row.pay_batch_item_ids || row.payBatchItemId || row.pay_batch_item_id);
    }

    const lostSelection = previousSelectedRowKeys.size > 0 && nextSelectedRowKeys.size !== previousSelectedRowKeys.size;
    const noSelection = nextSelectedRowKeys.size <= 0;

    child.correction = {
      ...previous,
      ...currentPaymentStatusSectionMeta,
      issueState: nextIssueState,
      rows: nextRows,
      manualPaidActionMode: previousManualPaidActionMode,
      manualPaidActionAvailable: nextIssueState.manualPaidActionAvailable === true,
      selectedRowKeys: nextSelectedRowKeys,
      selectedPayBatchCandidateIds: nextSelectedPayBatchCandidateIds,
      selectedTransferIds: nextSelectedTransferIds,
      selectedPayBatchItemIds: nextSelectedPayBatchItemIds,
      selectedUmbrellaGroup: noSelection ? null : (previous.selectedUmbrellaGroup ?? null),
      selectedTransferGroupKey: noSelection ? '' : toText(previous.selectedTransferGroupKey),
      selectionScopeMode: noSelection ? '' : toText(previous.selectionScopeMode),
      lastSelectionMode: noSelection ? '' : toText(previous.lastSelectionMode),
      sortKey: toText(previous.sortKey) || 'candidate_name',
      sortDir: String(previous.sortDir || 'asc').toLowerCase() === 'desc' ? 'desc' : 'asc',
      filterKey: toText(previous.filterKey) || 'action_needed',
      selection: previous.selection || null,
      plan: previous.plan || null,
      planSelectionSignature: toText(previous.planSelectionSignature),
      planError: toText(previous.planError),
      request: previous.request || null,
      progress: previous.progress || null,
      acceptedResolutionJson: previous.acceptedResolutionJson || null,
      acceptedResolutionSummary: previous.acceptedResolutionSummary || null,
      reauthToken: toText(previous.reauthToken),
      reauthVerifiedAtUtc: toText(previous.reauthVerifiedAtUtc),
      lastStatusLoadedAtUtc: toText(previous.lastStatusLoadedAtUtc)
    };

    if (lostSelection || noSelection) {
      child.correction.selection = null;
      child.correction.plan = null;
      child.correction.planSelectionSignature = '';
      child.correction.planError = '';
      child.correction.acceptedResolutionJson = null;
      child.correction.acceptedResolutionSummary = null;
      child.correction.selectedUmbrellaGroup = null;
      child.correction.selectedTransferGroupKey = '';
      child.correction.selectionScopeMode = '';
      try {
        if (typeof invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan === 'function') invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan(child.correction);
      } catch {}
    } else if (child.correction.selection && typeof getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature === 'function') {
      try {
        const existingSignature = toText(child.correction.planSelectionSignature);
        const currentSignature = toText(getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature(child.correction.selection));
        if (existingSignature && currentSignature && existingSignature !== currentSignature) {
          child.correction.selection = null;
          child.correction.plan = null;
          child.correction.planSelectionSignature = '';
          child.correction.planError = '';
          child.correction.acceptedResolutionJson = null;
          child.correction.acceptedResolutionSummary = null;
        }
      } catch {}
    }
  };

const normaliseChildFriendlyError = (errorValue, fallbackCode = 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', context = {}) => {
    const contextObj = (context && typeof context === 'object' && !Array.isArray(context)) ? context : {};
    const isObj = (value) => !!value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
    const parseObject = (value) => {
      if (isObj(value)) return value;
      const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
      if (!text) return null;
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        if (isObj(parsed)) return parsed;
      } catch {}
      const start = text.indexOf('{');
      const end = text.lastIndexOf('}');
      if (start >= 0 && end > start) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(text.slice(start, end + 1));
          if (isObj(parsed)) return parsed;
        } catch {}
      }
      return null;
    };
    const deriveBackendPayload = () => {
      if (isObj(contextObj.backendPayload)) return contextObj.backendPayload;
      if (isObj(contextObj.payload)) return contextObj.payload;
      if (isObj(contextObj.json)) return contextObj.json;
      if (isObj(errorValue?.backendPayload)) return errorValue.backendPayload;
      if (isObj(errorValue?.payload)) return errorValue.payload;
      if (isObj(errorValue?.json)) return errorValue.json;
      const parsedContextPayload = parseObject(contextObj.backendPayload) || parseObject(contextObj.payload) || parseObject(contextObj.body) || parseObject(contextObj.message) || parseObject(contextObj.error);
      if (parsedContextPayload) return parsedContextPayload;
      const parsedErrorPayload = parseObject(errorValue?.body) || parseObject(errorValue?.message) || parseObject(errorValue?.error) || parseObject(errorValue);
      if (parsedErrorPayload) return parsedErrorPayload;
      return null;
    };
    try {
      if (typeof bankingNormalizeApiError === 'function') {
        const backendPayload = deriveBackendPayload();
        const normaliseSource = backendPayload || errorValue;
        const normalized = bankingNormalizeApiError(normaliseSource, backendPayload || normaliseSource, {
          action: contextObj.action || 'banking_child_action',
          batchId: id,
          scope: contextObj.scope || null,
          userInitiated: contextObj.userInitiated === true,
          silent: contextObj.silent === true,
          fallbackCode,
          payload: backendPayload || undefined,
          backendPayload: backendPayload || undefined,
          original_error: errorValue || contextObj.technicalError || null,
          technical_message: errorValue?.message || contextObj.technical_message || contextObj.technicalMessage || ''
        });
        if (normalized && typeof normalized === 'object') {
          const normalizedCode = String(normalized.error_code || normalized.code || fallbackCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
          const normalizedAction = String(contextObj.action || '').trim().toUpperCase();
          const fallbackCodeForContext = String(fallbackCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
          const isPreviewRefreshAction = normalizedAction === 'PREVIEW' || normalizedAction === 'PREVIEW_REFRESH' || normalizedAction === 'POST_ACTION_PREVIEW_REFRESH' || fallbackCodeForContext === 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED';
          const isPollAction = normalizedAction === 'POLL_BANK_STATUS' || normalizedAction === 'CHECK_BANK_STATUS' || normalizedAction === 'BANKING_PAY_POLL' || normalizedAction === 'POLL';
          if (normalizedCode === 'BATCH_STALE') {
            const staleTitle = isPreviewRefreshAction ? 'Payment preview needs refreshing' : 'Payment batch has changed';
            const staleMessage = (normalizedAction === 'RETRY_BLOCKED_FUNDS' || normalizedAction === 'RETRY_UNSENT_PAYMENTS')
              ? 'Retry unsent payments could not start because this batch is no longer up to date. Refresh the batch and review the latest Current Payment Status.'
              : (isPreviewRefreshAction
                  ? 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.'
                  : 'This payment batch has changed. Refresh the batch, review the latest details, then try again.');
            return {
              ...normalized,
              ok: false,
              error_code: 'BATCH_STALE',
              code: 'BATCH_STALE',
              title: staleTitle,
              message: staleMessage,
              error: staleMessage,
              user_message: staleMessage,
              user_action: normalized.user_action || (isPreviewRefreshAction ? 'REFRESH_PREVIEW' : 'REFRESH_BATCH'),
              confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || 'OK',
              severity: normalized.severity || 'warning',
              show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
              http_status: 409,
              status_code: 409,
              friendly_error: {
                ...(normalized.friendly_error && typeof normalized.friendly_error === 'object' && !Array.isArray(normalized.friendly_error) ? normalized.friendly_error : {}),
                title: staleTitle,
                message: staleMessage,
                error_code: 'BATCH_STALE',
                user_action: normalized.friendly_error?.user_action || normalized.user_action || (isPreviewRefreshAction ? 'REFRESH_PREVIEW' : 'REFRESH_BATCH'),
                confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || normalized.friendly_error?.confirm_label || 'OK',
                severity: normalized.severity || normalized.friendly_error?.severity || 'warning',
                show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true
              },
              backendPayload: backendPayload || normalized.backendPayload || null
            };
          }
          if (isPollAction || normalizedCode === 'BANKING_PAY_POLL_FAILED' || normalizedCode === 'POLL_NOT_SUPPORTED_FOR_THIS_RAIL') {
            const setupCodes = new Set(['MISSING_RAIL_PROVIDER', 'RAIL_PROVIDER_REQUIRED', 'RAIL_NOT_CONFIGURED', 'UNKNOWN_RAIL_PROVIDER', 'POLL_NOT_SUPPORTED_FOR_THIS_RAIL', 'RAIL_ENV_MISMATCH']);
            const pollIsSetup = setupCodes.has(normalizedCode);
            const pollTitle = pollIsSetup ? 'Banking setup needs attention' : 'Payment status could not be refreshed';
            const pollMessage = pollIsSetup
              ? 'CloudTMS could not refresh this payment because the banking setup is incomplete or unavailable. Review Banking settings and try again.'
              : 'CloudTMS could not refresh this payment status. Refresh the batch and try again.';
            const pollCode = normalizedCode || 'BANKING_PAY_POLL_FAILED';
            return {
              ...normalized,
              ok: false,
              error_code: pollCode,
              code: pollCode,
              title: pollTitle,
              message: pollMessage,
              error: pollMessage,
              user_message: pollMessage,
              confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || 'OK',
              severity: normalized.severity || 'error',
              show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
              friendly_error: {
                ...(normalized.friendly_error && typeof normalized.friendly_error === 'object' && !Array.isArray(normalized.friendly_error) ? normalized.friendly_error : {}),
                title: pollTitle,
                message: pollMessage,
                error_code: pollCode,
                confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || normalized.friendly_error?.confirm_label || 'OK',
                severity: normalized.severity || normalized.friendly_error?.severity || 'error',
                show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true
              },
              backendPayload: backendPayload || normalized.backendPayload || null
            };
          }
          if (isPreviewRefreshAction || normalizedCode === 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED') {
            const previewTitle = 'Payment preview needs refreshing';
            const previewMessage = 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.';
            const previewCode = normalizedCode || 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED';
            return {
              ...normalized,
              ok: false,
              error_code: previewCode,
              code: previewCode,
              title: previewTitle,
              message: previewMessage,
              error: previewMessage,
              user_message: previewMessage,
              user_action: normalized.user_action || 'REFRESH_PREVIEW',
              confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || 'OK',
              severity: normalized.severity || 'warning',
              show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
              friendly_error: {
                ...(normalized.friendly_error && typeof normalized.friendly_error === 'object' && !Array.isArray(normalized.friendly_error) ? normalized.friendly_error : {}),
                title: previewTitle,
                message: previewMessage,
                error_code: previewCode,
                user_action: normalized.friendly_error?.user_action || normalized.user_action || 'REFRESH_PREVIEW',
                confirm_label: normalized.confirm_label || normalized.friendly_error?.confirm_label || 'OK',
                severity: normalized.severity || normalized.friendly_error?.severity || 'warning',
                show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true
              },
              backendPayload: backendPayload || normalized.backendPayload || null
            };
          }
          return {
            ...normalized,
            backendPayload: backendPayload || normalized.backendPayload || null
          };
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    const fallbackCodeUpper = String(fallbackCode || 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED').trim().toUpperCase() || 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED';
    const fallbackAction = String(contextObj.action || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const fallbackIsPreviewRefreshAction = fallbackAction === 'PREVIEW' || fallbackAction === 'PREVIEW_REFRESH' || fallbackAction === 'POST_ACTION_PREVIEW_REFRESH' || fallbackCodeUpper === 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED';
    const fallbackIsPollAction = fallbackAction === 'POLL_BANK_STATUS' || fallbackAction === 'CHECK_BANK_STATUS' || fallbackAction === 'BANKING_PAY_POLL' || fallbackAction === 'POLL' || fallbackCodeUpper === 'BANKING_PAY_POLL_FAILED';
    if (fallbackCodeUpper === 'BATCH_STALE') {
      const staleTitle = fallbackIsPreviewRefreshAction ? 'Payment preview needs refreshing' : 'Payment batch has changed';
      const staleMessage = fallbackIsPreviewRefreshAction
        ? 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.'
        : 'This payment batch has changed. Refresh the batch, review the latest details, then try again.';
      return {
        ok: false,
        error_code: 'BATCH_STALE',
        code: 'BATCH_STALE',
        title: staleTitle,
        message: staleMessage,
        error: staleMessage,
        user_message: staleMessage,
        confirm_label: 'OK',
        severity: 'warning',
        show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
        http_status: 409,
        status_code: 409
      };
    }

    if (fallbackIsPollAction) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error_code: fallbackCodeUpper,
        code: fallbackCodeUpper,
        title: 'Payment status could not be refreshed',
        message: 'CloudTMS could not refresh this payment status. Refresh the batch and try again.',
        error: 'CloudTMS could not refresh this payment status. Refresh the batch and try again.',
        user_message: 'CloudTMS could not refresh this payment status. Refresh the batch and try again.',
        confirm_label: 'OK',
        severity: 'error',
        show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
        http_status: 500,
        status_code: 500
      };
    }

    if (fallbackIsPreviewRefreshAction) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error_code: fallbackCodeUpper,
        code: fallbackCodeUpper,
        title: 'Payment preview needs refreshing',
        message: 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.',
        error: 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.',
        user_message: 'Payment details have changed. Refresh Banking Pay preview, review the latest details, then try again.',
        confirm_label: 'OK',
        severity: 'warning',
        show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
        http_status: 500,
        status_code: 500
      };
    }

    return {
      ok: false,
      error_code: fallbackCodeUpper,
      code: fallbackCodeUpper,
      title: 'Banking action failed',
      message: 'CloudTMS could not complete this Banking action. Please refresh and try again.',
      error: 'CloudTMS could not complete this Banking action. Please refresh and try again.',
      user_message: 'CloudTMS could not complete this Banking action. Please refresh and try again.',
      confirm_label: 'OK',
      severity: 'error',
      show_modal: contextObj.silent === true ? false : contextObj.userInitiated === true,
      http_status: 500,
      status_code: 500
    };
  };


  const showChildFriendlyNotice = async ({ title, message, confirmLabel = 'OK', kind = 'import-summary-banking-child-friendly-notice', confirmClass = 'btn btn-primary' } = {}) => {
    const titleText = String(title || 'Banking').trim() || 'Banking';
    const messageText = String(message || '').trim() || 'The Banking action has completed.';
    try {
      if (typeof openUiConfirmModal === 'function') {
        await openUiConfirmModal({
          kind,
          title: titleText,
          message: messageText,
          confirm_label: String(confirmLabel || 'OK').trim() || 'OK',
          hide_cancel: true,
          confirm_class: String(confirmClass || 'btn btn-primary').trim() || 'btn btn-primary'
        });
        return;
      }
    } catch {}
    try { toast(messageText); } catch {}
  };

  const reportChildFriendlyError = async (errorValue, fallbackCode = 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', context = {}) => {
    const contextObj = (context && typeof context === 'object' && !Array.isArray(context)) ? context : {};
    const friendly = normaliseChildFriendlyError(errorValue, fallbackCode, contextObj);
    const message = String(friendly.user_message || friendly.message || friendly.error || 'CloudTMS could not complete this Banking action. Please refresh and try again.').trim();
    child.error = message;
    child.friendlyError = friendly;

    if (typeof bankingHandleApiError === 'function') {
      try {
        bankingHandleApiError({
          message,
          status: friendly.http_status || friendly.status_code,
          json: friendly,
          body: (() => { try { return JSON.stringify(friendly); } catch { return message; } })()
        }, {
          action: contextObj.action || 'banking_child_action',
          scope: contextObj.scope || null,
          batchId: id,
          errorPath: ['pay', 'child', 'error'],
          userInitiated: contextObj.userInitiated === true,
          silent: contextObj.silent === true
        });
        return friendly;
      } catch {}
    }

    if (contextObj.userInitiated === true && contextObj.silent !== true && friendly.show_modal !== false) {
      await showChildFriendlyNotice({
        title: friendly.title || 'Banking action failed',
        message,
        confirmLabel: friendly.confirm_label || 'OK',
        kind: 'import-summary-banking-child-friendly-error',
        confirmClass: friendly.severity === 'critical' || friendly.severity === 'error' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn btn-primary'
      });
    } else if (contextObj.silent !== true) {
      try { toast(message); } catch {}
    }

    return friendly;
  };

  const isBlockedFundsExecutionResult = (value) => {
    const result = (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) ? value : {};
    const batchGet = (result.batch_get && typeof result.batch_get === 'object' && !Array.isArray(result.batch_get)) ? result.batch_get : {};
    const batchObj = (batchGet.batch && typeof batchGet.batch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(batchGet.batch)) ? batchGet.batch : {};
    return result.blocked_funds === true
      || String(result.post_execution_status || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'BLOCKED_FUNDS'
      || String(result.status || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'BLOCKED_FUNDS'
      || String(batchGet.status || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'BLOCKED_FUNDS'
      || String(batchObj.status || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'BLOCKED_FUNDS';
  };

  const isScheduledExecutionResult = (value) => {
    const result = (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) ? value : {};
    return result.scheduled === true
      || String(result.title || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'payment scheduled'
      || (
        String(result.execution_mode || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'STANDARD_BANK'
        && result.submitted_to_bank === false
        && result.rail_execution_attempted === false
        && String(result.scheduled_at_utc || '').trim()
      );
  };

  const isUncertainImmediateExecutionResult = (value) => {
    const result = (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) ? value : {};
    const code = String(result.error_code || result.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const status = String(result.status || result.post_execution_status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const railAttempted = result.rail_execution_attempted === true || result.standardImmediateRailExecutionAttempted === true;
    const submittedToBank = result.submitted_to_bank === true || ['SUBMITTED', 'COMMITTED'].includes(status);

    if (result.uncertain_execution_state === true) return true;
    if (result.review_needed === true && result.terminal === true) return true;
    if (code === 'STANDARD_BANK_IMMEDIATE_SAFE_STATE_NOT_RECORDED') return true;
    if (railAttempted && submittedToBank !== true && !['SUBMITTED', 'COMMITTED', 'BLOCKED_FUNDS'].includes(status)) return true;
    return false;
  };

  const isFriendlyFailedExecutionResult = (value) => {
    const result = (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) ? value : {};
    if (!result || !Object.keys(result).length) return false;
    if (result.ok === false) return true;
    const code = String(result.error_code || result.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return !!(code && code !== 'BLOCKED_FUNDS');
  };

  const executionResultProviderEvidencePresent = (value) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    const queue = [value];
    const providerStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ACCEPTED',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED_PRE_CALL'
    ]);
    const weakNonProviderCodes = new Set([
      'BLOCKED_FUNDS',
      'NO_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED',
      'CLAIMED_NOT_PROVIDER_CALLED_YET',
      'FUNDS_CHECK_FAILED',
      'FUNDING_ACCOUNT_REF_MISSING',
      'SCHEDULED_BATCH_MISSING_FUNDING_ACCOUNT_REF'
    ]);
    const readBool = (raw) => {
      if (raw === true) return true;
      if (raw === false) return false;
      const text = String(raw == null ? '' : raw).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (['true', 't', '1', 'yes', 'y', 'on'].includes(text)) return true;
      if (['false', 'f', '0', 'no', 'n', 'off'].includes(text)) return false;
      return false;
    };
    const readNum = (raw) => {
      const n = Number(raw);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
    };

    while (queue.length) {
      const node = queue.shift();
      if (node == null) continue;
      if (Array.isArray(node)) {
        node.forEach((entry) => queue.push(entry));
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof node === 'string') {
        const text = node.trim();
        if (!text) continue;
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
          if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') queue.push(parsed);
        } catch {}
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof node !== 'object' || seen.has(node)) continue;
      seen.add(node);

      const providerStatus = String(
        node.provider_submission_status ||
        node.providerSubmissionStatus ||
        node.outcome_code ||
        node.outcomeCode ||
        node.provider_state ||
        node.providerState ||
        node.provider_status ||
        node.providerStatus ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();
      const reviewReason = String(
        node.review_reason_code ||
        node.reviewReasonCode ||
        node.provider_submit_review_reason_code ||
        node.providerSubmitReviewReasonCode ||
        node.claim_blocker_code ||
        node.claimBlockerCode ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();

      if (providerStatuses.has(providerStatus) || providerStatuses.has(reviewReason)) return true;
      if (providerStatus && providerStatus.startsWith('PROVIDER_') && !weakNonProviderCodes.has(providerStatus)) return true;
      if (reviewReason && reviewReason.startsWith('PROVIDER_') && !weakNonProviderCodes.has(reviewReason)) return true;
      if (readBool(node.provider_request_sent) || readBool(node.providerRequestSent)) return true;
      if (readBool(node.provider_response_present) || readBool(node.providerResponsePresent) || readBool(node.provider_response_received) || readBool(node.providerResponseReceived)) return true;
      if (readBool(node.provider_submission_attempted) || readBool(node.providerSubmissionAttempted) || readBool(node.provider_called) || readBool(node.providerCalled)) return true;
      if (String(node.provider_error_code || node.providerErrorCode || '').trim()) return true;
      if (String(node.provider_error_message_redacted || node.providerErrorMessageRedacted || node.provider_error_message || node.providerErrorMessage || '').trim()) return true;
      if (String(node.provider_http_status || node.providerHttpStatus || '').trim()) return true;

      const outcomeCounts = (node.payment_execution_outcome_counts && typeof node.payment_execution_outcome_counts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(node.payment_execution_outcome_counts))
        ? node.payment_execution_outcome_counts
        : ((node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts && typeof node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts)) ? node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts : null);
      if (outcomeCounts) {
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_request_sent_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerRequestSentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_response_present_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerResponsePresentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_response_received_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerResponseReceivedCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_submitted_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerSubmittedCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_accepted_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerAcceptedCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_rejected_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerRejectedCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.rejected_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.rejectedPaymentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.failed_or_needs_review_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.failedOrNeedsReviewPaymentCount) > 0 && (
          readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_request_sent_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerRequestSentCount) > 0 ||
          readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_response_present_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerResponsePresentCount) > 0 ||
          readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_rejected_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerRejectedCount) > 0
        )) return true;
      }

      for (const key of [
        'json', 'payload', 'backendPayload', 'backend_payload',
        'friendly_error', 'friendlyError', 'response_json', 'responseJson',
        'body', 'raw_response_text', 'rawResponseText',
        'provider_submit_diagnostic', 'providerSubmitDiagnostic', 'provider_submit_claim_diagnostic', 'providerSubmitClaimDiagnostic',
        'payment_execution_review', 'paymentExecutionReview', 'provider_submission', 'providerSubmission',
        'operation_result', 'operationResult', 'operation_error', 'operationError', 'result', 'error',
        'progress', 'payment_issue_summary', 'paymentIssueSummary'
      ]) {
        if (node[key] && (typeof node[key] === 'object' || typeof node[key] === 'string')) queue.push(node[key]);
      }
      for (const key of [
        'submitted', 'errors', 'transfer_provider_outcomes', 'transferProviderOutcomes',
        'provider_outcomes', 'providerOutcomes', 'transfers', 'payment_issues', 'paymentIssues'
      ]) {
        if (Array.isArray(node[key])) queue.push(node[key]);
      }
    }

    return false;
  };

  const executionResultProviderReviewEvidencePresent = (value) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    const queue = [value];
    const reviewStatuses = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED',
      'UNKNOWN_PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_OUTCOME',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_ATTEMPTED_NO_EXTERNAL_ID'
    ]);
    const reviewReasons = new Set([
      'PROVIDER_REJECTED_PAYMENT',
      'PROVIDER_REQUEST_SENT_NO_RESPONSE',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_MALFORMED',
      'PROVIDER_RESPONSE_PRESENT_NO_EXTERNAL_ID',
      'STALE_RUNNING_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_CHUNK'
    ]);
    const readBool = (raw) => {
      if (raw === true) return true;
      if (raw === false) return false;
      const text = String(raw == null ? '' : raw).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (['true', 't', '1', 'yes', 'y', 'on'].includes(text)) return true;
      if (['false', 'f', '0', 'no', 'n', 'off'].includes(text)) return false;
      return false;
    };
    const readNum = (raw) => {
      const n = Number(raw);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
    };

    while (queue.length) {
      const node = queue.shift();
      if (node == null) continue;
      if (Array.isArray(node)) {
        node.forEach((entry) => queue.push(entry));
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof node === 'string') {
        const text = node.trim();
        if (!text) continue;
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
          if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') queue.push(parsed);
        } catch {}
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof node !== 'object' || seen.has(node)) continue;
      seen.add(node);

      const providerStatus = String(
        node.provider_submission_status ||
        node.providerSubmissionStatus ||
        node.outcome_code ||
        node.outcomeCode ||
        node.provider_state ||
        node.providerState ||
        node.provider_status ||
        node.providerStatus ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();
      const reviewReason = String(
        node.review_reason_code ||
        node.reviewReasonCode ||
        node.provider_submit_review_reason_code ||
        node.providerSubmitReviewReasonCode ||
        node.claim_blocker_code ||
        node.claimBlockerCode ||
        ''
      ).trim().toUpperCase();

      if (reviewStatuses.has(providerStatus) || reviewReasons.has(reviewReason)) return true;
      if (readBool(node.provider_submission_rejected) || readBool(node.providerSubmissionRejected) || readBool(node.provider_submission_unknown) || readBool(node.providerSubmissionUnknown)) return true;
      if (String(node.provider_error_code || node.providerErrorCode || '').trim()) return true;
      if (String(node.provider_error_message_redacted || node.providerErrorMessageRedacted || node.provider_error_message || node.providerErrorMessage || '').trim()) return true;

      const outcomeCounts = (node.payment_execution_outcome_counts && typeof node.payment_execution_outcome_counts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(node.payment_execution_outcome_counts))
        ? node.payment_execution_outcome_counts
        : ((node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts && typeof node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts === 'object' && !Array.isArray(node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts)) ? node.paymentExecutionOutcomeCounts : null);
      if (outcomeCounts) {
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.provider_rejected_count ?? outcomeCounts.providerRejectedCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.rejected_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.rejectedPaymentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.failed_or_needs_review_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.failedOrNeedsReviewPaymentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.unknown_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.unknownPaymentCount) > 0) return true;
        if (readNum(outcomeCounts.needs_review_payment_count ?? outcomeCounts.needsReviewPaymentCount) > 0) return true;
      }

      for (const key of [
        'json', 'payload', 'backendPayload', 'backend_payload',
        'friendly_error', 'friendlyError', 'response_json', 'responseJson',
        'body', 'raw_response_text', 'rawResponseText',
        'provider_submit_diagnostic', 'providerSubmitDiagnostic', 'provider_submit_claim_diagnostic', 'providerSubmitClaimDiagnostic',
        'payment_execution_review', 'paymentExecutionReview', 'provider_submission', 'providerSubmission',
        'operation_result', 'operationResult', 'operation_error', 'operationError', 'result', 'error',
        'progress', 'payment_issue_summary', 'paymentIssueSummary'
      ]) {
        if (node[key] && (typeof node[key] === 'object' || typeof node[key] === 'string')) queue.push(node[key]);
      }
      for (const key of [
