                    }
                  }
                  return false;
                })();

                const providerAccepted = providerSubmitHasAcceptanceEvidence(providerSubmitResolutionContext);
                const providerTransactionEvidencePresent = providerAccepted === true || rowHasProviderTransactionEvidence(row, diagnostic);
                const alreadyReleasedOrInProgress = rowLooksAlreadyReleasedOrInProgress(row, diagnostic);
                const rowSuccessfulOrAccepted = rowLooksAcceptedOrSuccessful(row, diagnostic);
                const providerRejectedStatus = providerStatus === 'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_REJECTED' || providerReviewReason === 'PROVIDER_REJECTED_PAYMENT';
                const bankStatus = String(row?.bankStatusLabel || row?.bank_status_label || row?.statusLabel || row?.status_label || row?.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                const issueKind = String(row?.issueKindForDisplay || row?.issue_kind_for_display || row?.issue_kind || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                const backendAction = String(row?.backendAction || row?.backend_action || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                const recoveryActionType = String(row?.recovery_action_type || row?.recoveryActionType || row?.recovery_action || row?.recoveryAction || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                const clearProviderRejectedNoPayment = row.clearProviderRejectedNoPayment === true || row.clear_provider_rejected_no_payment === true || (
                  providerRejectedStatus === true &&
                  providerAccepted !== true &&
                  providerTransactionEvidencePresent !== true &&
                  transferIds.length > 0 &&
                  alreadyReleasedOrInProgress !== true &&
                  rowSuccessfulOrAccepted !== true
                );
                const clearBankRejectedNoPayment = row.clearBankRejectedNoPayment === true || row.clear_bank_rejected_no_payment === true || (
                  providerRejectedStatus !== true &&
                  providerAccepted !== true &&
                  providerTransactionEvidencePresent !== true &&
                  transferIds.length > 0 &&
                  alreadyReleasedOrInProgress !== true &&
                  rowSuccessfulOrAccepted !== true &&
                  (
                    backendAction === 'UNWIND_FAILED_PAYMENT' ||
                    recoveryActionType === 'NO_PAYMENT_RELEASE_UNWIND' ||
                    issueKind === 'REJECTED' ||
                    bankStatus === 'REJECTED' ||
                    bankStatus === 'NOT PAID' ||
                    bankStatus === 'FAILED'
                  )
                );
                const staleEligible = providerStatus === 'PROVIDER_SUBMISSION_UNKNOWN_STALE_CHUNK' && providerReviewReason === 'STALE_RUNNING_PROVIDER_SUBMIT_CHUNK' && providerManualResolutionAvailable === true && providerAccepted !== true && alreadyReleasedOrInProgress !== true && rowSuccessfulOrAccepted !== true;
                const rejectedEligible = (clearProviderRejectedNoPayment === true || clearBankRejectedNoPayment === true) && providerTransactionEvidencePresent !== true && transferIds.length > 0;
                const operationId = String(
                  row.operation_id ||
                  row.operationId ||
                  diagnostic.operation_id ||
                  diagnostic.operationId ||
                  ''
                ).trim();
                return {
                  row,
                  diagnostic,
                  providerSubmitResolutionContext,
                  providerStatus,
                  providerReviewReason,
                  providerManualResolutionAvailable,
                  providerAccepted,
                  providerTransactionEvidencePresent,
                  alreadyReleasedOrInProgress,
                  rowSuccessfulOrAccepted,
                  clearProviderRejectedNoPayment,
                  clearBankRejectedNoPayment,
                  staleEligible,
                  rejectedEligible,
                  transferIds,
                  itemIds,
                  operationId
                };
              });

              const eligibleRows = rowEligibility.filter((entry) => entry.staleEligible || entry.rejectedEligible);
              if (eligibleRows.length !== rowEligibility.length) {
                await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                  title: 'Current Payment Status needs review',
                  message: 'This action is available only for stale provider-submit reviews or confirmed failed-not-paid payments where no provider acceptance or transaction evidence exists. Refresh the batch and review the latest payment state.',
                  confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                });
                return;
              }

              const selectedPayBankTransferIds = uniqueTexts(eligibleRows.flatMap((entry) => entry.transferIds));
              const selectedPayBatchItemIds = uniqueTexts(eligibleRows.flatMap((entry) => entry.itemIds));
              const hasRejectedNoPaymentRows = eligibleRows.some((entry) => entry.rejectedEligible);
              if (hasRejectedNoPaymentRows && selectedPayBankTransferIds.length <= 0) {
                await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                  title: 'Current Payment Status cannot be resolved',
                  message: 'The failed payment transfer ID is missing. Refresh the batch and try again.',
                  confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                });
                return;
              }

              const operationIds = uniqueTexts(eligibleRows.map((entry) => entry.operationId).filter(Boolean));
              const issueOperationId = String(
                attr('operationId', 'data-operation-id') ||
                primaryRow.operation_id ||
                primaryRow.operationId ||
                diagnosticForRow(primaryRow).operation_id ||
                diagnosticForRow(primaryRow).operationId ||
                issueState.operation_id ||
                issueState.operationId ||
                operationIds[0] ||
                ''
              ).trim();

              if (!issueOperationId) {
                await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                  title: 'Current Payment Status cannot be resolved',
                  message: 'Operation ID is missing. Refresh the batch and try again.',
                  confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                });
                return;
              }

              if (operationIds.length > 1) {
                await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                  title: 'Select one operation',
                  message: 'Selected failed payments belong to more than one operation. Resolve one operation at a time.',
                  confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                });
                return;
              }

              if (typeof openBankingNoPaymentCancelConfirmationFlow !== 'function') {
                await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                  title: 'Rewind financials unavailable',
                  message: 'The Rewind financials flow is not available in this version. Refresh CloudTMS and try again.',
                  confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                });
                return;
              }

              const resolutionScope = selectedPayBankTransferIds.length > 1
                ? 'selected_payments'
                : (selectedPayBankTransferIds.length === 1 ? 'single_payment' : 'whole_batch');

              if (resolutionScope === 'whole_batch') {
                const allIssueRows = correctionState.rows.filter((row) => row && typeof row === 'object');
                const selectedKeys = new Set(rowsForResolution.map((row) => String(row?.rowKey || row?.row_key || '').trim()).filter(Boolean));
                const hasAnyId = (row, keys = []) => keys.some((key) => {
                  const value = row ? row[key] : null;
                  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.some((entry) => String(entry == null ? '' : entry).trim());
                  return String(value == null ? '' : value).trim() !== '';
                });
                const wholeBatchPaymentRows = allIssueRows.filter((row) => {
                  const issueKind = String(row.issueKindForDisplay || row.issue_kind_for_display || row.issue_kind || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                  const bankStatus = String(row.bankStatusLabel || row.bank_status_label || row.statusLabel || row.status_label || row.status || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                  if (['DRAFT_REMOVE', 'MANUAL_PAID_ACTION'].includes(issueKind)) return false;
                  if (issueKind || bankStatus) return true;
                  if (row.manualActionAvailable === true || row.manual_action_available === true || row.selectableForApply === true || row.selectableForReview === true) return true;
                  if (row.providerSubmitDiagnostic || row.provider_submit_diagnostic) return true;
                  return hasAnyId(row, [
                    'payBankTransferIds', 'pay_bank_transfer_ids', 'payBankTransferId', 'pay_bank_transfer_id',
                    'payBatchItemIds', 'pay_batch_item_ids', 'payBatchItemId', 'pay_batch_item_id'
                  ]);
                });
                const wholeBatchUnsafeRows = wholeBatchPaymentRows.filter((row) => {
                  const diagnostic = diagnosticForRow(row);
                  return rowLooksAcceptedOrSuccessful(row, diagnostic) === true ||
                    rowHasProviderTransactionEvidence(row, diagnostic) === true ||
                    rowLooksReturnedOrReverted(row, diagnostic) === true ||
                    rowLooksAlreadyReleasedOrInProgress(row, diagnostic) === true;
                });
                const wholeBatchDetailedRows = wholeBatchPaymentRows.filter((row) => hasAnyId(row, [
                  'payBankTransferIds', 'pay_bank_transfer_ids', 'payBankTransferId', 'pay_bank_transfer_id',
                  'payBatchItemIds', 'pay_batch_item_ids', 'payBatchItemId', 'pay_batch_item_id'
                ]));
                const wholeBatchUnselectedRows = wholeBatchDetailedRows.filter((row) => {
                  const rowKeyCurrent = String(row?.rowKey || row?.row_key || '').trim();
                  return rowKeyCurrent && !selectedKeys.has(rowKeyCurrent);
                });
                if (wholeBatchUnsafeRows.length > 0 || wholeBatchUnselectedRows.length > 0) {
                  await showProviderSubmitResolutionNotice({
                    title: 'Resolve selected payments instead',
                    message: 'Whole-batch Rewind financials is only available when every payment in the batch is failed-not-paid eligible and no payment has acceptance, successful, returned, resolved, or transaction evidence. Resolve the failed selected payments individually instead.',
                    confirmClass: 'btn btn-primary'
                  });
                  return;
                }
              }
              const selectedProviderIssue = {
                ...issueState,
                ...primaryRow,
                provider_submit_diagnostic: diagnosticForRow(primaryRow),
                selected_rows: rowsForResolution.map((row) => ({
                  rowKey: String(row?.rowKey || row?.row_key || '').trim(),
                  pay_bank_transfer_ids: uniqueTexts(collectRowValues(row, ['payBankTransferIds', 'pay_bank_transfer_ids', 'payBankTransferId', 'pay_bank_transfer_id', 'transfer_id', 'transferId'])),
                  pay_batch_item_ids: uniqueTexts(collectRowValues(row, ['payBatchItemIds', 'pay_batch_item_ids', 'payBatchItemId', 'pay_batch_item_id', 'item_id', 'itemId'])),
                  provider_submission_status: providerSubmitStatusFromDiagnostic(row) || '',
                  provider_submit_diagnostic: diagnosticForRow(row)
                })),
                selected_pay_bank_transfer_ids: selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                pay_bank_transfer_ids: selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                selected_pay_batch_item_ids: selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                pay_batch_item_ids: selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                resolution_scope: resolutionScope
              };

              try {
                await openBankingNoPaymentCancelConfirmationFlow({
                  payBatchId: id,
                  operationId: issueOperationId,
                  issue: selectedProviderIssue,
                  batch: child.data,
                  parentModalCtx: window.modalCtx,
                  childModalCtx: child,
                  returnTabKey: 'payment_issues',
                  bankingState: st,
                  resolutionScope,
                  resolution_scope: resolutionScope,
                  selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                  selected_pay_bank_transfer_ids: selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                  payBankTransferIds: selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                  pay_bank_transfer_ids: selectedPayBankTransferIds,
                  selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                  selected_pay_batch_item_ids: selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                  payBatchItemIds: selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                  pay_batch_item_ids: selectedPayBatchItemIds,
                  selectedRows: rowsForResolution,
                  selected_rows: rowsForResolution,
                  successTitle: selectedPayBankTransferIds.length > 1 ? 'Selected payments rewound' : 'Selected payment rewound',
                  successMessage: selectedPayBankTransferIds.length > 1
                    ? 'The selected failed payments were rewound and will be recalculated for the next Banking Pay run.'
                    : 'The selected failed payment was rewound and will be recalculated for the next Banking Pay run.',
                  onBeforeMutation: async () => {
                    child.actionsBusy.resolvingProviderSubmitReview = true;
                    child.error = '';
                    child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
                    child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';
                    await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                  },
                  onAfterClose: async () => {
                    child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
                    child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';
                    await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                  },
                  onRefreshChild: async ({ returnTabKey: tabKey } = {}) => {
                    child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
                    child.ui.activeTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(tabKey || 'payment_issues') || 'payment_issues';
                    if (typeof child.__refreshAfterNoPaymentCancel === 'function') {
                      await child.__refreshAfterNoPaymentCancel({ returnTabKey: child.ui.activeTabKey });
                    } else {
                      await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false, silent: true, noPaymentCancelRefresh: true });
                      await rerenderChild();
                    }
                    return child.data;
                  },
                  onRefreshParent: async () => {
                    try {
                      if (typeof bankingPayBatchesList === 'function') {
                        const listState = st?.pay?.list && typeof st.pay.list === 'object' ? st.pay.list : null;
                        await bankingPayBatchesList({
                          status: listState ? listState.statusFilter : null,
                          limit: listState ? listState.limit : null,
                          offset: listState ? listState.offset : null,
                          reportError: false,
                          silent: true,
                          context: {
                            source: 'openBankingNoPaymentCancelConfirmationFlow',
                            action: 'NO_PAYMENT_SELECTED_PAYMENTS_RELEASE_SUCCESS',
                            pay_batch_id: id,
                            pay_bank_transfer_ids: selectedPayBankTransferIds
                          }
                        });
                      }
                    } catch {}
                  },
                  onRefreshAlerts: async () => {
                    try { if (typeof refreshBankingNavAttentionFromCachedRows === 'function') refreshBankingNavAttentionFromCachedRows(); } catch {}
                  },
                  onErrorRefresh: async () => {
                    try { await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false, silent: true, noPaymentCancelRefresh: true }); } catch {}
                    await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                  }
                });
              } finally {
                child.actionsBusy.resolvingProviderSubmitReview = false;
                child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
                child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';
                await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              }

              return;
            }


if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:startManualPaidAction') {
              await applyManualPaidActionMode(true);
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:cancelManualPaidAction') {
              await applyManualPaidActionMode(false);
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:toggleRow') {
              const rowKey = attr('rowKey', 'data-row-key');
              if (!rowKey) return;
              const row = correctionState.rows.find((item) => String(item?.rowKey || item?.row_key || '').trim() === rowKey);
              const nextSelected = (el && typeof el.checked === 'boolean') ? el.checked : !(row && row.selected === true);
              const result = (typeof setBankingPaymentIssueRowSelection === 'function')
                ? setBankingPaymentIssueRowSelection(correctionState, rowKey, nextSelected)
                : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              try { if (typeof invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan === 'function') invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan(correctionState); } catch {}
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:selectAllVisible') {
              const result = (typeof selectBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function') ? selectBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, 'allVisible') : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:clearAll') {
              const result = (typeof selectBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function') ? selectBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, 'clearAll') : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              try { if (typeof invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan === 'function') invalidateBankingPaymentIssuePlan(correctionState); } catch {}
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:selectReturned') {
              const result = (typeof selectBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function') ? selectBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, 'returned') : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:selectRejected') {
              const result = (typeof selectBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function') ? selectBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, 'rejected') : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:selectUmbrellaGroup') {
              const rowKey = attr('rowKey', 'data-row-key');
              const umbrellaId = attr('umbrellaId', 'data-umbrella-id');
              const transferGroupKey = attr('transferGroupKey', 'data-transfer-group-key');
              const payBankTransferId = attr('payBankTransferId', 'data-pay-bank-transfer-id');
              if (!umbrellaId && !transferGroupKey && !payBankTransferId && !rowKey) {
                toast('Select an umbrella payment first.');
                return;
              }
              const triggerRow = correctionState.rows.find((row) => String(row?.rowKey || row?.row_key || '').trim() === rowKey) || null;
              const result = (typeof selectBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function')
                ? selectBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, 'umbrellaGroup', { row: triggerRow, rowKey, umbrellaId, transferGroupKey, payBankTransferId })
                : null;
              if (await showIssueError(result, 'Select an umbrella payment first.')) return;
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:sort') {
              const sortKey = attr('sortBy', 'data-sort-by') || attr('column', 'data-column');
              const sortDir = attr('sortDir', 'data-sort-dir');
              if (typeof sortBankingPaymentIssueRows === 'function') sortBankingPaymentIssueRows(correctionState, sortKey, sortDir);
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:plan') {
              const requestedAction = attr('requestedAction', 'data-requested-action');
              const selection = await getSelectionForAction(requestedAction);
              if (!selection) return;
              correctionState.planLoading = true;
              correctionState.planError = '';
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              try {
                const plan = await bankingPayPaymentCorrectionPlan(id, selection);
                correctionState.plan = plan || null;
                correctionState.selection = selection;
                correctionState.planSelectionSignature = (typeof getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature === 'function')
                  ? getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature(selection)
                  : '';
                correctionState.planError = '';
              } catch (e) {
                correctionState.planError = (typeof sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError === 'function')
                  ? sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError(e, 'plan')
                  : 'Unable to review Current Payment Status.';
                toast(correctionState.planError);
              } finally {
                correctionState.planLoading = false;
              }
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:openConfirm') {
              const requestedAction = attr('requestedAction', 'data-requested-action');
              const selection = await getSelectionForAction(requestedAction);
              if (!selection) return;
              const rowsForAction = selectedRows();
              const draftOnlyRemoval = selectionIsDraftOnlyPaymentIssueRemoval(selection, rowsForAction);
              const signature = (typeof getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature === 'function')
                ? getBankingPaymentIssueSelectionSignature(selection)
                : '';
              if (!correctionState.plan || (signature && correctionState.planSelectionSignature !== signature)) {
                correctionState.planLoading = true;
                await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                try {
                  correctionState.plan = await bankingPayPaymentCorrectionPlan(id, selection);
                  correctionState.planSelectionSignature = signature;
                  correctionState.selection = selection;
                  correctionState.planError = '';
                } catch (e) {
                  correctionState.planError = (typeof sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError === 'function')
                    ? sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError(e, 'plan')
                    : 'Unable to review Current Payment Status.';
                  toast(correctionState.planError);
                  correctionState.planLoading = false;
                  await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                  return;
                } finally {
                  correctionState.planLoading = false;
                }
              } else {
                correctionState.selection = selection;
              }

              if (!draftOnlyRemoval && paymentIssueFinanceResolutionRequired(correctionState.plan, { draftOnlyRemoval }) && !correctionState.acceptedResolutionJson) {
                correctionState.planError = 'Review the suggested pay-channel resolution before continuing.';
                toast(correctionState.planError);
                await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                return;
              }

              if (typeof openBankingPayCorrectionConfirmModal !== 'function') {
                toast('Current Payment Status review is not available yet.');
                await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                return;
              }

              await openBankingPayCorrectionConfirmModal({
                payBatchId: id,
                batchPayload: child.data,
                correctionState,
                selectedRows: rowsForAction,
                currentPlan: correctionState.plan,
                selection: correctionState.selection || selection,
                selectionSignature: correctionState.planSelectionSignature || signature,
                issueKind: draftOnlyRemoval ? 'DRAFT_REMOVE' : correctionState.issueState?.issueKindForDisplay,
                actionKind: requestedAction || correctionState.selection?.requested_action,
                draftOnlyRemoval,
                draft_only_removal: draftOnlyRemoval,
                confirmMode: draftOnlyRemoval ? 'DRAFT_REMOVE_FROM_BATCH' : '',
                confirmTitle: draftOnlyRemoval ? 'Remove payment from draft batch?' : '',
                confirmPrimaryLabel: draftOnlyRemoval ? 'Remove payment from batch' : '',
                confirmBody: draftOnlyRemoval ? 'The selected item will be removed from this draft batch and its reservation will be released. No payment has been sent and no bank reversal, return, rejection, or remittance will be created.' : '',
                reason: correctionState.reason,
                reauthToken: correctionState.reauthToken,
                onRefreshParent: async (result) => {
                  if (result && typeof result === 'object') {
                    try { setProgressFromResult(result); } catch {}
                  }
                  await refreshAfterPaymentIssueAction({ draftOnlyRemoval, result });
                }
              });
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:processChunk') {
              const correctionRequestId = getCorrectionRequestId();
              if (!correctionRequestId) {
                toast('Current Payment Status request ID is required.');
                return;
              }
              const result = await bankingPayPaymentCorrectionProcess(correctionRequestId, 50);
              setProgressFromResult(result);
              refreshAttention();
              child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
              child.ui.activeTabKey = 'payment_issues';
              await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false });
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:refreshStatus') {
              const correctionRequestId = getCorrectionRequestId();
              if (!correctionRequestId) {
                toast('Current Payment Status request ID is required.');
                return;
              }
              const status = await bankingPayPaymentCorrectionStatus(correctionRequestId);
              setProgressFromResult(status);
              correctionState.lastStatusLoadedAtUtc = new Date().toISOString();
              refreshAttention();
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:exportReviewList') {
              if (typeof exportBankingPaymentIssueReviewList === 'function') {
                await exportBankingPaymentIssueReviewList(correctionState.progress || { correctionState }, { batchId: id, rows: correctionState.rows });
              }
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:reviewFinance') {
              const currentPlan = (correctionState?.plan && typeof correctionState.plan === 'object') ? correctionState.plan : null;
              const suggestionEnvelope = getPaymentIssueFinanceSuggestionEnvelope(currentPlan);
              if (suggestionEnvelope.required !== true) {
                toast('Finance review is not required for this action.');
                await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
                return;
              }
              if (typeof openBankingFinanceCaseRestructureModal !== 'function') {
                toast('Finance review is not available yet.');
                return;
              }
              await openBankingFinanceCaseRestructureModal({
                paymentIssueMode: true,
                currentPlan,
                suggested_resolution: suggestionEnvelope.suggestion,
                suggested_resolution_required: true,
                suggestedResolutionEnvelope: suggestionEnvelope.raw,
                acceptedResolutionJson: correctionState.acceptedResolutionJson || null,
                acceptedResolutionSummary: correctionState.acceptedResolutionSummary || '',
                onAccept: ({ acceptedResolutionJson, acceptedResolutionSummary } = {}) => {
                  if (acceptedResolutionJson && typeof acceptedResolutionJson === 'object') correctionState.acceptedResolutionJson = acceptedResolutionJson;
                  if (acceptedResolutionSummary != null) correctionState.acceptedResolutionSummary = acceptedResolutionSummary;
                }
              });
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }

            if (act === 'banking:pay:issue:acceptFinanceResolution') {
              const rawJson = attr('acceptedResolutionJson', 'data-accepted-resolution-json');
              if (rawJson) {
                try {
                  const parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson);
                  if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) correctionState.acceptedResolutionJson = parsed;
                } catch {}
              }
              const summary = attr('acceptedResolutionSummary', 'data-accepted-resolution-summary');
              if (summary) correctionState.acceptedResolutionSummary = summary;
              await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
              return;
            }
          } catch (e) {
            child.error = (typeof sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError === 'function')
              ? sanitizeBankingPaymentIssueError(e, 'process')
              : 'Current Payment Status action failed.';
            await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
            return;
          }

          await rerenderPaymentIssuesOnly();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:refresh') {
          if (hasPaymentExecuteRefreshContext()) {
            await forceChildExecutionRefresh(getPaymentExecuteRefreshOperationId(), 'refresh-button', { userInitiated: true, source: 'openBankingPayBatchChildModal.refreshButton' });
          } else {
            await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false });
          }
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:poll') {
          await loadBatch({ forcePoll: true });
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:viewExecutionStatus') {
          const operationId = String(
            el.getAttribute('data-operation-id') ||
            el.dataset?.operationId ||
            child.activeOperation?.operation_id ||
            child.activeOperation?.operationId ||
            child.active_child_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.activeChildOperation?.operationId ||
            child.settlement_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.settlementOperation?.operationId ||
            child.payment_settlement_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.paymentSettlementOperation?.operationId ||
            child.remittance_queue_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.remittanceQueueOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.active_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.activeOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.active_child_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.activeChildOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.payment_execute_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.paymentExecuteOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.settlement_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.settlementOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.payment_settlement_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.paymentSettlementOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.remittance_queue_operation?.operation_id ||
            child.data?.remittanceQueueOperation?.operationId ||
            child.data?.active_child_operation_id ||
            child.data?.activeChildOperationId ||
            child.data?.settlement_operation_id ||
            child.data?.settlementOperationId ||
            child.data?.remittance_queue_operation_id ||
            child.data?.remittanceQueueOperationId ||
            ''
          ).trim();
          if (!operationId) {
            toast('No active payment execution operation was found. Refresh the batch and try again.');
            return;
          }
          let progressPayload = null;
          try {
            if (typeof bankingPayOperationGet === 'function') {
              progressPayload = await bankingPayOperationGet(operationId, {
                mode: 'PROGRESS_LIGHT',
                progress_mode: 'LIGHT',
                progressMode: 'LIGHT',
                userInitiated: true,
                silent: false
              });
            }
          } catch (e) {
            await reportChildFriendlyError(e, 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', {
              action: 'GET_PAYMENT_EXECUTION_STATUS',
              userInitiated: true,
              silent: false,
              scope: null
            });
            await rerenderChild();
            return;
          }
          const operationPayload = (progressPayload && typeof progressPayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(progressPayload))
            ? progressPayload
            : (child.activeOperation || child.data?.active_operation || child.data?.payment_execute_operation || { operation_id: operationId, operation_type: 'PAYMENT_EXECUTE' });
          const operationTypeFromEl = String(
            el.getAttribute('data-operation-type') ||
            el.getAttribute('data-operation-kind') ||
            el.dataset?.operationType ||
            el.dataset?.operationKind ||
            ''
          ).trim().toUpperCase();
          child.activeOperation = {
            ...(child.activeOperation && typeof child.activeOperation === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.activeOperation) ? child.activeOperation : {}),
            ...(operationPayload && typeof operationPayload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(operationPayload) ? operationPayload : {}),
            operation_id: operationPayload.operation_id || operationPayload.operationId || operationId,
            operationId: operationPayload.operationId || operationPayload.operation_id || operationId,
            operation_type: operationPayload.operation_type || operationPayload.operationType || operationTypeFromEl || 'PAYMENT_EXECUTE',
            operationType: operationPayload.operationType || operationPayload.operation_type || operationTypeFromEl || 'PAYMENT_EXECUTE'
          };
          if (child.data && typeof child.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(child.data)) {
            const activeOperationType = String(child.activeOperation.operation_type || child.activeOperation.operationType || '').trim().toUpperCase();
            child.data.active_operation = child.activeOperation;
            child.data.activeOperation = child.activeOperation;
            if (activeOperationType === 'PAYMENT_EXECUTE' || activeOperationType === 'PAYMENT_RETRY_BLOCKED_FUNDS') {
              child.data.payment_execute_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.paymentExecuteOperation = child.activeOperation;
            } else if (activeOperationType === 'PAYMENT_SETTLEMENT') {
              child.data.active_child_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.activeChildOperation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.settlement_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.settlementOperation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.payment_settlement_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.paymentSettlementOperation = child.activeOperation;
            } else if (activeOperationType === 'REMITTANCE_QUEUE') {
              child.data.active_child_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.activeChildOperation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.remittance_queue_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.remittanceQueueOperation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.active_remittance_operation = child.activeOperation;
              child.data.activeRemittanceOperation = child.activeOperation;
            }
          }
          if (typeof openBankingPayOperationProgressModal === 'function') {
            const terminalCallbacks = createPaymentExecutionTerminalCallbacks('openBankingPayBatchChildModal.viewExecutionStatus');
            await openBankingPayOperationProgressModal(child.activeOperation, {
              payBatchId: id,
              pay_batch_id: id,
              operationId,
              operation_id: operationId,
              mode: 'PROGRESS_LIGHT',
              progress_mode: 'LIGHT',
              observer_only: true,
              observe_only: true,
              no_auto_advance: true,
              noAutoAdvance: true,
              onTerminalObserved: terminalCallbacks.onTerminalObserved,
              onTerminalClose: terminalCallbacks.onTerminalClose,
              onClose: terminalCallbacks.onClose,
              onProgressClose: terminalCallbacks.onClose,
              source: 'openBankingPayBatchChildModal.viewExecutionStatus'
            });
          } else {
            toast('Payment execution status modal is not available. Refresh the batch and try again.');
          }
          await rerenderChild();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:executePayment') {
          await executePaymentPipeline();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:retryBlockedFunds' || act === 'banking:pay:blockedFunds:retry') {
          await retryUnsentPaymentsPipeline();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:generateCsv') {
          if (!hasActivePaymentsForBatch(child.data)) {
            await blockNoActivePaymentsAction();
            return;
          }

          if (!isCsvExportAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
            const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(child.data) || '—';
            try { if (typeof window.__toast === 'function') window.__toast(`CSV export is not allowed in status: ${stx}`); } catch {}
            return;
          }
          await generateCsv();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:exportDetailCsv') {
          await exportDetailCsv();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:exportDetailPdf') {
          await exportDetailPdf();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:openPayeEntry') {
          const kindInfo = deriveBatchKindInfo(child.data);
          if (!kindInfo.isPayeish || kindInfo.isLoans) {
            toast('PAYE entry is only available for PAYE/MIXED batches.');
            return;
          }

          if (!isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
            const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(child.data) || '—';
            toast(`PAYE entry is only available for draft batches (current status: ${stx}).`);
            return;
          }

          if (typeof openBankingPayBatchPayeEntryModal === 'function') {
            try {
              await openBankingPayBatchPayeEntryModal(id);
              return;
            } catch {
              const msg = 'PAYE entry modal failed to open.';
              child.error = msg;
              toast(msg);
              await rerenderChild();
              return;
            }
          }
          toast('PAYE entry modal is not available yet.');
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:paye:exportWorksheet') {
          await exportChildPayeWorksheetCsv();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:paye:importSage') {
          if (!isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
            const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(child.data) || '—';
            toast(`Sage import is only available for draft batches (current status: ${stx}).`);
            return;
          }

          const inp = document.getElementById(`${rootId}__payeFile`);
          if (inp && typeof inp.click === 'function') inp.click();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:paye:printWorksheet') {
          await printChildPayeWorksheet();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:deleteDraft' || act === 'banking:pay:child:cancelBatch' || act === 'banking:pay:blockedFunds:cancel') {
          try {
            const b = deriveBatchObj(child.data) || {};
            const resolvedMode = (typeof bankingPayResolveBatchCancelMode === 'function')
              ? bankingPayResolveBatchCancelMode(b)
              : (act === 'banking:pay:child:deleteDraft' ? 'DRAFT_DELETE' : 'CANCEL_UNPAID');
            const isCancelAction = act === 'banking:pay:child:cancelBatch' || act === 'banking:pay:blockedFunds:cancel';
            const modeMatchesAction =
              (act === 'banking:pay:child:deleteDraft' && resolvedMode === 'DRAFT_DELETE') ||
              (isCancelAction && resolvedMode === 'CANCEL_UNPAID');
            if (!modeMatchesAction) {
              const stx = String(b?.status || '').trim().toUpperCase() || '—';
              toast(`Cancel/delete is not available in status: ${stx}.`);
              return;
            }
            await runBankingPayBatchCancelFlow({
              batch: b,
              batchId: id,
              mode: resolvedMode,
              st,
                       deepClone: deep,
                  onSuccess: async ({
                responsePayload,
                postCancelRefresh,
                postCancelResetOptions,
                previewResetOptions,
                currentWorkbenchSessionId,
                previewResetAlreadyApplied,
                postCancelPreviewResetAlreadyApplied,
                skipFollowUpPreviewReset
              }) => {
              const ownerAppliedPreviewReset = !!(
                previewResetAlreadyApplied === true ||
                postCancelPreviewResetAlreadyApplied === true ||
                skipFollowUpPreviewReset === true ||
                responsePayload?.previewResetAlreadyApplied === true ||
                responsePayload?.postCancelPreviewResetAlreadyApplied === true ||
                responsePayload?.skipFollowUpPreviewReset === true ||
                responsePayload?.preview_reset_already_applied === true ||
                responsePayload?.post_cancel_preview_reset_already_applied === true ||
                responsePayload?.skip_follow_up_preview_reset === true
              );
              const postCancelRefreshObj = (postCancelRefresh && typeof postCancelRefresh === 'object' && !Array.isArray(postCancelRefresh))
                ? postCancelRefresh
                : {};
              const canonicalPostCancelResetOptions = (() => {
                const candidates = [
                  postCancelResetOptions,
                  previewResetOptions,
                  responsePayload?.post_cancel_reset_options,
                  responsePayload?.preview_reset_options
                ];

                for (const candidate of candidates) {
                  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== 'object' || Array.isArray(candidate)) continue;
                  const modeText = String(candidate.mode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                  const reasonText = String(candidate.reason || candidate.mutation_context || candidate.mutationContext || '').trim().toUpperCase();
                  if (
                    modeText === 'POST_DRAFT_CANCEL_DISCARD_AND_REOPEN' ||
                    reasonText === 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS'
                  ) {
                    return {
                      ...candidate,
                      mode: 'POST_DRAFT_CANCEL_DISCARD_AND_REOPEN',
                      reason: 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS',
                      mutation_context: 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS',
                      force_new_session: true,
                      discard_source_session: true,
                      ignore_replacement_session: true,
                      preview_reopen_required: true
                    };
                  }
                }

                return null;
              })();
              const postCancelResetOptionsForPreview = canonicalPostCancelResetOptions || {
                mode: 'POST_DRAFT_CANCEL_DISCARD_AND_REOPEN',
                reason: 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS',
                mutation_context: 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS',
                force_new_session: true,
                discard_source_session: true,
                ignore_replacement_session: true,
                preview_reopen_required: true,
                source_session_id: String(postCancelRefreshObj.source_session_id || postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSessionId || currentWorkbenchSessionId || '').trim() || null,
                obsolete_session_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.obsolete_session_ids)
                  ? postCancelRefreshObj.obsolete_session_ids
                  : (String(postCancelRefreshObj.source_session_id || postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSessionId || currentWorkbenchSessionId || '').trim()
                      ? [String(postCancelRefreshObj.source_session_id || postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSessionId || currentWorkbenchSessionId || '').trim()]
                      : []),
                dirty_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.dirty_candidate_ids) ? postCancelRefreshObj.dirty_candidate_ids : [],
                pending_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.pending_candidate_ids)
                  ? postCancelRefreshObj.pending_candidate_ids
                  : (Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.dirty_candidate_ids) ? postCancelRefreshObj.dirty_candidate_ids : []),
                refresh_job_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.refresh_job_ids) ? postCancelRefreshObj.refresh_job_ids : [],
                source_snapshot_run_id: String(postCancelRefreshObj.source_snapshot_run_id || postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSnapshotRunId || '').trim() || null,
                source_session_version: postCancelRefreshObj.source_session_version ?? postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSessionVersion ?? null,
                source_session_signature: String(postCancelRefreshObj.source_session_signature || postCancelRefreshObj.sourceSessionSignature || '').trim() || null,
                snapshot_rebuild: (postCancelRefreshObj.snapshot_rebuild && typeof postCancelRefreshObj.snapshot_rebuild === 'object' && !Array.isArray(postCancelRefreshObj.snapshot_rebuild))
                  ? postCancelRefreshObj.snapshot_rebuild
                  : null,
                post_cancel_refresh: postCancelRefreshObj
              };
              const postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage = 'Draft was cancelled, but the payment preview could not be refreshed. Refresh Banking and try again.';

              try {
                if (st && st.pay && typeof st.pay === 'object') {
                  if (String(st.pay.selectedBatchId || '').trim() === id) st.pay.selectedBatchId = null;
                }
              } catch {}

              try {
                if (typeof bankingPayBatchesList === 'function') {
                  await bankingPayBatchesList({
                    status: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.statusFilter : null,
                    limit: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.limit : null,
                    offset: (st && st.pay && st.pay.list) ? st.pay.list.offset : null
                  });
                }
              } catch {}
              if (!ownerAppliedPreviewReset) {
                try {
                  if (typeof resetPayPreviewAndDecisions === 'function') {
                    await resetPayPreviewAndDecisions(postCancelResetOptionsForPreview);
                  } else {
                    const pd = String(st?.pay?.draftWizard?.pay_date || '').trim();
                    if (pd && typeof bankingPayPreview === 'function') {
                      await bankingPayPreview({
                        pay_date: pd,
                        mode: 'POST_DRAFT_CANCEL_DISCARD_AND_REOPEN',
                        hard_session_reload: true,
                        force_new_session: true,
                        discard_source_session: true,
                        ignore_replacement_session: true,
                        preview_reopen_required: true,
                        source_session_id: postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.source_session_id || null,
                        obsolete_session_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.obsolete_session_ids) ? postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.obsolete_session_ids : [],
                        dirty_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.dirty_candidate_ids) ? postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.dirty_candidate_ids : [],
                        pending_candidate_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.pending_candidate_ids) ? postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.pending_candidate_ids : [],
                        refresh_job_ids: Array.isArray(postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.refresh_job_ids) ? postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.refresh_job_ids : [],
                        source_snapshot_run_id: postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.source_snapshot_run_id || null,
                        source_session_version: postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.source_session_version ?? null,
                        source_session_signature: postCancelResetOptionsForPreview.source_session_signature || null,
                        mutation_context: 'CANCEL_DELETE_DRAFT_SUCCESS'
                      });
                    }
                  }
                } catch (previewRefreshError) {
                  try {
                    const friendly = await reportChildFriendlyError(previewRefreshError, 'BANKING_PAY_PREVIEW_FAILED', {
                      action: 'POST_DRAFT_CANCEL_PREVIEW_REFRESH',
                      userInitiated: false,
                      silent: true,
                      scope: null,
                      payload: postCancelResetOptionsForPreview,
                      backendPayload: previewRefreshError?.backendPayload || previewRefreshError?.payload || previewRefreshError?.json || postCancelResetOptionsForPreview,
                      technicalError: previewRefreshError
                    });
                    const friendlyMessage = String(friendly?.user_message || friendly?.message || postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage).trim();
                    const safeMessage = /no\s+payment\s+batch\s+has\s+been\s+created/i.test(friendlyMessage)
                      ? postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage
                      : (friendlyMessage || postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage);
                    child.error = safeMessage;
                    try { toast(safeMessage); } catch {}
                  } catch {
                    child.error = postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage;
                    try { toast(postCancelPreviewRefreshMessage); } catch {}
                  }
                }
              }

              try { stopAutoPoll(); } catch {}
              try { stopChildLiveWatch(); } catch {}
              try { child.__loadInFlight = false; } catch { child.__loadInFlight = false; }
              try { child.loading = false; } catch {}
              try {
                if (pay && pay.child && typeof pay.child === 'object') {
                  const currentToken = String(pay.child.openToken || '');
                  if (!currentToken || currentToken === String(child.openToken || '') || String(pay.child.batchId || '') === id) {
                    pay.child = null;
                  }
                }
              } catch {}
              try { child.openToken = `closed:${child.openToken || id}:${Date.now()}`; } catch {}

              await closeCancelOrChildFramesAfterBatchRemoval();

              try {
                if (typeof bankingRerender === 'function') {
                  await bankingRerender(null);
                }
              } catch {}
            }
            });
          } catch (e) {
            await reportChildFriendlyError(e, 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', {
              action: 'CANCEL_BATCH',
              userInitiated: true,
              silent: false,
              scope: null
            });
            await rerenderChild();
          }
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:expandAll') {
          const cands = deriveCandidates(child.data);
          const next = {};
          for (const c of cands) {
            const expansionKey = getCandidateExpansionKey(c);
            if (expansionKey) next[expansionKey] = true;
          }
          child.ui.expanded = next;
          child.ui.expandAllDefault = true;
          await rerenderChild();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:collapseAll') {
          child.ui.expanded = {};
          child.ui.expandAllDefault = false;
          await rerenderChild();
          return;
        }

        if (act === 'banking:pay:child:toggleExpandCandidate') {
          const cid = String(el.getAttribute('data-candidate-id') || '').trim();
          if (!cid) return;
          const cur = !!child.ui.expanded[cid];
          child.ui.expanded[cid] = !cur;
          await rerenderChild();
          return;
        }

      } catch {}
    };

    const onChange = async (ev) => {
      try {
        if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return;
        const root = findRoot(ev.target);
        if (!root) return;

        const el = ev.target;

        try {
          if (el && el.getAttribute && String(el.getAttribute('data-action') || '') === 'banking:pay:child:paye:setFilterCandidate') {
            child.paye.filterCandidateId = String(el.value || 'ALL').trim() || 'ALL';
            await rerenderChild();
            return;
          }
        } catch {}

        try {
          if (el && el.id === `${rootId}__payeFile`) {
            const f = (el.files && el.files[0]) ? el.files[0] : null;
            try { el.value = ''; } catch {}
            if (!f) return;

            if (!isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
              const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(child.data) || '—';
              toast(`Sage import is only available for draft batches (current status: ${stx}).`);
              await rerenderChild();
              return;
            }

            child.paye.importBusy = true;
            child.paye.importError = '';
            child.paye.importFriendlyError = null;
            await rerenderChild();

            try {
              const name = String(f.name || 'sage_export.csv');
              const text = await f.text();
              if (!String(text || '').trim()) throw new Error('File is empty');

              await postJson(`/api/banking/pay/batch/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/paye-net/sage`, {
                csv_raw: text,
                source_filename: name
              });

              child.paye.netDraft = {};
              await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false });

              toast('Sage import applied');
            } catch (e2) {
              const friendly = normaliseChildFriendlyError(e2, 'SAGE_IMPORT_INVALID', {
                action: 'PAYE_NET_IMPORT',
                userInitiated: true,
                silent: true,
                scope: deriveScopeForBatch(child.data)
              });
              child.paye.importError = String(friendly?.message || 'PAYE net amounts could not be imported.').trim();
              child.paye.importFriendlyError = friendly;
              await rerenderChild();
            } finally {
              child.paye.importBusy = false;
              await rerenderChild();
            }

            return;
          }
        } catch {}
      } catch {}
    };

    const onInput = async (ev) => {
      try {
        if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return;
        const root = findRoot(ev.target);
        if (!root) return;

        const el = ev.target;

        try {
          if (el && el.getAttribute && String(el.getAttribute('data-action') || '') === 'banking:pay:child:paye:setNetDraft') {
            const cid = String(el.getAttribute('data-candidate-id') || '').trim();
            if (!cid) return;

            if (!isPayeEntryAllowedByStatus(child.data)) {
              const stx = getBatchStatusUpper(child.data) || '—';
              toast(`PAYE worksheet entry is only available for draft batches (current status: ${stx}).`);
              await rerenderChild();
              return;
            }

            const fr = (typeof window.__getModalFrame === 'function') ? window.__getModalFrame() : null;
            const canEdit = !!(fr && (fr.mode === 'edit' || fr.mode === 'create'));
            if (!canEdit) {
              toast('Click Edit to enable PAYE net changes.');
              await rerenderChild();
              return;
            }

            child.paye.netDraft = (child.paye.netDraft && typeof child.paye.netDraft === 'object') ? child.paye.netDraft : {};
            child.paye.saveError = '';
            child.paye.netDraft[cid] = String(el.value || '').trim();

            markDirty();
            syncChildFooterState();
            return;
          }
        } catch {}
      } catch {}
    };

    const onDblClick = async (ev) => {
      try {
        if (!stillTopIsThisChild()) return;
        const root = findRoot(ev.target);
        if (!root) return;

        const el = findRemittancePreviewEl(ev);
        if (!el) return;

        const outboxId = String(
          el.getAttribute('data-remittance-outbox-id') ||
          el.getAttribute('data-outbox-id') ||
          ''
        ).trim();
        if (!outboxId) return;

        const channel = String(el.getAttribute('data-channel') || 'EMAIL').trim().toUpperCase() || 'EMAIL';

        ev.preventDefault();
        ev.stopPropagation();

        if (typeof openOutboxDetailModal !== 'function') {
          throw new Error('Outbox detail modal is not available.');
        }

        await openOutboxDetailModal({ channel, outbox_id: outboxId });
      } catch (e) {
        await reportChildFriendlyError(e, 'BANKING_ACTION_FAILED', {
          action: 'OPEN_REMITTANCE_PREVIEW',
          userInitiated: true,
          silent: false,
          scope: null
        });
        await rerenderChild();
      }
    };

    body.addEventListener('click', onClick, true);
    body.addEventListener('change', onChange, true);
    body.addEventListener('input', onInput, true);
    body.addEventListener('dblclick', onDblClick, true);

    body.__bankingPayBatchChildHandler = {
      openToken: child.openToken,
      bodyEl: body,
      installed: true,
      onClick,
      onChange,
      onInput,
      onDblClick
    };
  };

  function isDisabledEl(el) {
    try {
      if (!el) return false;
      const d = String(el.getAttribute('data-disabled') || '').trim();
      if (d === '1' || d.toLowerCase() === 'true') return true;
      const aria = String(el.getAttribute('aria-disabled') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (aria === 'true') return true;
      if (el.classList && el.classList.contains('disabled')) return true;
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  const scheduleWire = () => {
    try {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => { try { wire(); } catch {} }));
      return;
    } catch {}
    try { setTimeout(() => { try { wire(); } catch {} }, 0); } catch {}
  };

  await loadBatch({ forcePoll: false });

  try {
    if (isBootstrapBatchPayload(child.data)) {
      ensureChildLazySections(child.data);
    }
  } catch {}

  try {
    child.ui = (child.ui && typeof child.ui === 'object') ? child.ui : {};
    if (hasRequestedActiveTabKey) {
      child.ui.activeTabKey = requestedActiveTabKey;
    } else {
      child.ui.activeTabKey = 'overview';
    }
  } catch {
    try { child.ui.activeTabKey = requestedActiveTabKey || 'overview'; } catch {}
  }

   const modalTabs = (() => {
    const out = [
      { key: 'overview', label: 'Overview' },
      { key: 'payment_issues', label: 'Current Payment Status' }
    ];

    const kindInfo = deriveBatchKindInfo(child.data);
    if (kindInfo.isPayeish && !kindInfo.isLoans) {
      out.push({ key: 'paye_worksheet', label: 'PAYE Worksheet' });
    }

    out.push({ key: 'remittance', label: 'Remittance' });
    return out;
  })();

  try {
    const availableTabKeys = new Set(modalTabs.map((tab) => String(tab?.key || '').trim()).filter(Boolean));
    const currentTabKey = normalizeChildTabKey(child.ui?.activeTabKey || '') || 'overview';
    child.ui.activeTabKey = availableTabKeys.has(currentTabKey) ? currentTabKey : 'overview';
  } catch {}

  showModal(
    `Pay Batch — ${id.slice(0, 8)}`,
    modalTabs,
    renderTab,
    onSave,
    true,
    null,
    {
      kind: KIND,
      noParentGate: true,
      showSave: false,
      showApply: false,
      forceEdit: false,
      stayOpenOnSave: true,
      childOpenToken: child.openToken,
      _childOpenToken: child.openToken,
      payBatchId: id,
      batchId: id,
      onDismiss
    }
  );

  try {
    const fr = getTopModalFrameSafe();
    if (fr && String(fr.kind || '') === KIND) {
      fr.childOpenToken = child.openToken;
      fr._childOpenToken = child.openToken;
      fr.payBatchId = id;
      fr.batchId = id;
    }
  } catch {}

  child.__modalShown = true;

  try { ensureFirstPageForActiveTab(child.ui?.activeTabKey || 'overview'); } catch {}

  scheduleWire();
  ensureChildModalShellClass();
  await rerenderChild();
  scheduleWire();
}
